# MEMORY.md — everything we know about this project

> A snapshot taken on 2026-11-26 of the notes kept about the **Rasoi Pulse** project (a multi-tenant restaurant ordering platform; formerly TablePe / OneTap; the first store is Gazab Momos). It holds 73 notes: decisions, rules, how things were built and tested, what is deployed, and what is still open.
>
> **What is NOT in here, on purpose:** passwords, API keys, tokens and database connection strings (none were stored in the notes), and the server's address, SSH port, database host / user name and personal network addresses (shown as `<VPS-IP>`, `<SSH-PORT>`, `<atlas-cluster>`, `<db-user>`, `<my-public-IP>`). Keep it that way when you edit this file.
>
> A note's first line says when it was written. Newer notes override older ones. Dates are YYYY-MM-DD. `[[name]]` links point to another note in this file.

## How this file is used

- It is the project's long-term memory for people **and** for AI assistants (Claude Code): read the *standing rules* section first, then the notes for the area you are about to change.
- It is a snapshot. When something important is decided, fixed or deployed, add or update the matching note here. Do not paste secrets.
- The day-to-day overview for humans is [README.md](README.md); this file is the detail behind it.

## How to work on this project (standing rules and preferences)

### feedback-english-explanations

_From 2026-11-25 talk to the user in English (not Hinglish) — they want to improve their English, so explain things clearly and simply_

On 2026-11-25 the user (who wrote in Hinglish until then) said: "next time se english me baat krna hai. mujhe apni english acchi krni hai bss acche se samjha kr batana hai." — i.e. **reply in English from now on**, and explain things well so they learn.

**Why:** they want to improve their English; they do not want a language switch to cost them understanding.
**How to apply:** write in simple, correct English with short sentences and everyday words; explain a technical term the first time it matters (one clause is enough); keep the structure easy to follow (what happened → what it means → what to do). Don't switch back to Hinglish even when the user writes in Hinglish — answer in English. Supersedes the earlier "answer in Hinglish, simple, with examples" habit; the "simple, with examples" part still stands.

### feedback-ask-before-prod-deploy

_Since 2026-11-25 never deploy to production (rasoipulse.in) without asking the user first; tests + commit + push can go ahead_

User, 2026-11-25 (after the production domain went live and the dev server was switched off): "pm2 save kar do, aur prod par hi pooch kr" → **ask before deploying to production.**

**Why:** production now serves the real store; the old rule "after tests pass, deploy automatically and just tell me" was written when the target was the dev server ([dev-server-mytimelyne](#dev-server-mytimelyne)), which is OFF. They said earlier that code should reach prod only when it is stable.
**How to apply:** finish the change, run the targeted tests ([feedback-test-scope](#feedback-test-scope)), commit on `main` and push as before — then STOP and ask "deploy to production now?" with a short summary of what would go live. Deploy only after a yes (a yes covers that batch only). If the dev server is ever switched on again, the old auto-deploy rule applies to dev only. Never `pm2 save` again without need; the saved list is already correct (see [production-rasoipulse](#production-rasoipulse)).

### feedback-save-every-test

_Standing rule (2026-11-15): every test the user asks for must be saved in the repo (tests/e2e) with steps, what to expect, what must NOT happen, and what passed/failed — so all can be re-run at once later_

When the user says "test X" (they test features one by one), do the test AND save it permanently in `tests/e2e/` as a test file — never leave it only in the scratchpad. Each test must carry: the **steps** (`d.step`), **what to expect** (`meta.expect`), **what must NOT happen** (`meta.expectNot` + `d.never` checks), and **what passed / failed** (generated into `tests/e2e/docs/<id>.md` + `docs/CATALOG.md` by `node run.mjs`).

**Why:** the user said "save all the tests in a separate directory in the project so that later I can test all at once… after test or before test please save the steps. also save what to expect and what not, what passed and what failed." Earlier regression suites were lost when the scratchpad was wiped by a reboot.

**How to apply:** follow the "Adding a test" section of `tests/e2e/README.md`; include negative checks and try the forbidden thing in the UI itself (no direct API calls — see Chrome only below); run `node run.mjs --only tNN --visible` (watched, full-size Chrome) and report pass/fail + findings; add the new test to the catalog by running it; don't commit/push unless asked. See [test-bench](#test-bench).

**Chrome only (2026-11-15):** the user said "do not call API's directly, do all things on chrome" — tests must be driven entirely through the real screens (platform console, store dashboard, storefront) via `tests/e2e/bench/ui.mjs`, never `d.api`/fetch. t08–t12 were rewritten that way (the API versions were discarded). Gotchas learned: the overlay captions must be CSS pseudo-elements (real text leaked into page-text assertions); plan form starts with every feature ticked; number inputs need select-all+type; own domains can't carry a port, so a store is only reachable in the browser through its TablePe address (`<label>.localhost:3170`); owner/platform sessions share one cookie jar, so switch by signing out and in.

### feedback-test-scope

_Standing rule (2026-11-21) — test only the areas a change can affect; run the whole e2e suite only when the user explicitly asks for it_

User: "do not test everything always. i will ask you to test everything then you need to test every flow. normally you test only those areas which can be affected by the change."

**Why:** the full suite takes ~45 minutes and was being run before every deploy, delaying things the user wanted shipped.
**How to apply:** after a change, run the new/updated test plus the existing tests for the flows that change touches (e.g. menu-form tests for a menu-form change, order-flow tests for an order-flow change; tests that already passed on the identical code in a stopped/earlier run count). Run `node run.mjs` with no `--only` ONLY when the user says to test everything. Still say which tests were run and which were deliberately skipped. This replaces the earlier habit of a full regression before each deploy; "every requested feature has a saved test" ([feedback-save-every-test](#feedback-save-every-test)) still applies.

### feedback-undefined-names-check

_After moving/removing code in React files, scan for names used but never defined — vite build and node --check do NOT catch them, and tests only hit branches they render (2026-11-19 itemRow crash)_

On 2026-11-19 I moved the menu editor's category/item lists into `routes/menu/*.jsx` and deleted the `itemRow` style constant from `MenuEditor.jsx` — but the "Add-on groups" list further down the same file still used it. The page crashed with `ReferenceError: itemRow is not defined` as soon as the user had an add-on group. `vite build` passed, my Chrome test passed (the bench store has no add-on groups, so that branch never rendered), and the user hit it live.

**Why:** a build/bundler doesn't resolve free identifiers inside JSX expressions, and e2e tests only execute the branches their data reaches.
**How to apply:** whenever a change DELETES or MOVES a const/function/component in a JSX file, (1) grep the whole file (and importers) for every removed name, and (2) run the undefined-name scan before saying "done": parse with `@babel/parser` (found at `node_modules/.pnpm/@babel+parser@*/node_modules/@babel/parser`, plugin `jsx`), collect declared names (imports, function/class/var/params/destructuring), and flag identifiers not declared and not a browser/node global — treat `?.prop`, object keys, JSX tags in lowercase, `import.meta` as non-uses. It flagged the real bug in a probe. Run it over `git diff --name-only HEAD` + untracked source files (use `xargs -0`, zsh doesn't word-split variables). Also: when a page has conditional sections (empty-state vs list), make the e2e test create the data that renders the OTHER branch too.

### feedback-db-wipe-keep-website

_When wiping the real DB, the marketing-website content (sitepages, sitesettings) is NOT test data — keep it; the 2026-11-18 wipe removed it and it was restored from the backup_

On 2026-11-18 the user asked to "clean the DB completely" (keep only the super-admin account). I wiped EVERY collection, including `sitepages` (7 pages: home, pricing, faq, about, contact, privacy-policy, terms) and `sitesettings` (TablePe brand/SEO/footer) — the content of the TablePe marketing website (apps/website, :3073) which the user uses to promote the product. They noticed and asked for it back ("restore the website data, not any store data").

**Why:** the website content is product content they curated, not store/test data. "Clean completely" meant stores/orders/customers.

**How to apply:** any future DB wipe/reset → keep `sitepages` + `sitesettings` (and ask about `leads`, `plans`, `platformsettings`), or at least back up and say what is going. Restore recipe that worked: backup is `~/tablepe-db-backups/onetap-2026-11-18-10-36/` (EJSON per collection + `_manifest.json`); insert ONLY missing `_id`s from `sitepages.json` + `sitesettings.json` (verify fingerprints), then stamp `platformsettings.cacheExpiredAt = now` (what console "Expire all" does; APIs sharing the DB notice within ~30 s after a request), then POST `/api/revalidate` on each website with `x-revalidate-secret` (local default `dev-only-website-revalidate-secret`; dev server's secret is in `/var/www/tablepe-dev/apps/api/.env`, call 127.0.0.1:3083 from the server). Verified all headings render on localhost:3073 and mytimelyne.info. The real DB is shared by the local API and the dev server, so one restore fixes both.

**Not restored (left in the backup):** one `leads` doc (Sunny Singh, looks like test data), `plans` (the real DB has Starter/Business/Custom now), `platformsettings` (grace 7 days, cache 3600 s — the wipe reset them to defaults 30 days / 300 s; a doc now exists holding only `cacheExpiredAt`). See [onboarding-form](#onboarding-form), [tablepe-website](#tablepe-website).

### deploy-workflow

_What "deploy" means for OneTap going forward, and the production VPS setup from the first deploy (2026-09-04)_

**SUPERSEDED 2026-11-20 — NO MORE FEATURE BRANCHES:** the user said "from now, do not create new branch for any feature or bug. do the change in the main branch directly." Commit (and push, when asked) straight on `main`; never create a feature/bugfix branch. Feature branches that already exist (e.g. `feature/message-sending`, merged via PR #17) are history. Check `git branch --show-current` is `main` before committing; pull/fetch first so main isn't behind origin.

**"Deploy" means all of it:** when the user says "deploy" (or "ship it"), the workflow is: **git add → commit → push, then deploy the changes to prod.** Don't treat these as separate asks — one "deploy" request means do all of it. This was given as a memory-only instruction ("just save in memory, do not do anything at this time") — nothing was executed when this was said; act on it starting the next time deploy is actually requested.

(SUPERSEDED 2026-11-20 by the no-branches rule at the top of this note — kept for history only.) **User's standing instruction (2026-09-04, given again explicitly as "I will not tell this again, use your mind"):** always use a separate git branch for real feature work — decide for myself, without asking, whether a given change belongs on its own branch or is small enough for `main` directly. Rule of thumb applied so far: a multi-file, multi-session feature (e.g. the multi-outlet feature, branch `feature/multi-outlet`) gets its own branch, created after first committing whatever was already pending on `main`; a small isolated fix/tweak (a bug fix, a copy change, a single-component UI fix) goes straight to `main` as before. Branch naming used so far: `feature/<kebab-case-name>`.

**Blocker to check before the first git-based deploy:** local `git status` has been erroring all session with `fatal: .git/index: index file smaller than expected` — a corrupted index, not yet fixed. There is also no confirmed first commit yet (per [project-overview](#project-overview), the repo was public+empty with a `origin` remote at `https://github.com/shanuraj715/OneTap.git`, never pushed to). Before the first "add, commit, push" cycle, check `git status`/`git log` again — the index may need `rm .git/index && git reset` (safe, just rebuilds the index from HEAD/objects, doesn't lose committed work) or a fresh `git init` if there's truly nothing committed yet. Also confirm the user wants this pushed to that GitHub remote specifically (and that it's private — an earlier memory flagged it was public+empty and the user was advised to make it private before a first push).

**How production is actually deployed right now (first deploy, done manually over SSH, 2026-09-04):**
- VPS: `<VPS-IP>`, SSH port `<SSH-PORT>`, user `root`. My local machine's `~/.ssh/id_ed25519` is now an authorized key there — no password needed for future sessions (`ssh -p <SSH-PORT> root@<VPS-IP>`). **The root password was pasted in chat that day — should be rotated by the user at some point; not done yet.**
- This is a **shared box** hosting several of the user's other live sites (mytimelyne.com, myqrv2.com, gaanaworld.in, techfacts007.in, thatplatetho.com, a portfolio, etc.) under `/var/www/<name>/` + pm2. Treat it accordingly on every future deploy — don't touch other sites' nginx configs, ports, or pm2 entries; only add/modify `food-api`, `food-storefront`, and the `admin.food.shanuthewebdev.in` static root.
- App lives at `/var/www/food-app` — the **whole monorepo** (not just built output), since it's a pnpm workspace and the apps need their workspace-linked `@onetap/*` packages resolved via `pnpm install`, not just copied build artifacts.
- **Node**: the box's system Node is v20 (used by the other pm2 apps — do not change it). This app needs Node ≥22 (pnpm 11.25.0's own engine requirement), installed via **nvm**, isolated at `/root/.nvm/versions/node/v22.23.2/bin/node`. `pnpm` itself: corepack is broken on this box (`ERR_VM_DYNAMIC_IMPORT_CALLBACK_MISSING` under Node 20's corepack shim) — installed instead via `npm install -g pnpm@11.25.0` *after* `nvm use 22`.
- **Redeploy steps** (until the git-push workflow above replaces this): `rsync -az --delete -e "ssh -p <SSH-PORT>" --exclude node_modules --exclude .next --exclude dist --exclude .turbo --exclude .git --exclude .local-db --exclude '*.tsbuildinfo' --exclude .env --exclude .env.local --exclude .env.production --exclude .DS_Store ./ root@<VPS-IP>:/var/www/food-app/` (⚠️ the exclude list **must** keep excluding every `.env*` — the production secrets live only on the server, never sync a local `.env` over them) → then on the server: `export NVM_DIR=/root/.nvm; . $NVM_DIR/nvm.sh; nvm use 22; cd /var/www/food-app; pnpm install --frozen-lockfile; pnpm build` → then `pm2 restart food-api food-storefront` (admin needs no restart, it's static files nginx serves straight from `apps/admin/dist` — a rebuild alone updates it) → `pm2 save`.
- **When switching to git-based deploy**: decide whether `/var/www/food-app` becomes an actual `git clone` of the pushed repo (then redeploy = `git pull` + build, cleaner and matches what the user asked for) versus keeping the rsync step. A git checkout on the server is the more natural fit for "push, then deploy" — worth proposing next time this comes up, rather than assuming rsync-from-local stays forever.
- **Ports** (chosen to avoid the ~10 other services already bound on this box — re-check `ss -tlnp` before reusing if this ever changes): `food-api` → 3072, `food-storefront` → 3070 (`next start -p 3070`). Admin has no port — static `dist/` served by nginx.
- **pm2** (correction 2026-11-14: there is NO ecosystem file in /var/www/food-app — food-api/food-storefront were started from the CLI with the nvm Node 22 interpreter; the dev deployment in [dev-server-mytimelyne](#dev-server-mytimelyne) does use a server-side ecosystem file; system pm2 is v5.3.1 at /usr/bin/pm2): originally noted as an ecosystem file defining both apps with an explicit `interpreter` pointing at the nvm Node 22 binary (don't rely on PATH — pm2's daemon may resolve to the system Node 20 otherwise). Next's own `.bin/next` shim is a **shell script**, not JS — pointing pm2's `interpreter: node` at it directly fails with a syntax error; the ecosystem file instead points `script` at the real JS entry `node_modules/next/dist/bin/next`. pm2 auto-starts on boot already (`systemctl is-enabled pm2-root` → enabled) — `pm2 save` after any process-list change is what persists it.
- **nginx**: three server blocks, `/etc/nginx/sites-available/{food.shanuthewebdev.in, api.food.shanuthewebdev.in, admin.food.shanuthewebdev.in}`, all proxying to `127.0.0.1:<port>` (WebSocket upgrade headers included on the API one — it serves `/realtime` and `/preview` WS endpoints) except admin which is a static `root` + `try_files ... /index.html`. Real Let's Encrypt cert already issued for all three (one multi-SAN cert, `certbot --nginx`), auto-renews via the box's existing certbot timer.
- **DNS / Cloudflare**: all three `*.food.shanuthewebdev.in` records point at `<VPS-IP>`. `food.shanuthewebdev.in` itself works proxied (orange cloud) because it matches Cloudflare's free `*.shanuthewebdev.in` wildcard cert. **`api.food` and `admin.food` do NOT** — they're two-level subdomains, outside that wildcard's coverage, so Cloudflare's edge can't TLS-handshake for them while proxied (`ERR_SSL_VERSION_OR_CIPHER_MISMATCH` client-side). Told the user to either flip those two to DNS-only (grey cloud, bypasses Cloudflare, hits the origin's own valid cert directly) or turn on Cloudflare's **Total TLS** (covers arbitrary subdomain depth while staying proxied) — **not yet confirmed which one the user picked, or whether it's fixed; check `curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' https://api.food.shanuthewebdev.in/health` and the admin URL next time before assuming they work.**
- **Environment**: production `apps/api/.env` reuses the **same MongoDB Atlas database** as local dev (`<atlas-cluster>.mongodb.net/onetap` — see [security-credentials](#security-credentials) for the rotation caveat on that credential), with a **freshly generated `ENCRYPTION_KEY`** (32 random bytes, generated server-side via `openssl rand -hex 32`, never left the server, not recorded here). `CORS_ORIGINS` = the two food.* frontend origins, `ALLOW_MOCK_GATEWAY=false`. `apps/storefront/.env.production` and `apps/admin/.env.production` each set their `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL` / `VITE_API_URL` (+ `VITE_STOREFRONT_URL`) to `https://api.food.shanuthewebdev.in` / `https://food.shanuthewebdev.in` — **these are baked in at build time**, so any URL/domain change requires a full rebuild, not just an env edit + restart.
- The demo outlet's `hostnames` array in Mongo was updated to add `food.shanuthewebdev.in` (kept the local dev hostnames too, additive) and `canonicalHostname` was pointed at it.
- **The demo admin password was rotated** the same day (was the long-published `momos1234`, pasted everywhere in chat/scratchpad/memory all session) — new password was generated and given to the user directly in chat, not recorded here; if it's needed again, ask the user or generate + rotate a new one the same way (`PATCH /api/users/:id` with the logged-in owner's own id, password field) — this account is `owner@gazabmomos.example`.
- **R2 (Cloudflare S3-compatible) storage was configured the same day** — bucket `food`, region `auto`, endpoint `https://168936d65ad9e1853a9dec1d55792ff2.r2.cloudflarestorage.com`, verified working via the app's own built-in `POST /api/storage/config/test` (round-trip put+delete against the real bucket) **run against production, not local dev** — local dev has a different `ENCRYPTION_KEY` than production, so any test of encrypted credentials (storage, payments, notifications — anything using `encryptSecret`/`decryptSecret`) must be run against whichever environment actually saved them, or it fails with "Stored credentials could not be read" for reasons that have nothing to do with the credentials themselves. **Outstanding**: the "Public base URL" field was left as that same private R2 API endpoint, which doesn't serve public reads (confirmed: returns 400 to an anonymous GET) — told the user to enable the bucket's `r2.dev` public subdomain or connect a custom domain and put *that* in Public base URL instead; not yet confirmed done.


**SUPERSEDED 2026-11-25 for production — see [feedback-ask-before-prod-deploy](#feedback-ask-before-prod-deploy): ask before deploying to rasoipulse.in; the auto-deploy rule below applied to the DEV server only (now switched off).**

**STANDING RULE from 2026-11-21 (user: "each time your test done, please deploy automatically. just inform me after deployment"):** after finishing a requested change and its targeted tests pass, commit on `main` in logical commits, push, and deploy to the dev server (recipe in [dev-server-mytimelyne](#dev-server-mytimelyne)) WITHOUT waiting for the word "deploy"; tell the user afterwards. Still never `pm2 save`, never touch prod `food-*` apps, never use the real DB for tests, and if a test fails or a deploy step errors, stop and report instead of shipping.

## The product and its architecture

### project-overview

_What the Gazab Momos / "Thali" project is — a multi-tenant restaurant SaaS platform, its locked scope decisions, and current status_

Greenfield project in `<repo>` (empty as of 2026-09-03, not yet a git repo).

**Goal:** a multi-tenant restaurant SaaS platform — one codebase renders many restaurants, each themed / feature-toggled / configured entirely from an admin panel, no code change to onboard a restaurant that fits the component library. Working name **"Thali"** (placeholder, user may rename). First tenant: **Gazab Momos** (a momos shop).

**Locked scope decisions (2026-09-03, two Q&A rounds):**
- Operating model: **full multi-tenant SaaS sold to independent restaurant owners** — user wants the self-serve billing/onboarding layer built (not a Gazab-Momos-first cut), but agreed it sequences to Milestone C
- Tenant shape: **Brand → many outlets**, config cascades platform → brand → outlet
- Payments: **each outlet brings its own gateway keys** (Razorpay/PhonePe/PayTM/COD) — no RBI PA licence needed
- Order paths in v1: **counter (staff billing), takeaway, dine-in QR, reservations, delivery** (own riders first, aggregator APIs at Milestone D). Counter billing = lightweight staff order-entry, NOT a full POS (no shifts/cash reconciliation until D)
- Menu: **sizes + options + add-ons from the start**; combos later
- **Solo build.** Year-one expectation <20 tenants → hand-onboarding fine early
- Editor: **TipTap** + media upload through storage layer

**STACK (user-specified, 2026-09-03):**
- FE storefront: **Next.js** (App Router, SSR/ISR/CSR via page templates)
- Admin: **React + Vite SPA** (served static; CMS + counter billing live here)
- API: **Node + Express** (TypeScript, modular monolith) + BullMQ worker + Socket.IO
- DB: **MongoDB** — use **Atlas** (managed; transactions need a replica set). Mongoose. NO row-level security → one mandatory tenant-scoping Mongoose plugin IS the isolation boundary (injects brandId/outletId, throws on unscoped query)
- Host: **own VPS** (already running **nginx** — user confirmed, staying on nginx) + Docker Compose. Caddy is NOT adopted; only to be reconsidered at Milestone C when per-tenant custom domains need runtime TLS issuance (nginx alternative there = a certbot-automation layer). Subdomains use one wildcard cert on nginx — no issue. Cloudflare CDN/R2. MongoDB Atlas confirmed.
- Hostnames: outlet served on **subdomain AND/OR its own custom domain** — both confirmed requirements. Outlet has primary hostname + optional additional hostnames, one canonical + others 301-redirect. Custom domains = Milestone C.

**"How do code updates reach all 100 restaurants?" (user asked 2026-09-03):** This is automatic from multi-tenancy — ONE codebase, ONE deployment serves all tenants; a restaurant is data (Mongo docs + hostnames), not a code copy. Commit → CI builds Docker images → VPS pulls → rolling restart → all tenants on new code at once. Deploy effort is constant regardless of tenant count. Disciplines: (1) one bug = everyone's bug instantly → staging mirror + E2E on money paths + synthetic order check + 1-command rollback (keep previous image); (2) deploy ≠ release → ship behind feature flag off, enable Gazab Momos first, then roll out / gate to plan tier. DB migrations run ONCE on shared DB (never per-tenant): additive changes need none; structural changes = versioned migrate-mongo scripts, batched, expand/contract pattern, tested on staging copy of prod data; config schema is versioned + migrated forward. Now documented in artifact §3 ("One codebase, one deploy") + §20 ("Database migrations").
- Shared: Turborepo+pnpm; `packages/ui` framework-agnostic (no next/* imports); Redis; Lucia auth; provider-adapter pattern for storage/payments/notifications/printing; CSS-variable design tokens + CI lint rule banning hard-coded colour.

**Timeline:** solo, full scope ~16–20 working-software months. 4 milestones: A Gazab Momos live (~mo 0–6: counter+takeaway+QR, Razorpay+COD, CMS, theming, 6 cards, menu, GST, KDS, HTML+ePOS print) · B 2nd–3rd restaurant (~mo 6–11: reservations, delivery-own-riders, combos, R2 migration, more variants) · C open self-serve (~mo 11–16: billing, plans, onboarding wizard, custom domains, super-admin) · D scale & breadth (~mo 16–22+: PhonePe/PayTM, WhatsApp, delivery aggregators, full POS, print bridge, hardening).

**Status (2026-09-03):** SCAFFOLD BUILT. User said "start creating the project" — monorepo scaffolded in `<repo>` and verified running on localhost. See [scaffold-state](#scaffold-state) for structure, ports, how to run, and known issues.

Next: build out Milestone A features on top of the scaffold (auth/RBAC, menu with variants+modifiers, CMS, counter billing, KDS, Razorpay, OTP dev-bypass, GST invoicing). See [planning-deliverable-and-open-questions](#planning-deliverable-and-open-questions).

**Honest limits surfaced:** thermal printing always needs on-site setup (ePOS LAN / cloud print / local agent); India SMS needs external DLT registration + WhatsApp needs Meta template approval; "zero code forever" is ~90-95% realistic; true marketplace payments would need RBI PA licence (avoided by BYO-keys). Mongo tenant isolation is app-layer only (discipline cost vs Postgres RLS). Menu/catalog module and GST invoicing were NOT in user's original list but are mandatory.

### scaffold-state

_OneTap monorepo scaffold — structure, ports, how to run locally, and open items_

Scaffolded 2026-09-03 in `<repo>` (folder name is "Gazab Momos"; product/repo is "OneTap"). Turborepo + pnpm workspaces. Node 24, pnpm 11.25. Git initialised, remote `origin` = https://github.com/shanuraj715/OneTap.git (NOT committed/pushed yet — repo is public + empty; user should make it private).

**Structure:**
- `apps/storefront` — Next.js 15 App Router, port **3070**, `transpilePackages` for @onetap/ui + @onetap/config-schema, `outputFileTracingRoot` set to monorepo root
- `apps/admin` — React 19 + Vite 6 SPA, port **3071**, @tanstack/react-query + react-router-dom installed (router not wired yet)
- `apps/api` — Node + Express 5 (TypeScript, ESM), port **3072**, tsx for dev, pino logger. Modules: `health`, `outlets`. Middleware: `error` (Zod/HttpError aware), `request-context` (reads x-onetap-brand/outlet headers → req.tenant)
- `packages/config-schema` — Zod: `themeTokensSchema`/`themeSchema` (+ `tokensToCssVars`), `featureFlagsSchema` (nested: ordering/reservations/payments/ui), `outletConfigSchema` (version 1) + `defaultOutletConfig()`
- `packages/db` — Mongoose 8. `connectDb`/`dbState` (connection.ts), **`tenantScope` plugin + `tenantFilter()` helper** (tenant-scope.ts — the isolation boundary; pre-hooks throw on unscoped find/update/delete, opt out with `{ allowGlobalQuery: true }`). Models: `BrandModel` (tenant root, not scoped), `OutletModel` (scoped, has hostnames[] + canonicalHostname + config)
- `packages/ui` — `Button` + `tokens.css` (CSS custom properties, light + `[data-theme=dark]` + prefers-color-scheme). Framework-agnostic, no next/* imports.

**Local MongoDB (no Docker, no Homebrew):** Homebrew mongodb-community install FAILED (needs Command Line Tools update for Xcode 26.3 — user's decision). Using **`mongodb-memory-server`** instead: `pnpm db:local` downloads a standalone mongod 7.0.24 arm64 binary (cached in `node_modules/.cache/mongodb-memory-server/`), serves 127.0.0.1:27017, data persisted in `apps/api/.local-db/` (gitignored). For real data → point `apps/api/.env` `MONGODB_URI` at Atlas.

**How to run:** `pnpm install`, then two terminals: (1) `pnpm db:local` (2) `pnpm dev` (turbo runs all 3 apps). Or `pnpm dev:api` / `dev:storefront` / `dev:admin`. Seed the Gazab Momos demo tenant: `pnpm seed` OR POST `/api/outlets/seed-demo` OR the admin button.

**Verified working 2026-09-03:** all 3 apps boot on their ports; storefront SSR-fetches API `/health` (db: connected); admin (React Query) renders health + seeded outlet; `POST /api/outlets/seed-demo` creates Brand "Gazab Momos" + Outlet "Gazab Momos — Laxmi Nagar" with full defaulted config; tenant-scope plugin + `tenantFilter` in use; `tsc --noEmit` clean across all 6 packages; `pnpm --filter @onetap/api build` compiles.

**Gotchas hit & fixed:** mongoose is CJS → models use `import mongoose from "mongoose"` + `const { Schema, model, models } = mongoose` (named `models` import fails under ESM); `@onetap/db` needs `@types/node` for mongoose's types to resolve; pnpm 11 build-script approvals go in `pnpm-workspace.yaml` under `allowBuilds:` (map of name→bool), not package.json; `mongoose.connection.readyState` can be `99` so `dbState()` uses a `Record<number,...>` not a tuple; `tsx watch` orphans child procs on kill — user's other projects on this machine: Kalki Law Associates vite (port 3051, PID varies) and another node/mongo project (do not kill).

**Vertical slice added 2026-09-03 (after "app is empty?"):** turned the scaffold into a working app.
- `packages/config-schema`: `deepMerge` (merge.ts), `themeCss(theme)` (theme.ts) — emits :root + dark CSS var blocks.
- `packages/ui`: `Header` (centered variant), `Hero`, `Footer` (shows FSSAI/GSTIN), `MenuTeaser` (veg/non-veg mark uses FIXED FSSAI colours #0E8A3E / #B23B3B — deliberately NOT themed).
- `apps/api` outlets module: `GET /api/outlets/resolve?host=&slug=` (public, cross-tenant hostname lookup, uses allowGlobalQuery), `GET /api/outlets/:id`, `PATCH /api/outlets/:id/config` (deep-merge + Zod re-validate + save; needs `x-onetap-brand` header → 401 without), `POST /api/outlets/seed-demo?reset=true`. Seed now sets real Gazab Momos identity + hostnames `["gazab-momos.localhost:3070","localhost:3070"]`.
- **KEY BUG FIXED:** `tenantFilter(ctx, ...)` adds `outletId` when ctx has it, but the Outlet doc has no outletId (it IS the outlet) → findOne returned null → 500. Outlet-level queries now use a local `byBrand(ctx)` helper (brandId only). `tenantFilter` is for models *under* an outlet (menu items, orders).
- `apps/storefront`: `src/lib/outlet.ts` (resolve by Host header, slug fallback), `src/components/ThemeStyle.tsx`, `src/app/page.tsx` = real themed outlet homepage (Header/Hero/MenuTeaser/Footer + injected theme CSS). `dynamic = "force-dynamic"`.
- `apps/admin`: react-router-dom wired (BrowserRouter in main.tsx). Shell in App.tsx (sidebar: Dashboard/Theme/Settings + "View storefront"). Routes: `Dashboard` (outlet summary, feature flags, health, seed btn), `ThemeEditor` (colour pickers → PATCH theme.light, live preview card), `Settings` (identity form → PATCH identity). `src/lib/useOutlet.ts` (React Query hooks), `src/ui.tsx` (Card/Field/ColorInput/TextInput/PageHeader/Toast).
- **VERIFIED end-to-end:** PATCH primary colour with tenant headers → storefront SSR reflects it immediately; unscoped PATCH → 401. All 6 packages `tsc --noEmit` clean.
- Preview screenshots (gitignored): `.onetap-preview-{storefront,admin,theme-editor}.png` in project root.

**MENU MODULE built 2026-09-03 (3rd slice):**

Helpers: `formatINR`, `itemFromPrice`, `itemPriceLabel`. **Money is always integer paise.**

All use `tenantScope`; these DO live under an outlet so queries use `tenantFilter(ctx)` (brandId + outletId) — correct, unlike the Outlet model.

Wired into `seedGazabMomos` (reset also clears menu). Registered at `/api/menu` in app.ts.

Sample items chosen to include one with sizes+tag, one plain, one sold-out.

Both shown in the gallery frame and the admin picker. `pick()` in registry.ts resolves by id, code, or legacy unprefixed id and falls back to the first entry. `allVariants` exports the flat list.

**AUTH + RBAC built 2026-09-03 (5th slice):**
- `packages/config-schema/src/rbac.ts` — `ROLES` (super_admin, owner, manager, staff, kitchen, editor, accountant), `ROLE_LABELS`, `ROLE_DESCRIPTIONS`, `PERMISSIONS` (`outlet:read/update`, `menu:read/update`, `appearance:update`, `theme:update`, `settings:update`, `user:read/manage`), `ROLE_PERMISSIONS`, `permissionsForRole`, `sessionUserSchema`.
- `packages/db/src/models/user.ts` — `UserModel` (email unique, passwordHash, isSuperAdmin, embedded `memberships[{brandId, role, outletIds}]`, isActive) — deliberately NOT tenant-scoped (login is by email before a tenant is known). `SessionModel` (sha256 tokenHash, userId, expiresAt with a TTL index).
- `apps/api/src/modules/auth/` — `password.ts` (node:crypto **scrypt**, no native dep), `auth.service.ts` (login/logout/userFromToken/toSessionUser/ensureOwner), `auth.routes.ts` (`POST /api/auth/login` sets an httpOnly SameSite=Lax cookie `onetap_session`, `POST /logout`, `GET /me`).
- `apps/api/src/middleware/auth.ts` — **replaced `request-context.ts`**. `authenticate` (attaches req.user), `requireUser`, `requireOutletContext(req, permission)` and `requireBrandContext`. KEY: the client still sends `x-onetap-outlet`, but brand + rights now come from the SESSION — a forged header buys nothing. `x-onetap-brand` is gone.
- `apps/api/src/modules/users/users.routes.ts` — brand-scoped user CRUD behind `user:read`/`user:manage`; can't change your own role or delete yourself.
- Outlet config PATCH maps each config area to its own permission (theme→`theme:update`, layout→`appearance:update`, identity→`settings:update`).
- Added `cookie-parser`. `app.ts` mounts `/api/auth`, `/api/users`, runs `authenticate` globally.
- Seed also calls `ensureOwner` → **owner@gazabmomos.example / momos1234** (dev only), returned from `seed-demo`.
- `apps/admin` — `lib/useAuth.ts` (useAuth/useLogin/useLogout/useUsers + mutations), `routes/Login.tsx` (with a "Set up demo data" button that calls the unauthenticated dev seed, solving the chicken-and-egg), `routes/Users.tsx` (team list, role select, enable/disable, remove, add user, role reference table), `App.tsx` gates on session, nav is permission-filtered, sidebar shows user + role + Sign out. All API calls use `credentials: "include"`.
- **VERIFIED end-to-end:** unauthenticated writes/users/me → 401; wrong password → 401 (non-leaky); owner login → cookie + 9 perms; owner writes OK; kitchen role → menu write 403 "Your role can't do this (menu:update)", users list 403, menu READ 200; logout kills session; storefront + public menu still 200. All 6 packages typecheck clean.

**CART + CHECKOUT built 2026-09-03 (6th slice):**
- `packages/config-schema/src/tax.ts` — `taxSchema { pricesIncludeTax (default TRUE), defaultGstRatePct 5, placeOfSupply, serviceChargePct }`, added to outletConfig as `config.tax`.
- `packages/config-schema/src/order.ts` — **the pricing engine** (`priceCart`, `totalsFor`, `PricingError`) plus `ORDER_CHANNELS` (takeaway/counter/dine-in/delivery), `ORDER_STATUSES` (placed→accepted→preparing→ready→completed, +cancelled), `NEXT_STATUSES` transition map, `cartLineSchema`/`cartSchema`, `PricedLine`/`OrderTotals`/`PricedOrder`. Tax-inclusive math: `net = round(lineTotal / (1+rate))`, CGST/SGST split, grand total rounded to the rupee with `roundOff` recorded. **Client prices are never trusted** — the server always re-prices from the live menu, and validates that a chosen variant is required/exists and that modifier options belong to a group actually attached to that item.
- `packages/db/src/models/order.ts` — `OrderModel` (tenant-scoped; **immutable line snapshot** so menu edits never rewrite history; statusHistory; unique index brandId+outletId+orderNumber), `CounterModel` + `nextSequence(key)` (atomic `$inc` upsert — used for daily order numbers now, GST invoice numbers later).
- `packages/db/src/models/customer.ts` — `CustomerModel` (per-brand diner, phone/email), `OtpChallengeModel` (sha256 codeHash, attempts, TTL index), `CustomerSessionModel` (TTL index).
- `apps/api/src/modules/notifications/notify.ts` — **placeholder provider registry**. `sendOtp` logs and returns `devCode` outside production; `hasProvider()` returns false. Production with no provider → OTP request throws 503 rather than letting unverified orders through.
- `apps/api/src/modules/customer/` — OTP request/verify (rate limits: 5 codes/hour per destination, 5 attempts per challenge, 10-min TTL), `onetap_customer` httpOnly cookie, `/me`, `/logout`. `currentCustomer(req)` helper.
- `apps/api/src/modules/orders/` — `POST /quote` (public), `POST /` (place; **takeaway requires a verified customer session, counter requires staff `order:update`**; checks the channel's feature flag), `GET /` (staff, `order:read`), `PATCH /:id/status` (staff, `order:update`, validates the transition; COD auto-marks paid on completion), `GET /:id?outletId=` (the diner's own order only).
- New permissions `order:read` / `order:update`; granted to owner/manager/staff/kitchen (accountant read-only).
- `packages/ui/MenuList` gained optional `onSelectItem` — cards become clickable (keyboard accessible) without touching the 11 card variants.
- `apps/storefront/src/components/Ordering.tsx` — one `"use client"` component: cart in localStorage per outlet, item customiser modal (variant radios + add-on checkboxes + stepper), floating cart bar, slide-over panel with cart → OTP verify → confirmation. Server-quoted totals shown live.
- `apps/admin` — `/orders` route: live queue polling every 10s, status filter chips, per-order lines, next-status buttons driven by `NEXT_STATUSES`.
- **VERIFIED end-to-end:** quote rejects a missing required variant; 8pcs ₹90 + Cheese Dip ₹30 ×2 = ₹240 → CGST ₹5.72 / SGST ₹5.71 on ₹228.57 taxable ✓; order without OTP → 401; wrong OTP → 400; verify → order #0001 placed; staff advanced placed→accepted→preparing→ready→completed (COD flipped to paid); illegal transition → 409; another diner reading the order → 401, owner → 200. All 6 packages typecheck clean.

**TYPOGRAPHY + 18-FAMILY VARIANT SYSTEM built 2026-09-03 (7th slice):**
- `packages/config-schema/src/typography.ts` — `FONT_FAMILIES` catalogue (19 curated faces: system + Google sans/serif/display/mono; **no arbitrary uploads — web-font licensing is the tenant's risk**), `typographySchema { headingFont, bodyFont, baseSize, scale (compact/default/spacious), headingWeight, headingLetterSpacing, uppercaseHeadings }`, `googleFontsUrl()`, `typographyCss()` (emits `--font-heading/-body/-size-base/-weight-heading/--letter-spacing-heading/--text-sm…3xl`). Added to config as `config.typography`.
- `ThemeStyle` now takes `{theme, typography}`, injects the Google Fonts `<link>` + both token blocks + base body/heading rules.
- Admin `/typography` (`routes/TypographyEditor.tsx`) — font pickers grouped by category, **each button rendered in its own typeface** (preloads all catalogue fonts), base-size slider, scale/weight/letter-spacing pills, uppercase toggle, live preview. Gated on `theme:update`.
- **`packages/ui/src/variants/`** — 15 new component families, all token-driven: `buttons`(10) `badges`(8) `chips`(8) `alerts`(7) `toasts`(7) `modals`(8) `popovers`(7) `accordions`(8) `faqs`(7) `contentCards`(8) `carousels`(7) `dropdowns`(7) `listGroups`(8) `pagination`(7) `progress`(8). Plus headers 5→**8** and footers 4→**8**. `variants/types.ts` holds the shared prop shapes + `Tone` (info/success/warning/danger).
- `tokens.css` gained **semantic status tokens** `--tone-{info,success,warning,danger}` + `-wash`, deliberately independent of the brand accent (light + dark).
- **`registry.tsx` (renamed from .ts — it contains JSX)** is now a GENERIC SLOT REGISTRY: `VariantSlot { key (config.layout field), label, description, flush?, variants, preview(meta, ctx) }` and `VARIANT_SLOTS` (18 entries). `PreviewContext` carries outlet identity + sample menu items. Adding a whole family = one slot entry. `getVariant(slotKey, ref)` + `allVariants` + `slotByKey`.
- `layoutSchema` now has 18 slot fields (headerVariant, itemCardVariant, buttonVariant, …, footerVariant).
- Storefront `/variants` gallery + admin `/appearance` both loop `VARIANT_SLOTS` — fully generic, gallery has a jump-to index.
- **TOTAL: 142 variants across 18 families.** Codes: H1–H8, C01–C11, BTN01–BTN10, BDG/CHP/ALT/TST/MDL/POP/ACC/FAQ/CNT/CRS/DRP/LST/PAG/PRG + F1–F8.
- **RSC gotchas hit & fixed:** (a) `registry.ts` had JSX → must be `.tsx`, and Next's dev cache needed `rm -rf apps/storefront/.next` + restart after the rename; (b) interactive families need `"use client"` (buttons, chips, modals, accordions, faqs, dropdowns, carousels, popovers); (c) **a server-rendered preview cannot pass a function prop to a client component** — `ChipProps` gained a serializable `removable?: boolean` separate from `onRemove`, so the gallery shows the × without passing a handler.
- **VERIFIED:** gallery 200 with all 18 families rendering; PATCHing `config.typography` (playfair+lora → bebas+work-sans → inter) is reflected on the storefront each time. All 6 packages typecheck clean.

**PAYMENTS built 2026-09-03 (8th slice):**
- `packages/config-schema/src/payments.ts` — `GATEWAYS` = cod | razorpay | mock, `GATEWAY_LABELS/DESCRIPTIONS`, `GATEWAY_FIELDS` (per-gateway credential spec with `secret: boolean`), `paymentSettingsSchema { enabled: Gateway[], testMode }` → `config.payments`. **Credentials are deliberately NOT in outlet config** (that blob is public to the storefront).
- `packages/db/src/models/payment.ts` — `PaymentCredentialModel` (per outlet+gateway, `publicFields` plain + `encryptedFields` AES-GCM envelopes, unique index), `PaymentModel` (one attempt per intent: gateway, status, amount, gatewayOrderId/PaymentId, `events[]` audit trail), `WebhookEventModel` (unique gateway+eventId, 30-day TTL → replay protection).
- `apps/api/src/lib/crypto.ts` — AES-256-GCM `encryptSecret`/`decryptSecret` (`v1.<iv>.<tag>.<ct>` base64url), `maskSecret`, `safeEqual`. Key from `ENCRYPTION_KEY` env (scrypt-derived); **process exits at boot if the dev default is still set in production**.
- `apps/api/src/modules/payments/providers/` — `PaymentProvider` interface (`createIntent`/`verify`/`parseWebhook`), adapters: `cod` (no gateway), `razorpay` (real API: POST /v1/orders with Basic auth, HMAC-SHA256 `order|payment` signature verify, webhook `x-razorpay-signature` check), `mock` (simulated gateway for local testing, refuses to run unless `ALLOW_MOCK_GATEWAY`). Registry in `providers/index.ts`.
- `payments.service.ts` — `listGatewayConfig` (masked), `saveCredentials` (empty secret = keep existing), `createIntent`, `verifyPayment`, `handleWebhook` (finds the outlet via the payment's gatewayOrderId, verifies per-outlet secret, dedupes via unique index), `listPaymentsForOrder`.
- `payments.routes.ts` — `/config` GET/PUT/DELETE (`payment-config:read`/`:manage`), `/intent` + `/verify` (diner, ownership-checked), `/webhook/:gateway` (public, trust from signature), `/order/:orderId` (staff audit).
- `app.ts` — `express.json({ verify })` captures `req.rawBody` so webhook signatures can be checked over exact bytes.
- **Order.payment reshaped**: `{ gateway: Gateway; status: PaymentStatus; paymentId? }` (was `method: "cod"|"online"`). `placeOrder` takes `gateway`.
- New permissions `payment-config:read` / `payment-config:manage` / `order:refund`.
- Admin `/payments` — enable/disable gateways at checkout, per-gateway credential forms (secrets masked, blank = unchanged), Razorpay webhook URL, security notes.
- Storefront checkout gained a payment-method chooser; `payOnline()` runs the gateway's own checkout (mock inline; Razorpay loads checkout.js) then posts the result for **server-side** signature verification.
- **VERIFIED:** gateway config lists cod/mock/razorpay correctly; Razorpay keys saved → keyId visible, secrets masked `iNtP••••EkMc`; kitchen role → 403; order placed `mock/pending`; **forged signature → 400 "Signature did not match"**, real signature → 200 paid; order flips to `mock/paid`; audit trail shows both attempts; another diner → 403; **zero secret leakage in the public `/api/outlets/resolve` config**. All 6 packages typecheck clean.
- ⚠️ **Razorpay adapter is written to their documented API but NEVER exercised against their servers** — no real credentials. The mock gateway proves the flow shape end to end.
- **Gateway-error handling (2026-09-03):** a failure from a gateway's own API (bad keys, account not activated) used to become a generic **500 "Internal server error"** — `createIntent`/`verifyPayment` throw a plain `Error` from the provider, which the error middleware doesn't recognise. Now wrapped → **502** with the real reason surfaced ("Razorpay could not start this payment: Authentication failed. Check the razorpay keys in Admin → Payments."). Storefront `VerifyStep` also made **payment-retry-safe**: `verified` + `pendingOrder` state so a retry after a payment hiccup resumes (doesn't re-verify a consumed OTP or re-place the order); button switches to "Try payment again", with a "Order #N is saved but not paid" note. **The demo outlet's stored Razorpay keys are placeholders (`rzp_test_<key>`)** — real test keys must be entered in Admin → Payments, or use the "Test gateway (dev only)" (`mock`) option.

**PAYMENT-PENDING ORDER BUCKET (2026-09-03):** an unpaid prepaid order (`payment.gateway != "cod"` && `payment.status == "pending"` && not cancelled) = abandoned checkout / failed payment; **hidden from the default order list** so it doesn't clutter the queue or fire SLA chimes. `listOrders(ctx, {..., paymentPending: "hide"|"only"|"all"})` — `hide` is the default (`$or: [{gateway:"cod"},{status:{$ne:"pending"}}]`), `only` narrows to the bucket; the response always carries `counts.paymentPending` (fresh `countDocuments`). `GET /api/orders?paymentPending=only|all`. Admin: `useOrders(o,status,poll,paymentPending)`, a **"Payment pending (N)" toggle chip** on the Orders toolbar → server-side `only` view; header adds "· N awaiting payment"; SLA suppressed in that view. **Verified:** unpaid mock order hidden from default + counted, shown in `only`, COD-pending still visible in default, order returns to default once paid + count drops. Also **search box widened 210→300px with a focus ring on the wrapper** (was clipping the placeholder). Cleaned 7 abandoned test orders from the demo db.

**DARK-MODE THEME BUG FIXED + MOTION + DINE-IN QR built 2026-09-03 (9th slice):**
- **BUG (user-reported as "ALT04 alert text invisible"), root cause was wider:** `themeCss` did `{...light, ...(theme.dark ?? {})}`. Mongo drops empty objects, so `theme.dark` was MISSING → the dark-mode block emitted the LIGHT palette (`--color-text: #1A1A1A`) while `--tone-*-wash` correctly went dark → dark text on dark wash in EVERY tone-using component. Fixed in `theme.ts`: added `DARK_FALLBACK` + `resolveDarkTokens()` so an outlet with no dark palette gets proper dark surfaces (brand colour, radius, fonts still carry over), and **moved the `--tone-*` tokens into `themeCss` itself** (TONES_LIGHT/TONES_DARK) so colours can never desync between tokens.css and the per-outlet block. Verified: dark block now emits `bg=#14130F text=#ECE6D8 successWash=#17281C`.
- **Motion system** in `tokens.css`: `--motion-fast/base/slow`, `--ease-out/in-out/spring`; keyframes `ot-fade/pop/drop/rise/slide-in/sheet-up`; classes `.ot-anim-*`, `.ot-collapse` (grid-template-rows 0fr→1fr — animates to auto height with no JS measuring), `.ot-press`, `.ot-nav` (for controls that own their transform, e.g. centred carousel arrows — `.ot-press` scale would fight `translateY(-50%)`), `.ot-lift`, `.ot-chevron`, `.ot-bar`, `.ot-swap`. **One `prefers-reduced-motion` block disables all of it.** Applied across buttons, chips, alerts, toasts, modals (pop/sheet/drawer), dropdowns, popovers, accordions, FAQs, carousels, progress, pagination, list groups, content cards.
- **Dine-in QR + table sessions:** `packages/config-schema/src/tables.ts` (TABLE_STATUSES free/seated/bill-requested/needs-cleaning/reserved). `packages/db/src/models/table.ts` — `TableModel` (number, zone, seats, status, activeSessionId, **qrSecret**) + `TableSessionModel` (tableId, customerId, status, **tableHistory[]** = the move audit trail). Orders gained `tableId`/`sessionId`.
- QR: `tableToken()` = HMAC(ENCRYPTION_KEY, `outletId|tableId|qrSecret`) truncated to 24 chars; URL `/t/<tableId>?k=<token>`; PNG via the `qrcode` package (in apps/api). **Rotating `qrSecret` invalidates every printed code.**
- `tables.service.ts` / `tables.routes.ts` — CRUD (`table:read`/`table:manage`), `/：id/qr` + `/qr/rotate`, public `/scan/:tableId` (signature-checked), `/scan/:tableId/session` (needs verified diner), `/sessions/active`, **`/sessions/:id/move`** (orders follow, both tables restatused, history appended), `/sessions/:id/close`, `/sessions/:id/tab`. `dineInContext()` in orders.routes rejects a dine-in order whose sessionId isn't the caller's open session.
- `/api/outlets/resolve` now also accepts `?id=`, for the table landing page.
- Admin `/tables` — floor grid by zone with status colours, add table, QR panel (PNG download + rotate + remove), **"Seated now"** list with move-to dropdown + close + move history.
- Storefront `/t/[tableId]` — validates the signed code server-side, themed shell, `Ordering` extended with a `dineIn` context: rejoins an open session silently, skips the payment chooser (one tab settled at the end), shows a "Ordering to Table N / Running tab ₹X" banner, and "Send to kitchen" once seated.
- **VERIFIED:** forged QR token → 403; unverified diner → 401; two dine-in orders → running tab ₹425; dine-in with no sessionId → 400; a different diner ordering on that session → 403 and seating at an occupied table → 409; **move 5→7 carried the ₹425 tab and 2 orders, left 5 "needs-cleaning" and 7 "seated", history "5 → 7"**; move onto an occupied table → 409; QR rotate changed the token; close → session closed. Storefront `/t/...` renders the themed menu; forged link shows "This code didn't work". All 6 packages typecheck clean.
- ⚠️ Two apparent failures in my first test run were **test-harness bugs** (sent GET instead of POST to `/qr/rotate` and `/sessions/:id/close`) — not product bugs.
