### local-dev-servers

_The user's own local dev servers (ports 3070-3073) and how they were started; permission to stop the storefront one for testing; STATE: storefront stopped by me for testing and RESTARTED 2026-11-15 (log in the session scratchpad); kill test servers by PID, never by name_

**Permission (2026-11-15, user's words):** "for test you can stop the server and run for your test. after test restart the dev server. (local server)". So stopping the LOCAL storefront dev server for my own testing is allowed, but **I must restart it afterwards** — and tell the user. Without that permission the standing rule is: never run `next build` or a second `next dev` in `apps/storefront`.

**How they were started (found 2026-11-15; all with nvm node v24.3.0, launchers are orphaned `pnpm` processes, PPID 1):**
- API :3072 + admin vite :3071 — one root `pnpm dev` (→ `turbo run dev`; the API runs as `node --watch src/server.js`) since Nov 6.
- **Storefront :3070 — `pnpm dev` run inside `apps/storefront` (→ `next dev -p 3070`)**, since Nov 13. Env comes from `apps/storefront/.env.local` (`NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL`), nothing special exported.
- Website :3073 — root `pnpm dev:website` (→ `pnpm --filter @onetap/website dev` → `next dev -p 3073`), since Nov 14.

**To restart the storefront exactly as it was:**
`cd "<repo>/apps/storefront" && rm -rf .next && (PATH="<home>" nohup pnpm dev > <scratchpad>/storefront-dev.log 2>&1 &)` — then check `lsof -iTCP:3070 -sTCP:LISTEN` and that `http://localhost:3070/` answers. (`rm -rf .next` because my own `next dev` runs leave a cache and the routes/components changed a lot; the user's terminal no longer owns the process, so tell them where the log is.)

**STATE — RESTARTED 2026-11-15 (afternoon): storefront is running again on :3070**, started with the recipe above (`rm -rf .next`, then `pnpm dev`) by me, so the user's old terminal does not own it; its log is `<session scratchpad>/storefront-dev.log` (…/scratchpad/storefront-dev.log of session 04b5269f). To take it back: `lsof -ti:3070 | xargs kill`, then `pnpm dev` in `apps/storefront` in their own terminal. The user was told. API/admin/website were left running the whole time (the API is `node --watch`, so it reloaded itself on every edit of `apps/api/src` — against the REAL database).

**Lesson (2026-11-15):** to stop a test server, kill by PID from `lsof -iTCP:<port> -sTCP:LISTEN` — a name pattern like `pkill -f next-server` also matches the user's website dev server (:3073) and was blocked by the permission classifier. My own test ports: sandbox API :3097, test storefront :3170, throwaway admin preview :3171.

Related: [store-disable](#store-disable), [dev-server-mytimelyne](#dev-server-mytimelyne).

### test-bench

_Feature-testing harness (set up 2026-11-15): throwaway API+dashboard+storefront with fake data on ports 3097/3171/3170, driven in a VISIBLE Chrome window with a red cursor + captions; how to start/reset/rebuild, logins, rules, and why it exists_

The user wants to test the product feature by feature ("if a feature is disabled in the admin panel, what is the storefront flow?") and **watch** it. I have NO tool to control their own Chrome (no Claude-in-Chrome / built-in browser / computer-use in this session — checked via ToolSearch), so I drive a separate **visible Google Chrome** (Playwright `channel: "chrome"`, `headless: false`, clean temp profile) that opens on their screen.

**Where (UPDATED 2026-11-15): the bench + all tests now live in the repo at `tests/e2e/` (untracked/uncommitted until the user says; run `node run.mjs`, see its README and `docs/CATALOG.md`) — see [feedback-save-every-test](#feedback-save-every-test). The old scratchpad copy below is obsolete.** (Old: `<session scratchpad>/bench/` — VOLATILE, wiped once by a reboot.) Files: `serve-bench.mjs` (in-memory API + seed), `bench.sh` (up | down | reset | rebuild | status), `watch.mjs` (visible-browser helper: `openWatch()`, `d.say/click/type/choose/scroll/shot/check/hold`, `adminLogin`), `demo.mjs`, `smoke.mjs`, README.md. If gone, rebuild from this note; offered the user to keep it in the repo (e.g. `tools/test-bench/`) — not decided.

**Rules:** the real database is NEVER used for testing — `serve-bench.mjs` chdirs away from any `.env` and uses mongodb-memory-server. Only ports 3097/3171/3170; stop by killing the LISTENING pid only (never `pkill` by name — it would hit the user's 3070-3073 servers). The storefront under test is a production build in `bench/storefront` (rsync of `apps/storefront/src` + the real next.config minus outputFileTracingRoot), NEVER in `apps/storefront/.next` (that's the user's dev server's).

**Seed:** super admin `super@sandbox.test/Super123!`; Acme Momos on a "Pro" plan (all features) — owner `o@acme.test/Owner123!`, manager `m@acme.test/Manager123!`, cashier `s@acme.test/Staff1234!`, kitchen `k@acme.test/Kitchen123!` — outlets Main (takeaway+delivery+dine-in, cash+mock gateway, 5 km radius, ₹30 fee) at `http://localhost:3170/main` and Second (takeaway only); Beta Bites on a "Starter" plan (takeaway, dine-in, coupons only) at `http://beta.localhost:3170/only`, owner `o@beta.test/Owner123!`. Menu with variants, a sold-out item, tables T1–T5, coupons SAVE20/FLAT50/WELCOME15 (Beta: BETA10), message bar + carousel + published pages, WhatsApp numbers/chats (worker off), returning customer Asha Rao 9876543210 with 50 coins. Any phone signs in with code 123456.

**Gotchas found:** Settings sub-tabs are `role="tab"`; Next must be started through `apps/storefront/node_modules/.bin/next` (pnpm shim sets NODE_PATH) because `lucide-react` is imported by the storefront but NOT declared in `apps/storefront/package.json` (latent bug — works only through pnpm hoisting). A full-page overlay injected in React 19 apps mounts fine on `body` after DOMContentLoaded.

**Test style per feature:** do it in the dashboard (visible), open the storefront, record what changes, then try to bypass via the API (server must enforce what the UI hides); report bugs found; `./bench.sh reset` between tests when state matters.

**Update (same day):** the user asked for a FULL-SIZE Chrome window — `openWatch()` now launches with `viewport: null` and maximizes through CDP (`Browser.setWindowBounds`, windowState "maximized"; verified: outer size = screen available size, 2056×1289 on the user's display) and supports named tabs in one window (`d.tab("customer", "label")` / `d.tab("staff")`, a red who-label at the bottom-left, `d.spot()` outlines an element, `d.press`). `HEADLESS=1 SLOW=0.15` = fast dry run without a window — always dry-run first, then `./bench.sh reset`, then run visibly in the background (`nohup node t1-takeaway.mjs > logs/t1.log &`, poll for `^SUMMARY`; the script then `hold()`s the window open for the user). The API now attaches the live feed (`attachRealtime`/`attachPreview`) so the Orders page shows "Live".

**Test 1 done — takeaway order ≥ ₹999 (`t1-takeaway.mjs`):** new customer signs up (name+phone → code 123456 → gender/age/email), 8 items ₹1,030 cash at counter → order #0001 → appears LIVE on the staff Orders screen → Accepted → Preparing → Ready → Completed (each with a confirm box; Enter confirms) → payment auto "paid", status history has all 5 steps, Dashboard home Revenue today ₹1,030, coins 103 earned at completion (never at placement). 32/32 checks. **Finding:** the storefront has NO order-tracking/"my orders" page — the customer's confirmation screen never updates after "Order #… placed"; progress reaches them only via WhatsApp/SMS alerts if the owner switched them on (off by default). `GET /api/orders/:id` already lets a signed-in customer read their own order, so a tracking page is feasible. Also: no minimum-order-value rule is set on the bench stores (₹999 was just the cart size).

**Repo layout (tests/e2e, standalone npm project, Playwright 1.63.0):** `bench/{serve-bench,bench,watch,suite}.mjs`, `tests/tNN-*.mjs` (each exports `meta` + `run(d,h)`), `suites/` (older stand-alone scripts wrapped by `runSuite`), `run.mjs` (flags --visible --only --hold --rebuild --docs-only --list; auto-rebuilds when app code is newer than the bench build; resets fake data before `fresh` tests), generated `docs/*.md` + `docs/CATALOG.md`, `results/latest.json` + `history.jsonl` (commit these), `.bench/` (builds/logs, git-ignored). Tests so far: t00 bench smoke, t01 takeaway order (found a real cross-store order-read leak in `GET /api/orders/:id?outletId=` — signed-in staff branch had no membership check; FIXED 2026-11-15 in orders.routes.js — uses requireOutletContext, non-members fall through to the customer rules; uncommitted, not deployed), t02 WhatsApp API (117 checks; real-QR phase opt-in WA_REAL_QR=1), t03-t05 WhatsApp dashboard UI, t06 storefront header/categories, t07 favicon plan rules. Rsync to the dev server must `--exclude tests`. The mongodb-memory-server comes from apps/api.

**2026-11-15 later — t08–t12 added (lifecycle chain, all pass):** t08 plans Basic/Pro/Custom + Basic→Pro upgrade + outlets 1-3 + menus + every outlet setting read back + delivery radius/fee + load management (68 checks; bench API now has `STORE_SUBDOMAIN_BASE=localhost:3170`, so `<label>.localhost:3170` works), t09 subdomain, t10 downgrade/grace/after-grace/disabled outlet, t11 owner deletes outlet (outlets with orders are protected, so outlet 3 deliberately never takes an order; a blocked outlet can't be managed by its owner), t12 delete store. Findings: load-management 'stopped' only blocks DELIVERY server-side (takeaway still accepted). Rules learned: after grace nothing auto-blocks; plan features gate via `applyEntitlements` on the public config (stored owner switches are kept) and `requireFeature` only on coupons/printing admin routes.

**t08–t12 rewritten Chrome-only (2026-11-15, all pass headless 42/36/37/13/19; headless chain ~6.5 min, visible at SLOW=0.5 ~22 min):** `bench/ui.mjs` is the screen-helper library; platform Outlets tab lists outlets by customer-facing name (identity title), owner's Basic dashboard greys out Add outlet (tooltip gives the reason) and shows Delivery/Coin wallet as "Not in your plan"; an outlet with orders can't be deleted ('order history'); remove-address uses an inline "Yes, remove it".

### rename-merge-rasoi-pulse

_2026-11-25 — feature/rename-to-rasoi-pulse merged into main (ce6c45e); the encryption-salt trap found and fixed (t79); what a production deploy of the rename changes (logouts, localStorage resets, print-agent header); NOT yet deployed_

User: "merge feature/rename-to-rasoi-pulse to main and tell me whether needs to deploy again." Done in the `main` worktree (`$SCRATCH/main-wt`), merge commit 2eef40d, pushed (main = ce6c45e after later commits). Only conflict: generated `tests/e2e/results/history.jsonl` (kept main's side, dropped one old failed t78 line). Merged tree == the rename branch tree. Rename covers 395 files: package scope `@onetap/*`→`@rasoipulse/*`, header `x-onetap-outlet/-brand/-agent`→`x-rasoipulse-*`, cookies `onetap_session/_customer/_outlet_slug`→`rasoipulse_*`, localStorage keys `onetap.*`→`rasoipulse.*`, visible "TablePe"→"Rasoi Pulse", default DB name `onetap_dev`→`rasoipulse_dev` (prod `.env` sets `MONGODB_DB=onetap` explicitly, so unaffected). No DB model/collection renames, no env-variable renames. Verified from the merged tree: frozen install, admin + storefront build, API `createApp()` loads (324 routes).

**THE TRAP (fixed in 008aff0):** the rename also changed the scrypt salt in `apps/api/src/lib/crypto.js` (`"onetap.payment-credentials.v1"`→`"rasoipulse…"`). That salt derives the AES key for EVERY stored secret (gateway keys, R2/S3, AI keys, SMS/WhatsApp Business tokens, printer cloud keys, linked WhatsApp sessions `whatsapp/auth-state.js`), so a deploy would have made all of them unreadable. Restored to the original text with a "DO NOT RENAME" comment; test t79 (stored-secrets) uses a golden envelope made by an independent copy of the algorithm and fails (3 checks) if the salt is changed. **Rule: when a rename/refactor touches crypto salts, HMAC pepper, cookie names, header names or storage keys, treat them as DATA FORMATS not text — grep for them explicitly.** The rename branch itself still has the bug (it was not changed).

**What deploying the rename to production does (not deployed yet; ask first — [feedback-ask-before-prod-deploy](#feedback-ask-before-prod-deploy)):** all four apps must go together (API + admin + storefront + website; the outlet header name changes on both sides; an old admin tab breaks until reloaded); needs `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` on the server (lockfile changed) and rebuilds one app at a time; everyone signed in is signed out once (cookie names); browser-saved settings reset (print-station choice per device, selected outlet, sidebar, kitchen sound, carts, in-progress "Take order" drafts); external print agents must send `x-rasoipulse-agent` (agent program is NOT in the repo; only `printing/agent-auth.js` is) — unknown whether any is in use, could make the API accept both headers; health `service` field is now "rasoipulse-api". Read-only DB scan: the old product name appears only in `platformstorages` and `storagemigrations` (internal probe text), so no stored website/page content needs editing.

**Working-folder note:** the user's checkout is still on `feature/rename-to-rasoi-pulse` (does NOT have the salt fix or later menu commit) — never deploy from it; deploy from main's SHA via a clean worktree. Recipe for tests from a worktree: symlink `tests/e2e/node_modules` from the user's folder (remove before commit), `NODE_OPTIONS="--import file://$SCRATCH/force-chrome.mjs"`, `node run.mjs --only tNN --rebuild`, then kill ONLY the bench pids whose cwd is the worktree (ports 3097/3170/3171).

**Update (same day):** the user ran `git switch main` and git refused because MY worktree `$SCRATCH/main-wt` held `main`. I removed it (`git worktree remove --force`, it was clean + pushed). Lesson: a worktree on `main` blocks the user's own checkout of `main` — when I'm done with one, remove it at once (or work on a throwaway branch name). Only `deploy-wt` (detached) is left. If the user's folder is on `main` again, normal rules apply: commit straight on `main` in their folder.

Console route `/platform/server` (sidebar "Server", super admin only) + a health strip on the dashboard. **Not committed or pushed** (user hasn't asked; last push eba970d) — it sits with the cache/dashboard/delete work, see [cache-dashboard-store-delete](#cache-dashboard-store-delete). Deployed to the dev VPS 2026-11-15 ([dev-server-mytimelyne](#dev-server-mytimelyne)).

**Code:** API `apps/api/src/modules/sysmon/` — `parsers.js` (pure /proc + macOS parsers), `collect.js` (reads; every read tolerates failure), `monitor.js` (5 s sampler, 1 h in-memory ring, NO database writes), `health.js` (`scoreHealth`), `request-stats.js` (middleware), `sysmon.routes.js` mounted at `/api/platform/server` (`/`, `/health`, `/history`, `/processes`, `/folders`, POST `/folders/scan`). Admin: `routes/platform/PlatformServer.jsx`, `ServerParts.jsx` (HealthBoxes/LevelLabel/ServerStrip), shared chart toolkit in `routes/platform/viz/`.

**Design decisions worth keeping:**
- It describes the machine the API RUNS ON (laptop locally, the VPS on dev/prod). Linux = full detail; macOS = fallbacks (no disk speed, no per-process disk, no open-connections).
- "Remaining" memory = kernel `MemAvailable` (reclaimable cache counts); "Used" = total − available. So Used is ~180 MB higher than `free`'s "used" column — deliberate, stated on the page.
- Health = 6 parts (CPU, Load, Memory, Disk space, Disk activity, API & database), each 0–10; overall = min(average, worst+3, <7 if any part <7). Boxes are clamped into the colour band (7–10 green, 4–6 yellow, 0–3 red) so a yellow part can never make the whole thing green. Unmeasurable parts are left out, never scored 0/10. Colour is never alone: number + word + icon.
- Disk-space rule: absolute-space caps only when freePct < 25 (a mostly empty small /boot must not drag the score down).
- Folder scan ("What is using the disk") runs only on a button press, `nice`/`ionice du`, in the background; roots from env `SYSMON_SCAN_ROOTS` or defaults (/var/www, /root, /home, /opt, /var/log …).
- pm2 panel: only if the pm2 daemon pidfile is alive (asking pm2 otherwise would START it); looks for `pm2` on PATH, then beside node, then /usr/local/bin, /usr/bin — verified with a bare PATH on the VPS.
- Secrets are redacted from process command lines before they leave the API.

**Verified on the real VPS 2026-11-15** (by importing the module on the server and comparing with `free`, `df`, `ps`, `/proc`, dd, curl — no login, no DB): cores/load/swap/disk size-used-free/NIC totals/process RSS all matched to the byte; a 1.5 GB direct write showed as 1500 MB in the monitor's per-second samples with 50 % disk-busy for a 0.5 s burst; a 60 MB download showed 57.7 MiB received at ~10 MiB/s (curl said 10.4); pm2 lists 11 apps (3 online, 8 stopped). VPS facts: Ubuntu 22.04, 2 cores (Xeon Gold 6148), 1.92 GB RAM + 2 GB swap, 37 GB root LVM on `sda`, NIC `ens18`, up ~267 days.

**Gotchas / lessons:**
- Per-process disk totals (`/proc/<pid>/io` read_bytes/write_bytes) INCLUDE reaped children, so `systemd` (pid 1) shows 475 GB written and a script gets blamed for tools it ran. That is how the kernel/iotop report it; the page now says so in a note.
- The health score read "swap NaN%" until `scoreHealth` was fed the sampler's `swapUsedB` (it expected `swapFreeB`); `swapPercent()` in health.js now accepts both. Regression tests exist (health unit test + API test).
- The console follows the OS dark setting via `prefers-color-scheme` as well as `data-theme`; `viz.css` only handled `data-theme="dark"`, so charts/boxes were wrong in a dark browser (also affected the dashboard). Fixed — dark set is now written for both selectors.
- `.viz-tile` is a flex COLUMN; the dashboard strip needed an explicit `flex-direction: row`.
- LineChart now thins x-axis labels to what fits the width (phone 390 px was overprinting); the dashboard charts use the same code.
- Standalone verify scripts that `import` the monitor from `/var/www/tablepe-dev/apps/api/src/modules/sysmon/` and run with the server's node are a safe way to test Linux behaviour without logging in or touching the DB (health shows "database not reachable" there — expected, no DB connection).

**Not done / for later:** no alerts or notifications (page only); history is in memory (lost on API restart; one API process's view); on a multi-process API each process has its own request stats; no commit/PR yet.

### ops-batch-2026-11-21

_2026-11-21 batch — dine-in table auto-free on Completed, "Needs cleaning" status removed, platform WebSocket on/off switch (5 s polling), print station 8 s + chip, phone layouts; t66, deployed to dev_

User request (all deployed to dev, commits 1b8f04b + 60ffea8, test t66):
- **Table auto-free:** `freeTableIfDone` in orders.service.js — when a dine-in order becomes Completed (updateStatus or manual) and no other order on the session is open and all is paid → `closeSession` (409/404 swallowed). A table with another open order stays Seated.
- **"Needs cleaning" removed everywhere:** `TABLE_STATUSES` is now free/seated/bill-requested/reserved; `normalizeTableStatus` maps legacy values to free; closing/moving leaves the table "free"; migration script `apps/api/src/scripts/migrate-tables-needs-cleaning.js` (ran on dev DB: 0 rows). `bill-requested` and `reserved` still exist but nothing sets them automatically.
- **Free table button:** `PartyDoneBanner` at the top of the Take order menu screen (visible on a phone before any item is added); after freeing, `seenSessionId` is reset to null (otherwise the table-conflict banner appears).
- **Platform switch:** PlatformSettings `realtimeWebsocket` (default true) → Platform settings → "Live order updates". Off: hub refuses upgrades (503), closes open sockets with 4410, `me` carries `realtime.websocket`, `useOrderStream` goes to status "polling", `useOrders`/kitchen poll every 5 s (`POLL_WITHOUT_SOCKET_MS`). `me` refetches every 60 s so open pages follow. The waiter bell (kds.ready) needs the socket and is quiet while off.
- **Print station:** `PRINT_POLL_MS = 8000` (also the print-jobs list), min gap enforced for focus/visibility re-polls; sidebar chip shows green CheckCircle2 + "· Ns" countdown, spinner + "Checking…"; **never active for a super admin in the platform console** (path without /store/<name>/<id>).
- **Phone layouts:** Dashboard 2 columns on ≤599 px (hook order bug: `useMedia` must be before early returns), PageHeader wraps, Orders filters in one `.ot-hscroll` row (≤767 px), store detail header buttons short labels.
- Gotchas learned: Playwright `unroute` while a handler is awaiting → "Route is already handled" (use a flag); `checkbox.uncheck()` fails on an auto-saving controlled checkbox (use click).

### security-credentials

_Live credentials the user pasted into chat on 2026-09-03 that must be rotated — do not treat as safe_

On 2026-09-03 the user pasted, in plaintext chat:
1. A **MongoDB Atlas** connection string (user `<db-user>`, cluster `<atlas-cluster>.mongodb.net`, db `support_tickets` — this is a DIFFERENT project's cluster). Advised: rotate the DB password, lock Network Access, and use a SEPARATE Atlas project/cluster + dedicated scoped user for OneTap.
2. **Razorpay LIVE keys** — `rzp_live_...` key id + live secret. Advised: regenerate immediately (Razorpay Dashboard → Account & Settings → API Keys), check Settlements/Refunds for anything unexpected, use TEST-mode keys (`rzp_test_`) for all development.

**Never store these values anywhere** (memory, code, artifact, files). If the user references "the Atlas URI" or "the Razorpay keys", assume they should have been rotated and belong only in a git-ignored `.env` locally / encrypted-in-DB in production.

**Update 2026-09-04: the Atlas cluster is now actually in use, password NOT rotated.** User asked to move off local Mongo. I pushed back twice (reuse of a chat-pasted, unrotated credential; `support_tickets` being a different project's database); user's final call: keep the same `<db-user>` credential, just point at a new database. `apps/api/.env` `MONGODB_URI` now targets `<atlas-cluster>.mongodb.net/onetap` (was `/support_tickets`) — same cluster, same user, still the password that was pasted in plaintext chat twice. All 309 local dev documents (25 collections) were migrated over via a one-off script (deleted after). Local `mongodb-memory-server` on 127.0.0.1:27017 is no longer what the API points to (still runnable for offline dev if `.env` is switched back). **If asked to help with production hardening, security review, or anything Razorpay-live-key adjacent, resurface: this DB password has never been rotated despite being pasted in chat twice, and the OneTap app now shares an Atlas project/cluster with an unrelated `support_tickets` app under one account with (as far as known) unscoped access.** See [scaffold-state](#scaffold-state).

Also: the GitHub repo `shanuraj715/OneTap` is **public** (and was empty as of 2026-09-03) — user advised to make it private before first commit.

**How secrets flow in OneTap:** restaurant payment/SMTP/etc. credentials are entered in the admin panel → encrypted (libsodium sealed box) in MongoDB → decrypted only in memory. Never in code or env files in production. Dev: `.env` files, git-ignored. See [scaffold-state](#scaffold-state).

### cloudflare-token-compromise

_User's Cloudflare account was abused via a leaked API token (Oct 2026); attacker redirect rules send shanuthewebdev.in, food.*, mytimelyne.com to cloud.msldistributors.com — still live as of 2026-11-14_

From 2026-10-04 an attacker used a leaked Cloudflare **API token** (audit log: actor "Account", context "API token", not the user's login) to add an account-level ruleset + list, zone redirect rules on `shanuthewebdev.in` and `mytimelyne.com`, and edit 2 DNS records on `shanuthewebdev.in`. Result: those domains and subdomains (including the OneTap/Gazab prod storefront `food.shanuthewebdev.in`) 301 to `cloud.msldistributors.com` (briefly `authcloudflare.to`, a phishing-looking domain); `admin.food.`/`api.food.` fail TLS at Cloudflare. Origin server <VPS-IP> is healthy and serves 200 when hit directly with `--resolve`. No Cloudflare credential was found stored on the VPS (certbot uses nginx authenticator; WP Cloudflare plugins had no key) — leak source unknown.

**Still unfixed on 2026-11-14** (over a month later): rules still active. Fix is dashboard-only: revoke all user + account API tokens and roll Global API Key FIRST, then delete the rules/ruleset/list, restore the DNS records.

**How to apply:** if a deploy "works on the server but the site redirects / 522s / TLS fails publicly", check this first (curl the domain publicly vs `--resolve <host>:443:<VPS-IP>`). Don't blame nginx. The user asked for observation-only when investigating the server; get the go-ahead before changing nginx (offered: serve the portfolio on the bare IP instead of the `location = /` 301 to the domain). See [deploy-workflow](#deploy-workflow), [security-credentials](#security-credentials).

## Security, limits and caching

### login-protection

_Brute-force protection on dashboard + customer-code sign-in (2026-11-16, branch feature/login-protection): express-rate-limit flood cap + escalating DB-backed IP blocks, blocked IPs see a notice instead of the form, platform Security page; LOGIN_* env, nginx real-IP requirement, gotchas_

Built 2026-11-16 on `feature/login-protection` (branched from origin/main, separate from `feature/marketing`). User's ask: rate limit the login API, block IPs for a time that grows with more invalid attempts, hide the login page from blocked IPs (suggested `express-rate-limit`, now a dependency of apps/api).

**How it works:** `apps/api/src/modules/security/login-guard.js` — (1) `floodLimiter` (express-rate-limit, 30 sign-in requests/min/address), (2) escalating block stored in Mongo (`LoginGuardModel`, packages/db models/security.js, TTL cleanup): `LOGIN_MAX_FAILURES`=5 wrong tries inside `LOGIN_FAILURE_WINDOW_SECONDS`=900 → blocked for the next step of `LOGIN_BLOCK_STEPS_SECONDS`=300,900,3600,21600,86400 (stays on the last); climb forgotten after `LOGIN_STRIKE_FORGET_SECONDS`=86400 quiet. Two separate counters: "staff" (POST /api/auth/login, dashboards + platform console) and "customer" (POST /api/customer/otp/verify wrong codes). Successful logins deliberately do NOT reset the failure count (an attacker with one valid account could otherwise interleave). Paused-account 403 and "enter your name" errors don't count. `GET /api/auth/guard` and `GET /api/customer/guard` say whether the caller is blocked; the admin Login page and storefront CustomerGate ask first and show `BlockedNotice` (countdown) INSTEAD of the form; the 3rd wrong try answers 429 `details.code:"ip_blocked"` + `attemptsLeft` on earlier ones. Platform console → Security (`/platform/security`, `PlatformSecurity.jsx`) lists blocked/watched addresses with Unblock / Unblock everyone (`/api/platform/security/blocks`). `LOGIN_GUARD=off` disables everything.

**Address detection is the trap:** `callerIp()` reads X-Forwarded-For + the socket from the RIGHT and skips our own (loopback/private) hops, because customers reach the API through the storefront's /api pass-through and nginx — plain `req.ip` would make every customer one address. Needs nginx `proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;` and, behind Cloudflare, `real_ip_header CF-Connecting-IP` + `set_real_ip_from` (documented in `deploy/login-protection.md`, `deploy/wildcard-subdomains.md`). IPv6 grouped by /64.

**Tests:** `tests/e2e/tests/t15-login-protection.mjs` (Chrome only; 28 checks). The bench sets 3 failures / blocks of 6,12,24 s; the test fakes two visitors with an `x-forwarded-for` header added by a Playwright `context.route` ONLY for localhost hosts (a context-wide extra header breaks Google Fonts CORS); tabs share one cookie jar, so sign out before checking another tab's login form.

**Not built (could be asked):** per-ACCOUNT lockout (distributed attack on one email from many IPs), configuring the numbers from the console (they're env settings), email alert on repeated blocks.

### rate-limiting

_API-wide request limits (2026-11-19, branch feature/message-sending, commit 77f94a2 PUSHED + DEPLOYED to dev): one POLICIES table in middleware/rate-limit.js (33 limits), per-account sign-in guard, WebSocket limits, route-inventory tool + coverage test t28, docs/api-rate-limiting.md_

User asked (2026-11-19): "add rate limiting for all those api's which needs to be protected — message resend, login, register… scan all api's… at last create a doc which api's are protected and why." Built on `feature/message-sending`; **commit 77f94a2, pushed, and DEPLOYED to the dev server 2026-11-19** (user said "deploy it"). Full e2e suite t00–t28 green first (29 tests, 0 fails). Deployed FROM A CLEAN `git worktree` of the commit (the working tree already held untested account/delete work) — admin rebuilt, only tablepe-dev-api restarted, no env/deps change. Verified live through Cloudflare→nginx: `ratelimit` headers count per real visitor, `site-challenge` allowed 40 then 429 + Retry-After, storefront's own `/api` path counts the same visitor key, SSR/pages unaffected.

**Where:** `apps/api/src/middleware/rate-limit.js` — ONE `POLICIES` table (name → windowMs, limit (number or fn(req)), key `ip|user|identity|store|…`, `why`, optional `allows`/`appliesTo`/`managedBy`) + `limiter(name)` (cached express-rate-limit instance, adds `.policy`), `onlyWrites()`, `usesPolicy()` (marker for limits applied inside a handler: sign-in guard, code-resend waits), `allowUpgrade()/refuseUpgrade()` for WebSocket upgrades, `WRITE_EXEMPT` (only `POST /api/auth/logout`), `limitFor(name, req)`. Env: `RATE_LIMITS=on|off`, `RATE_LIMIT_SCALE` (read LIVE on every request — tests flip `env.RATE_LIMITS`/`env.RATE_LIMIT_SCALE` in-process; fixtures made with limits off).
**Rules:** `app.js` mounts `limiter("api")` (600/min visitor address, 1500/min signed-in user) on `/api` and `onlyWrites(limiter("team-write"))` (240/min/user) on `TEAM_ROUTES`; specific policies are inlined in route files BEFORE body parsers (`limiter("x")` right after the path). **Address-keyed limits skip our own network** (`isInternal(callerIp)`: storefront/website SSR over loopback have no X-Forwarded-For) — so in dev/bench address limits never fire unless a test sends `x-forwarded-for: <public ip>`; user/store/account-keyed limits always apply. If nginx stops passing XFF every visitor looks internal and address limits turn OFF (sign-in guard instead fails closed, one shared address).
**New protections:** per-ACCOUNT sign-in guard in `modules/security/login-guard.js` (`ACCOUNT_GUARD`: 10 wrong passwords/15 min from any address ⇒ account paused 1/5/15/60 min; counts unknown emails too; cleared on success; stored in `LoginGuardModel` scope `staff-account`, ip field = masked email "o•••@acme.test", shown on the platform Security page as "One account (any address)"); `otp-send-ip` 15/10 min, `otp-send-store` 300/h (keyed by body outletId), enquiry `site-lead`/`site-challenge` moved onto the central limiter (old `tooMany` in the challenge route removed; DB-backed lead caps in site/guard.js unchanged), `coupon-guess`, `table-scan`, `public-compute`, `order-place` (12/10 min customer, 150 staff), `payment`, `webhook` (240/min), `print-agent`, uploads/exports/bulk-send/chat-send/wa-link/hardware/destructive/secrets/pii-read/report/preview/outbound-fetch/account-write/coins-code; WebSocket `/realtime` + `/preview`: 30 connects/min/address, `maxPayload` 4 KB / 1 MB, preview editor messages capped 20/s.
**Tools/tests:** `node apps/api/src/scripts/route-inventory.js [--json|--markdown|--write-doc]` loads the real app, patches `Router.prototype.route/use` to record routers (Express 5 `router` pkg — patch the PROTOTYPE), walks mounts, lists every route + its policies (272 routes, 154 writes). `docs/api-rate-limiting.md` has hand-written explanation + a generated block between `generated:begin/end` markers — re-run `--write-doc` after changing policies. Test `t28-rate-limits-api` (suites/rate-limits.mjs, port 3089, scale 0.1, 124 checks): pushes every limit to its edge, 429 shape (`details.code "rate_limited"`, `policy`, `Retry-After`), isolation between addresses, spoofed XFF, internal skip, account guard, WebSockets, and COVERAGE (every write has a non-`api` policy or is exempt; every policy used; doc names every policy).
**Gotchas:** ws upgrade events bypass Express (limits there are manual); `team-write` also counts anonymous customers' POSTs by address; webhook limit is per gateway address (shared by all merchants — raise if the platform grows); counters are in-memory per process (fine with one pm2 process). Full e2e suite re-run needed after this (user-keyed limits can bite tests that hammer one login).
See [login-protection](#login-protection), [message-sending](#message-sending) (resend waits = `otp-resend-number`).

### cache-dashboard-store-delete

_2026-11-14/15 work (UNCOMMITTED; written on feature/store-subdomains, which PR #9 has since merged into `main`, so the working tree is now on `main` — branch before committing): API in-memory cache with DB-set lifetime + expire all/one store, platform dashboard with charts + sign-in log, delete-a-store; design decisions, measured query counts, caveats, and DB-call findings not acted on + (2026-11-15) a read-only cache VIEWER page_

Built after the user asked: (1) a way to delete a store from the console, (2) a platform dashboard (monthly/yearly revenue, progress/downfall, comparison graphs, login logs, store counts), (3) caching of store/theme/site config to cut DB calls, lifetime in the DB and configurable from the console, with expiry of ONE store or ALL (user added: "I should be able to expire cache for any specific shop or also expire all"). Deployed to the dev server (admin.mytimelyne.info) 2026-11-15; **not committed or pushed** — the user hasn't asked (last push was eba970d). See [dev-server-mytimelyne](#dev-server-mytimelyne), [store-subdomains](#store-subdomains).

**Cache design** — in-process `TtlCache` (`apps/api/src/lib/ttl-cache.js`): lifetime read on every access (changing it applies at once), single-flight loads, tags (`brand:<id>`, `menu:<outletId>`, `ns:<name>`, `neg:host`), LRU + byte cap (1500 entries / 48MB, separate 300-entry pool for "not found"), values deep-frozen, a value whose tag was expired DURING its load is never stored. App wiring in `modules/cache/`: `platform-config.js` (settings doc snapshot, re-read ≤ every 30s only while traffic flows), `cache.js` (singleton, `expireEverything`, `expireStore`), `invalidation.js` (listens to `onModelWrite`). Models announce their own writes (`packages/db/src/write-events.js`, plugin on Brand/Outlet/Plan/PlatformSettings/SitePage/SiteSettings/Menu*) so nobody has to remember to invalidate; `$setOnInsert` and bookkeeping fields (overLimitSince, cache stamps) are ignored. Setting = `PlatformSettings.cacheTtlSeconds` (default 300, 0 = off); manual expiry writes `cacheExpiredAt` / `cacheStoreExpiredAt.<brandId>` so OTHER API processes on the same DB pick it up within ~30s. Cached: address→store (`host:`), store bundle with effective config (`store:`), menu (`menu:`), outlet→brand (`outlet:`, used by ~9 public endpoints), plan (fixed 30s, ignores the setting), marketing-site reads (`site:`), dashboard (`dash:platform`), capacity counts (10s). NEVER cached: order pricing (`getMenu(ctx,{fresh:true})` in `quote()`), payments, customer/session reads. Console: Settings → Cache card (lifetime, stats, Expire everything, Expire one store) and a Cache tab on each store page.
**Caveat to remember:** local API and dev API share the real DB but have separate caches — an edit made through one can show on the other's storefront for up to the lifetime (≤5 min) unless expired; edits through the SAME API are instant.

**Measured (in-memory Mongo, query counting via `mongoose.set("debug")`):** warm restaurant visit (for-host + resolve + menu) 9–10 queries → 0; 100 visits 933 → 0; first visit after expiry ≈9; table-QR page 6 → 0; picker-store cold first request 16 → 6. 14 storefront endpoint responses are byte-identical before/after.

**Delete store** — `DELETE /api/platform/stores/:id` body `{confirmName}` (exact name) + `GET .../deletion-summary`; `modules/platform/store-delete.service.js`. Cascade = every model whose `brandId` is REQUIRED (tenant-scope plugin) → future collections covered automatically; deletes Outlets first (store goes dark at once), order counters, accounts that belonged only to the store (+sessions), then the Brand; people who also work elsewhere keep their account. LoginLog survives (its brandId is optional). Uploaded photos are NOT deleted (storage isn't tracked per store; they live under `<brandId>/`) — the dialog says so. Console: red "Delete store" button on the store page → typed-name dialog.

**Dashboard** — `GET /api/platform/dashboard[?fresh=1]`, cached; UI `PlatformDashboard.jsx` + hand-built SVG charts in `routes/platform/viz/` (no chart lib; dataviz skill rules: thin marks, legend, hover+keyboard tooltip, Table twin on every chart; palette validated). Two "revenue"s kept apart: SALES (orders not cancelled, `totals.grandTotal` paise, Indian calendar months, only stores that exist) and SUBSCRIPTIONS (ESTIMATE = plan `marketing.monthly` × stores; no billing exists). The month in progress is compared with the SAME DAYS of last month (never the whole month). Sign-in log: `LoginLog` model (TTL 180 days, failed attempts logged ≤30/hour/IP, never stores passwords), `GET /api/platform/login-logs`, section on the dashboard.

**DB-call findings NOT changed (user asked to be told; offer them):** (a) print dispatcher polls every 5s forever: `sweepStaleClaims` + `find` = ~34,500 queries/day per running API (local + dev + food all share the DB); (b) every signed-in request does 2 lookups (session, user) — a ~30s session cache would remove them but touches security; (c) restaurant root page = 3 API round trips (for-host → redirect → resolve → menu); (d) capacity poll every 20s per open order page (now served from cache unless limits are on). Real-DB side effects of the first dev start: new `loginlogs` collection + TTL index, `orders` index on createdAt.

Test recipe that worked: throwaway harness in the scratchpad (in-memory Mongo + the real `createApp()` on 3097, cwd = scratchpad so the real `.env` is never read, query counting), plus a throwaway second admin Vite on 3171 with its own `cacheDir`; both removed afterwards. 49+29+27+34+34+29 API checks and 42 browser checks passed.

Sibling work in the same uncommitted pile: the Server page, see [server-monitor](#server-monitor), and disabling a store instead of deleting it, see [store-disable](#store-disable) (the Delete dialog now offers "Disable instead").

**Cache viewer (added 2026-11-15, same uncommitted pile, deployed to dev)** — the user asked to "also view cache data from my dashboard" ("dashboard" = the console). Console page `/platform/cache` (sidebar "Cache"): summary tiles (items/size vs the 1,500-item/48 MB limits, working %, lifetime, last expired), filters (store / kind / search / sort), a table (item named in words, store, size, age, reloads in, uses), 50 at a time; click a row → dialog with facts, the data as a lazy collapsible tree or plain JSON, Copy, Download, Refresh, "Expire <store>'s cache", and technical details (key, tags). Also: a Cache strip on the dashboard, "View what's cached" in Settings → Cache, "View data" on every row of a store's Cache tab. API (super admin only): `GET /api/platform/cache/entries` and `GET /api/platform/cache/entry?key=…` (`modules/platform/cache-inspect.service.js`, `cache-present.js`).
- **Looking changes nothing:** `TtlCache#peek()` / `describe()` never count a hit, move the LRU order or reset age (tested: stats/order/uses/loadedAt identical after many reads). Entries now also count their own `hits`/`lastUsedAt`.
- **Safety:** secret-looking FIELD NAMES (password, secret, token, api key, credential, cookie, encrypted, signature, otp…) are replaced by "••• hidden" and counted; long lists/texts are trimmed in tiers so a response stays under ~1.5 MB (flagged `trimmed`). Real data has no such field names today (secrets live in separate credential collections).
- **Gotchas found while building:** the cache-test harness must be restarted before each browser run (a run expires/creates entries); the console's own refresh re-reads store plans, so a `plan:` entry reappears right after expiring a store (expected); a remembered "not found" outlives its 60 s until the 60 s sweeper runs — the dialog says so; header buttons go icon-only on phones (`.cv-label`).
- **Real settings observed 2026-11-15:** the REAL `cacheTtlSeconds` is **86,400 s (24 hours)**, not the 300 s default. Edits made through the same API process still show at once (it clears what changed), but an edit made through a DIFFERENT API process on the shared DB (e.g. local `localhost:3072` while testing on mytimelyne.info) can take up to a day to show on the other — press Expire (all or that store) after cross-process edits. Real data on dev: 1 store (Gazab Momos, 2 outlets), warm cache ≈ 27 KB.
- Tests (scratchpad, not in repo): `ttl-unit` 40, `cache-present-test` 27, `cache-view-test` 49, `cache-ui-test` 70 browser checks (desktop, 390 px phone, dark), plus all earlier suites still green.

### cache-audit-tracking

_2026-11-21 — order tracking page, friendly validation messages, opt-in auto-cancel, and the cache audit (auth/outlet/config/offers remembered; orders/tables/bookings live); store-by-store cache table; t67–t70; deployed to dev_

Commits b57ddbc / b492052 / 7ad2cd8, deployed to dev 2026-11-21 (API + admin + storefront).

**Order tracking:** `/<outlet>/order/<id>` (`OrderTracker.jsx`, polls `GET /api/customer/orders/:id` every 6 s while the order is open; rate policy `order-tracking` 40/min). "Order placed" screen has `done-follow`; Account rows have `order-follow`. API returns `history` (status+time only, never `by`), `cancelReason`, `late` ({kind accept|prep}) from the SLA limits. `GET /api/orders/:id` for a customer also strips `by`.
**Auto-cancel (opt-in):** `operations.sla.autoCancelMinutes` (0 = never, default). `sweepUnacceptedOrders()` every minute (server.js `startMissedOrderSweeper`); skips orders paid online, accepted, young, pre-orders (clock = pickup − 60 min). `updateStatus(..., { reason })` stores the reason in the history.
**Messages:** `describeValidation()` in middleware/error.js turns zod errors into "<Box> can be at most N." etc. (custom schema messages shown as written). Tables: case-insensitive duplicate check (`sameNumber`), seats 0/blank refused in the admin form (never silently 4).

**Cache audit (evidence tool: `node tests/e2e/audit/db-calls.mjs`; bench records DB ops per `x-audit` request on :3189):**
- Remembered (fixed `maxAgeMs`, so they work even if the platform cache is "off"): staff sign-in `auth:<hash>` (30 s), outlet access `outletaccess:<id>` (60 s), effective outlet config `cfg:<id>` (30 s, callers get a structuredClone), customer sign-in `cauth:<hash>` (30 s), public coupons `coupons:<outlet>` (60 s). All dropped at once by write events (User/Session → ns:auth; Customer → cauth:<id>; Coupon; Outlet/Brand/Plan → store family incl. ns:cfg/ns:outletaccess/ns:coupons). User, Session, Customer, CustomerSession, Coupon models got the `writeEvents` plugin.
- GOTCHA: a write made straight in MongoDB (raw driver / shell) bypasses write events → seen only after ≤30–60 s. Tests that raw-write users/sessions/customers/outlets/coupons must write through the model (t29 fixed this way).
- `completeProfile` now loads its own customer document (the signed-in copy is lean/read-only).
- Kept LIVE on purpose: orders list/status, kitchen, tables, sessions, bookings, reservations availability, print jobs, dashboard stats (15 reads/call, polled 60 s), riders, notify logs, pickup-slots' taken counts. Admin edit-form reads (outlets list, users, coupons, pages, printers, notify/*, cart-reminders) are single live reads of the data being edited.
- Console: `/platform/cache` has "Store by store" (view items / clear this store's cache); sign-in entries are hidden from the viewer. New kinds: cfg, outletaccess, coupons.
- Not done / ideas: dashboard stats could be remembered with Order-write invalidation; notify/messaging (6 reads) and cart-reminders (7) pages.

**Follow-ups the same day (deployed to dev, commits 6b31286, 2a4f774):**
- Storefront links no longer reload the app: `packages/ui/src/link.jsx` (`AppLink` + `LinkProvider`), storefront `RouterLinks.jsx` in the root layout hands Next's `Link` to header/footer/announcement/carousel links (footers.jsx became "use client"). t71 proves it with a window marker. Store page *content* links (PageBlocks, server-rendered) are still plain `<a>`.
- Menu drawn in chunks: `MenuSections` `chunkItems` (default 24, next 24) with an IntersectionObserver sentinel (`data-testid="menu-more"`); category jumps (`goTo`) raise the budget up to the target; scroll-spy "bottom = last category" only when everything is drawn; admin previews pass `chunkItems={Infinity}`. t72. NOTE: only the DRAWING is chunked — the whole menu is still in the page's data (real store: ~55 KB gzip). True network paging would need a paginated menu API and the cart/item-pop-up to cope with items not loaded yet.
- "Not added" notice (order page after Order again): bordered alert with icon, per-dish lines, dismiss, 24 px below; t73.
- 2026-11-21 real dev DB check: store "TablePe" (slug gazab-momos) now has ONLY the outlet `noida` (Ludhiana/Delhi were removed by the user) — /ludhiana is a 404 by data, not a regression.

## Storefront, menu and look

### storefront-banners-pages-orderbar

_2026-11-15 storefront batch — separate message bar (8 styles), 13 carousel styles, 15 headers, per-outlet store pages, Takeaway/Delivery switch + outlet dropdown with km, new order-placed screen, platform-wide image storage (owners can't see it); rules learned, real-data check, deployed to dev, UNCOMMITTED on main_

Done in one go on 2026-11-15 from the user's 9-point request (screenshot of the cramped "Order #0002 placed" screen). Working tree is on `main`, **UNCOMMITTED** (together with the other uncommitted features) — branch (e.g. `feature/storefront-banners-pages`) before committing; commit/push only when the user asks. Deployed to the dev server ([dev-server-mytimelyne](#dev-server-mytimelyne)) the same day.

**What exists now**
- **Message bar** is its own family (`announcementVariants` ANN01–08 in `packages/ui/src/registry.jsx`; chosen in `layout.announcementVariant`, content in per-outlet `config.announcement`). Not part of any header any more. Legacy `header.top-bar` is upgraded on read (`upgradeLegacy` in `config-schema/outlet-config.js`): becomes `header.left-logo` + a split bar carrying the old tagline/phone, **not closable** (the old bar had no ✕). Brand-level appearance wins over an outlet's own layout, so both real Gazab outlets show it.
- **Headers** 15 (H1–4, H6–16; H5 retired). **Carousels** 13 (CRS01–13), content in `config.carousel` (slides, autoplay, arrows/dots, height). Both configured on the dashboard **Storefront → Banners** page (live previews, style pickers, 5 messages / 10 slides max).
- **Store pages** (About us, Contact, FAQ, policies…): model `StorePage` per OUTLET, API `/api/pages`, dashboard **Storefront → Pages** (list, reorder, ready-made drafts, block editor with live preview), storefront route `/[outletSlug]/[pageSlug]`. 8 block types, safe links only (https/mailto/tel//path/#anchor, never javascript:), reserved addresses (order, t, api…), drafts are 404 to the public, page links go to header/footer via `Chrome.jsx`.
- **Order bar** (`apps/storefront/src/components/OrderBar.jsx`): Takeaway/Delivery switch + outlet dropdown (distance by haversine, "Delivers to you · ~N min · ₹fee" via the same `checkDelivery` as the server). Location only when the visitor presses "Use my location". Choice kept in `localStorage["onetap.orderType"]` and carried into checkout. Message-bar dismissal key: `onetap.announcement.dismissed` (signature of the messages, so an edited message shows again).
- **Order-placed screen**: `DoneView` in `OrderPage.jsx` (check, title, line, summary card, button; 28–32 px gaps; takeaway/delivery/dine-in incl. running tab).
- **Storage is platform-wide**: one `PlatformStorage` doc, console **Platform → Storage** only; store owners have no storage endpoints/permissions/UI. A store's old own doc keeps working until the platform doc is first saved; console button "Use for the whole platform" adopts it (secret stays encrypted). Saving or adopting a setup that lacks required fields is **refused** (an incomplete S3 would break every store's uploads).

**Rules found while testing (not obvious from the code)**
- **Delivery is always prepaid** (`orders.service.js` 409 "Delivery orders must be paid online"); checkout offers Delivery only if an online gateway is enabled. The menu-page switch/dropdown now follow the same rule (`offersDelivery` in `outlets/outlet-cache.js`); Settings → delivery info text says so. Real Gazab outlets have delivery off, so they show only Takeaway.
- Two outlets with the same display name (real Gazab: both "Gazab Momos") are listed by their outlet names ("Gazab Momos - Laxmi Nagar") in `for-host` (also fixes the old location picker).
- The item grid forced a 320 px column → 24 px sideways scroll on 320 px phones; now `minmax(min(Npx,100%),1fr)`. Owner route table now has a catch-all (old `/storage` bookmark no longer blank).
- Never `pkill -f next-server` — it also matches the user's own website dev server (the permission classifier blocked it). Kill by PID found via `lsof -iTCP:<port>`.

**Real-data check (read-only, on the dev server, 2026-11-15):** Laxmi Nagar shows the same tagline + phone bar as before; Preet Vihar (no tagline/phone/address) gets the default line "Fresh, hot and made to order" in its bar (owner may want to edit in Banners); images still resolve to Gazab's own S3 via the legacy fallback; platform storage unsaved (`inUse: store`), 0 store pages. Pre-existing outlet facts: both outlets share hosts `gazab-momos.mytimelyne.info`.

**Verification (all in-memory sandbox, scripts in the session scratchpad — ephemeral):** storefront browser suite 98, dashboard browser suite 67, storage API 51, pages API 73, plus smoke 29 / store-disable 94 + UI 76 / cache 49+49 + UI 70 / dashboard 34 / delete 27 / login-log 34 / sysmon 40+40+38 / server UI 60, storefront `next build` + prod run. Older suites that need a pre-seeded website sandbox (site-api 4 checks, sub-api, guard-api, notfound) were not re-run.

**Noticed, not changed:** opening hours are not enforced anywhere (so the switcher doesn't show open/closed); `useCapacity` (busy kitchen / riders) is documented for the menu page but only the order page uses it.

Related: [store-disable](#store-disable), [customer-auth-checkout](#customer-auth-checkout), [local-dev-servers](#local-dev-servers).

### storefront-category-bar

_The storefront's sticky category bar (packages/ui MenuSections.jsx) — one row that scrolls sideways instead of wrapping, how it works, test t35, and the thumbnails idea that is still not built_

User (2026-11-19, screenshot with 33 categories wrapping into 7 rows): "too many categories in the storefront UI… in mobile it doesn't even fit the screen… should take very little height like a normal row."

**Cause:** `.ot-fx-bar` toolbar is `position: sticky` and its chips used `flex-wrap: wrap`; ≥1024 px shows every section as a chip, <1024 px shows a "Categories" button + sheet, but the filter row could still wrap.
**Now (commit 2a3af50, deployed to dev):** `ChipStrip` component in `packages/ui/src/MenuSections.jsx` — chips in ONE `overflow-x:auto` row (no scrollbar, soft fade via CSS vars `--fade-l/--fade-r`, `‹ ›` arrow buttons for mouse users hidden on touch via `@media (hover:none)`), the toolbar is no longer wrapping (filter `flex:0 1 auto` scrolls, strip `flex:1 1 0`, Categories button `margin-left:auto`), scroll-spy now runs for flat menus too (`aria-current` chip highlighted and scrolled into view; at the very bottom the LAST chip is highlighted), nested sub-category row is a ChipStrip too. CSS in `packages/ui/src/tokens.css` (`.ot-chip-row`, `.ot-chip-strip`, `.ot-strip-arrow`). Desktop bar ≈ 52 px (was ~450 px with 33 categories); phone bar 59 px. Only admin + storefront use packages/ui (website doesn't) — rebuild those two.
**Tests:** t35 `storefront-category-bar` (suites/storefront-category-bar.mjs, Playwright against bench :3170, adds 30 categories via the bench API and removes them in `finally`; 34 checks incl. phone sheet) + the older t06 (still 28/28). Bench quirk: a hand-made 289 MP test image needs `limitInputPixels:false` in sharp's create (see [image-compression](#image-compression)).
**Not built:** menu-card thumbnails/variants (cards show 72–104 px pictures but load the full ≤400 KB file).

**Follow-up, same day (commits 532273e + c6c2b55, deployed to dev):** user asked for (1) less page side padding on phones AND tablets, desktop unchanged, and (2) on phones only a path title ("South Indian › Idli & Vada" — comes from CUSTOM menu-layout sections whose title is the category path) on two lines (parent tiny + muted, name ~0.86em) with a smaller View all button. Done in `packages/ui`: class `.ot-page-px` on every page-width container (menu, hero, MenuList, PageBlocks, footers, offers section, OrderBar, table banner/header, account, [pageSlug] title) → 14px at ≤1023px (desktop keeps its own 18/24px; `.ot-header-px` also 14px ≤1023px so the header lines up); `.ot-sec-crumb/.ot-sec-name` spans + `.ot-viewall-title/.ot-viewall` classes, phone rules at ≤640px in tokens.css. The admin live preview keeps its own `.ot-preview-shell .ot-menu-sections` padding (more specific). **Lesson:** my first phone-bar test used the bench's 3 filter chips; the real store has 4 (All/Veg/Egg/Non-veg) and the Categories button wrapped onto a 2nd row (103px) — found only by measuring the LIVE site. Fix: `.ot-chip-row` basis 0 on ≤1023px + compact chips/button on phones; t37 (suites/storefront-mobile-layout.mjs, 43 checks) now creates an egg dish so all four chips exist. Real store after: phone bar 55px, tablet 59, desktop 53. Always measure a real-data page after deploying a UI change, not just the bench.

**View all + animation (2026-11-19, UNCOMMITTED, not deployed):** user asked (1) "View All" as configured on a wide screen but ALWAYS below the cards, centred, on mobile and tablet **including landscape**; (2) expand/collapse animated. Done in `packages/ui/src/MenuSections.jsx` + `tokens.css`: small-screen rule = `@media (max-width: 1023px), (hover: none) and (pointer: coarse) and (max-width: 1400px)` [SUPERSEDED 2026-11-20: now `max-width: 719.98px` only — see [storefront-pinned-bars](#storefront-pinned-bars)] (touch tablets in landscape up to ~1400px count; a mouse laptop does not); the title-right button gets `ot-viewall-lg-only` (hidden on small screens) and a bottom container `ot-viewall-sm-only` (hidden on big) is always rendered; left/right bottom containers `ot-viewall-row` are centred by CSS `!important`. Animation = `CollapsibleCards`: first `initialItems` cards always; extras in a `display:contents` + `inert` group mounted only while open/closing (SSR unchanged); a clipping box's height is animated with WAAPI (420 ms), reverses cleanly mid-slide, reduced-motion = instant, `keepSectionInView` scrolls a far-away heading back after closing. Tests: t41 `storefront-view-all` (64 checks), t37 updated (45), t40 (React dev warnings) still passes. Lesson: tests that read DOM state right after `.click()` in the same JS task miss React's re-render — wait a frame.

**Order-type switch always shows both (2026-11-19, UNCOMMITTED until "deploy"):** user: even when an outlet only has takeaway, show BOTH Takeaway and Delivery on the menu page's order bar, with the unavailable one DISABLED. `apps/storefront/src/components/OrderBar.jsx`: `SWITCH_TYPES = ["takeaway","delivery"]` always rendered; the one not in `offeredOrderTypes(here.ordering)` gets `disabled`, `title="X isn't available from this outlet"`, faded `segOff` style, and a line under the switch (`data-testid="order-type-hint"`); the bar is no longer hidden for a one-outlet/one-choice store (`offered.length < 1` now); with neither takeaway nor delivery there is still no switch. Remembered choice (`onetap.orderType` in localStorage) for a disabled type is ignored (`startingOrderType` falls back). **Gotcha:** the public storefront counts Delivery as ON only if the outlet ALSO has a non-COD payment method (`offersDelivery` in api `outlets/outlet-cache.js`: delivery is prepaid), so "delivery enabled in Settings" can still show disabled until an online payment method is on. Tests: t42 `storefront-order-type` (20 checks, sets the outlet via the API and restores it), t10 changed (after a downgrade Delivery is still listed but disabled).

**Checkout page order type + coupon box (2026-11-20, commit 1d77188, deployed to dev):** (1) user screenshot of checkout ("Step 4 of 4 · Review & pay") asked "where is the disabled delivery option?" — the always-both rule had only been done on the menu page. `OrderPage.jsx` "How would you like it?" now ALWAYS shows Takeaway + Delivery for non-dine-in orders (dine-in via table QR still shows no picker); `ChoiceBtn` takes `disabledReason`/`testId` (`fulfilment-takeaway|delivery|hint`); reasons: "Delivery isn't available from this outlet." (outlet off, or no online payment — delivery is prepaid) / "Delivery is paused right now." (load management stop) / "Takeaway isn't available from this outlet."; new `takeawayEnabled` prop (order/page.jsx) → a delivery-only outlet starts on Delivery. t42 extended (39 checks); t08 load-stop check changed from "Delivery not visible" to "listed but disabled". (2) user bug: coupons locked for a store but checkout still showed the coupon box. Cause: "Coupons" is a PLAN feature (platform console store Features lock) — dashboard nav and the API already honoured it, the storefront never knew. Fix: `features.coupons` (schema default true, never set by owners) in `packages/config-schema/src/features.js`; `applyEntitlements` sets it to `has("coupons")`; storefront order pages pass `couponsEnabled={features.coupons}` (both `[outletSlug]/order` and `t/[tableId]/order`); OrderPage hides the Coupon section. Test t43 (`suites/checkout-coupons.mjs`). Bench gotcha: the bench API keeps running between runs, so a change in packages/config-schema needs an API restart (`import("./bench/bench.mjs").reset()` = fresh fake data) — `--rebuild` only rebuilds dashboard+storefront.

### storefront-pinned-bars

_2026-11-20 storefront batch — header + order bar + category bar pinned to the top (StickyBar), View all breakpoint now 720px, "Order takeaway" hero button removed, card C02 re-laid-out, one-line card descriptions, redesigned item pop-up; rules learned; tests t47/t48/t49_

User requests (2026-11-20, screenshots of the order bar + category bar): pin those sections to the top after scroll on all devices; View all "as configured" from 720px up, under the cards below 720px; remove the "Order takeaway" hero button; card C02: price after the veg/non-veg mark and title+description full width; any card description max ONE line + ellipsis (user said 2, then changed to 1); item pop-up "more attractive, image starts exactly at the modal edge — add padding". "Make the components variations like this" was ambiguous — I read it as "must work with every header style/effect".

**How it works**
- `packages/ui/src/StickyBar.jsx` (client): wraps a bar in `position: sticky`, publishes its height as `--ot-<name>-h` on `<html>`; `below="header"` stacks it under another. Header pinned in `StoreTop` (`apps/storefront/src/components/Chrome.jsx`, z 32); order bar controls pinned in `OrderBar.jsx` (z 31); the category toolbar in `MenuSections.jsx` is `top: calc(var(--ot-header-h,0px) + var(--ot-bar-h,0px))`. Scroll helpers (`glideTo`, `keepSectionInView`, `#menu` scroll-margin) use `pinnedReach()` so headings never end up hidden under the bars.
- **Too tall to pin = not pinned:** header taller than min(150px, 20% of window height) or order bar taller than min(100px, 14%) scrolls away and publishes height 0 (so the bars below pin to the very top). The Banner (≈237px) and Stacked (≈195px) header styles are too tall; the other 13 pin. Landscape phones: header/bar don't pin, the category bar still does.
- The order bar's NOTES (the "Delivery isn't available" hint, the delivery-check status) are a separate block under the pinned controls and scroll away; on phones (≤640px) switch + outlet share one row (`.ot-ob-*` classes in tokens.css; pill basis 120px — 150px wrapped).
- The mobile nav drawer is rendered with `createPortal(…, document.body)` in `headers.jsx` — inside the pinned header it would be trapped under the cart bar / pop-ups (sticky = its own stacking layer).
- View all: `@media (max-width: 719.98px)` in tokens.css (was max-width:1023px + touch rule). Width decides, not the device.
- C02 `RowThumbLeft` and `descStyle` (now `clampOneLine`: `-webkit-box`, line-clamp 1) in `packages/ui/src/cards.jsx` / `primitives.jsx` — every card style; C08/C09 show no description. Dish pop-up still shows the whole description.
- Item pop-up (`Customiser` in `apps/storefront/src/components/Ordering.jsx`): picture inset in 18px padding, 16px radius, round close button on it, FoodMark + name + "from ₹X", sections with Required/Optional pills, option TILES (native inputs kept, highlighted when chosen, "Free" for ₹0), footer (stepper + Add + Cancel) outside the scrolling body.

**Gotchas found**
- Mixing `border` shorthand with a `borderColor` override makes React clear the colour (border turns black when un-selected) — use longhands (`borderWidth/Style/Color`).
- The menu layout's own `menuLayout.defaultCardVariant` wins over `layout.itemCardVariant` — tests must set both.
- Fractional pinned offsets break tests that round tops (`Math.round(t/6)`); compare centre lines. Category/dish lists in the bench are alphabetical (find the dish by name, not `[0]`).
- Header at the top of the page sits BELOW the (non-pinned) message bar, so "header top = 0" only holds once scrolled.
- Tests: t47 (101 checks, all 15 header styles), t48 (69, hero + C02 + every card style), t49 (42, pop-up); t37/t41/t35 updated for the new geometry/breakpoint.

Related: [storefront-category-bar](#storefront-category-bar), [storefront-banners-pages-orderbar](#storefront-banners-pages-orderbar).

### storefront-title

_2026-11-25 — storefront tab title = the store's name (home, outlet picker, table QR page), per-request cached outlet lookups, t78; DEPLOYED to production 2026-11-25 (user said "yes, deploy"); also the concurrent rename sessions + branch gotchas_

User: "the storefront title is not same as the store name … titlebar shows TablePe always." Cause: `apps/storefront/src/app/layout.jsx` has ONE fixed `metadata.title`; only account/book/order/store-pages set their own. Fix (main commits 9b311a2 + e867dae, pushed; DEPLOYED to prod 2026-11-25 ~04:05 UTC after the user's yes — [feedback-ask-before-prod-deploy](#feedback-ask-before-prod-deploy)): `generateMetadata` on `[outletSlug]/page.jsx` (title = `storeName(outlet)`, description = tagline), on `app/page.jsx` (the multi-outlet picker: `resolved.brand.name`), on `t/[tableId]/page.jsx` ("Table T1 — Store"; wrong code → "This code didn't work"); `lib/outlet.js` now wraps `getOutletBySlug / getOutletsForHost / getOutletById` in React `cache` (one lookup per request, shared by title + page; the table scan endpoint is rate limited so `resolveScan` is cached too). Test t78 (16 checks, renames the bench store and restores it). Related tests re-run and green: t19, t29, t64, t71, t72.

**Concurrent sessions gotcha (found 2026-11-25):** while I worked, ANOTHER actor (the user or a parallel Claude session) switched the shared working folder `main` → new branch `feature/rename-to-rasoi-pulse` and committed "rename TablePe/OneTap to Rasoi Pulse" (earlier, 2026-11-23: `feature/rename-to-thalios`, `@thalios/*`; the rename branch also changes the outlet header `x-onetap-outlet` → `x-thalios-outlet`, package scope `@onetap/*` → `@rasoipulse/*`). My two commits therefore landed on THAT branch (addc249, 1c7aedd) and were cherry-picked onto `main` from a separate worktree (`$SCRATCH/main-wt`, `git worktree add <path> main`). They still sit on the rename branch too (harmless; identical changes). Rules from this: (1) ALWAYS run `git branch --show-current` right before committing; (2) when the folder is not on `main`, do main work in a separate worktree, don't switch the user's checkout; (3) the bench API process may be from another branch's code — after any branch switch restart it (`bench.reset()`) or tests fail with "No outlet selected"; (4) generated files `tests/e2e/results/*` and `docs/CATALOG.md` conflict on every cherry-pick — keep main's history.jsonl + append the new lines, take the commit's latest.json/CATALOG.


**Deploy 2026-11-25 (prod, rasoipulse.in):** `main` e867dae via `$SCRATCH/deploy-wt`; rsync sent only the 4 storefront files, no dependency change so no `pnpm install`; built storefront only, `pm2 restart tablepe-prod-storefront` only (API/website untouched, API health ok, db connected). Verified live: https://rp-demo.rasoipulse.in/noida → `<title>Rasoi Pulse</title>` = the H1; `/noida/account` → "Your account — Rasoi Pulse". Note: when checking a store from outside, FIRST read the store's current hostnames from the DB — they had changed between my two checks (see [production-rasoipulse](#production-rasoipulse)).

### storefront-card-columns-nav-progress

_2026-11-25 — manual "N cards a row" is now a maximum that adapts to width (card --card-min at 97% of variant.minWidth), the aspect-ratio min-width overflow trap, and the storefront page-change progress bar (NavProgress); tests t81/t82; superseded by the ELASTIC rule below (main b605426 pushed AND DEPLOYED to production 2026-11-26 on the user's "deploy to production now")_

User (with a tablet screenshot): "cards are not adjusting their size on tablet; if I set 3 a row manually but the device is too narrow, show 2" + "add a progress bar at the top while changing the page" (asked while the rename deploy was running, then "fix and deploy").

**Card grid (packages/ui `tokens.css` + `registry.jsx gridPropsFor`):**
- Real cause of the overflow: `card.image-overlay` has `minHeight: 190` + `aspect-ratio` 16:9 → browsers turn the min-height into a MIN-WIDTH of ~338px (190×16/9) for the grid item, so 3 columns at ~317px overflowed past the right edge and overlapped. Fix = `.ot-grid > *, .ot-grid-auto > * { min-width: 0 }` (auto-fill grids got `className: "ot-grid-auto"` too).
- Explicit columns (`section.columns` or `variant.defaultColumns`) now use `repeat(auto-fill, minmax(min(100%, max(var(--card-min), (100% - (N-1)*gap)/N - 0.5px)), 1fr))`: N is a MAXIMUM; a row holds fewer (3→2→1) when cards would drop below `--card-min`. `--card-min` = **97% of `variant.minWidth`** (list variants without minWidth fall back to 280). Why 97%: laptop menu area is 1032px (maxWidth 1080 − 2×24 padding) and 3×340 (thumb-left C02, default 3 columns!) + gaps = 1044 → exactly 97% keeps 3 on a laptop (3×330+24); a first try at the full minWidth made t40 (react-style-warnings, default menu uses C02 ×3) fail with 7 sections instead of 14+ because it fell to 2 columns. Portrait tablets (≈700–780px) still get 2 for overlay/image-top.
- Kept: ≤720px (phone) → 1 column; `@container (max-width: 680px)` → 1 column in admin preview shells; the old "721–960px → at most 2 columns" cap is KEPT (implemented as `--cols: 2` in a media query and a `@container (681–960)` query; without it an 820px tablet got 3 overlay cards of ~256px), above 960px the width/min rule decides. `1` stays 1.
- Test t81 (suite `storefront-card-columns.mjs`): ten card styles ×3 per row (+2/4/1) at 390/744/820/1024/1280px; checks column count = formula, no card outside/overlapping, cards share the row, no sideways page scroll; 19 of its 163 checks fail without the fix (incl. the overlay overflow at 1024).

**Progress bar (apps/storefront `components/NavProgress.jsx`, mounted in `app/layout.jsx`, CSS at the end of `globals.css`):** 3px fixed line at the very top (z-index 2147483000, pointer-events none), `data-testid="nav-progress"` with `data-state` idle|running|done. Starts on a click of an internal link to a different path/search (not same page, #hash, other origin, target=_blank, download, ctrl/meta/shift/middle click, `data-no-progress`), on `popstate`, and via exported `startNavProgress()` (called before `router.push` in OrderBar.goTo, AccountView "order again", OutletPicker.choose). Creeps to a 92% ceiling, finishes (slides to 100%, fades) when `usePathname()` changes, gives up after 8 s. NOT done for the admin console (single bundle, no lazy routes → instant page changes) or the marketing website. ### theme-effects-contrast

_2026-11-26 — storefront style effects (glass/neumorph/clay/aurora/cyberpunk/dark-premium) make active chips unreadable when `data-active` is missing (order-type switch, desktop category chips); the pixel-based contrast scan t83 (suites/effects-contrast.mjs); DONE + pushed as main 27493f6, NOT deployed (SSH blocked)_

User (5 screenshots of the Takeaway/Delivery switch under different effects): only "None" light/dark is right; in Glass/Neumorph/Clay/Aurora… the SELECTED tab's label is white on a light panel → "fix for light and dark, and scan the whole app for the same kind of issue with all the themes".

**Cause:** effects (`packages/ui/src/effects/*.js`) restyle the `chip` role through `--fx-chip-*` variables on `.ot-fx-chip` and give an ACTIVE chip its own look only under `.ot-fx-chip[data-active="true"]` (tinted bg + `--fx-chip-color`). `fx("chip", {...})` rewrites background/color to `var(--fx-chip-bg, original)`. Elements whose "active" state is only an inline style (primary bg + on-primary text) and that do NOT carry `data-active` get the effect's translucent/surface background but keep the inline white text. Known offenders: `apps/storefront/src/components/OrderBar.jsx` order-type switch (`aria-pressed` only) and `packages/ui/src/MenuSections.jsx` desktop category jump links (`aria-current` only). OK already: FilterChip, BookingView, OrderPage (choice buttons/day/slot/pay), CustomerGate (active style spread outside fx → readable). Rule: every active chip needs `data-active`.

**Scan (t83, suite `tests/e2e/suites/effects-contrast.mjs`, written, UNCOMMITTED, first run started 2026-11-26):** per effect (with its suggested palette from `EFFECT_PRESETS`, brand #B5338A) × light/dark × laptop 1280/phone 390 × states (home whole page, outlet list, phone menu, categories sheet, item popup, cart bar, checkout, account, store page, table page): lists text + SVG icons with real colour, hides all text/icons (`color: transparent !important`, svg hidden), screenshots, reads pixels behind each element in a canvas, contrast = 25th percentile over sampled pixels; compared with the same element under "None": problem if <3:1 where None isn't, or <4.5 where None ≥4.5 and ≥1 worse. `AUDIT_REPORT=1` lists everything <3:1. Runner script `$SCRATCH/run-t83.sh` (resets bench, `--rebuild`, runs t83); output `$SCRATCH/run-t83.out` / task output file.
**Next:** read the scan's findings, fix ALL (add `data-active` where missing; check item-popup options, steppers, selected variants, cart bar, forced-dark effects), re-run until clean, run related tests (t35 t37 t40 t41 t47 t48 t49 t72 t78 t80 t81 t82), commit on main, then ASK before deploying ([feedback-ask-before-prod-deploy](#feedback-ask-before-prod-deploy)).

**RESULT 2026-11-26 (pushed main `27493f6`, NOT DEPLOYED — SSH to the VPS was blocked, my IP <my-public-IP>):** scan went 20 failing combos → 0 after: `data-active` on the order switch + desktop category chips; new theme var `--color-primary-text` (packages/config-schema `readablePrimary`, target 4.8:1 vs bg, surface, and on a dark page also a 10%-lifted surface; used in 43 places via `var(--color-primary-text, var(--color-primary))`, admin falls back); effect palettes' `colorTextMuted` lifted (5.8:1+ even on 14% brand tints) and `DARK_FALLBACK` + tokens.css dark muted → #b4afa4; Clay light muted #524C62; Glass glow lighter, Glass button 2nd stop 90%, Glass/Aurora modal 95/94% opaque; toast × opacity .9. **Real bug found by the scan:** `themeCss(..., {forceDark:true})` (Cyberpunk, Dark Premium) emitted a bare `:root{}` which tokens.css's `@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark){:root:not([data-theme="light"])}` beats on dark devices → the store's palette was replaced by platform defaults; now the selector list names the same selectors. **Toolbar:** Glass/Aurora/Cyberpunk/Dark Premium now set `--fx-toolbar-edge/-radius/-pad-x` (14px) like Clay/Neumorph (t85, 105 checks). Scan quirks learnt: icons must be measured from their painted SHAPES (the <svg> root has default black fill); veg/egg/non-veg marks are exempt (standard symbol); skip elements inside the chip-strip's 22px mask fade; element at several scroll steps counts at its BEST view (sticky-bar shadow passes over it); two brand colours (magenta white-on-button, yellow dark-on-button) — a muted grey that passes on the plain palette can fall to ~4:1 on a brand-tinted panel. Env filters: AUDIT_EFFECTS / AUDIT_MODES / AUDIT_VIEWPORTS / AUDIT_PALETTES.

### menu-categories-tree

_Nested menu categories (any depth, cap 6), category pictures, tree with connecting lines + drag-and-drop, add-several-at-once boxes (each with its own picture), bulk-select delete for categories and items (2026-11-19, branch feature/message-sending, UNCOMMITTED): data model, API, storefront rules, dashboard editor, tests t32+t33_

User asked (2026-11-19): categories inside categories (South Indian → Dosa → Masala Dosa as a dish); answered "multiple depth" and "yes" (a parent may also hold dishes); "build now"; also a **picture for a category**, **multi-select delete for categories** and **the same for items**. Later: a **tree with connecting lines and nodes + drag-and-drop** (move AND re-parent); a **wider category panel**; and **adding several categories at once** — first built as comma-separated text, then the user changed it: "instead of separated by comma, should be able to add input boxes and when added the image button should also be added for that category". So: separate boxes, each with its own name + picture button. The comma splitter was deleted on purpose — don't bring it back.

**Data:** `MenuCategory` gets `parentId` (null = top level) and `image {url,key,width,height}`. Shared helpers in `packages/config-schema/src/menu.js`: `MAX_CATEGORY_DEPTH = 6`, `MAX_CATEGORIES_AT_ONCE = 50`, `cleanCategoryName`, `duplicateCategoryName` (first spelling of a repeat, ignoring capitals/spaces), `flattenCategories` (depth-first, with depth/trail), `categorySubtreeIds`, `categoryLevel`, `categoryHeight`, `categoryPathLabel` ("South Indian › Dosa"), `activeCategoryIds` (shown only if it AND every ancestor is active), `planCategoryMove` (ONE rule shared by server and editor so the drop indicator never promises something the server refuses). A parent that no longer exists = top level.
**API (`modules/menu`):** create/update accept `parentId` + `image` (null removes); refuses a missing parent, a move into itself/own branch, depth > 6; `POST /categories/order {ids}` reorders ONE level; `POST /categories/move {id,parentId,beforeId}` (drag-and-drop; renumbers both levels, per-doc updateOne because bulkWrite skips the cache hooks); **`POST /categories/bulk {parentId?, categories:[{name,image?}], isActive?}`** → 201 `{categories}` — everything validated BEFORE the first is created (empty name, same name twice, >50, bad parent, too deep → 400, nothing made); `POST /categories/bulk-delete {ids, deleteItems?}` deletes whole branches — 409 `category_has_items` unless `deleteItems`; `DELETE /categories/:id` same service; `POST /items/bulk-delete {ids}` all-or-nothing; bulk deletes limited by `destructive`. Picture files removed only when nothing else still points at the key (`removeImageIfUnused`). **Import** matches categories by place in the tree and copies pictures.
**Storefront rules (`resolveMenuSections` in homepage.js, `MenuSections.jsx` in packages/ui):** auto mode = sections in tree order with `depth`, `trail`, `rootId`, `imageUrl`, `headingOnly`; empty/switched-off branches dropped; `pruneEmptySections` for the food filter. Custom sections: `includeSubcategories`. UI: h2/h3/h4 by depth, picture beside the heading, top-level chips + second row for what is inside, indented sheet on phones.
**Dashboard:** `routes/menu/CategoriesPanel.jsx` — tree with connecting lines (`data-line` through/elbow-v/elbow-h/stem, `cat-elbow`, `cat-node` = fold button on parents / dot on leaves), native HTML5 drag-and-drop (grip `title="Drag to move"`; top/bottom 27% of a row = before/after, middle = inside, strip `drop-top-level` = top level; `drop-line` indicator), ⋯ menu (Add sub-category / Edit… / Move up / Move down / Delete…), "Select all", bulk bar `cat-bulk`. **Add window = `AddCategoriesDialog`** (title "Add category"; `new-cat-rows` → `new-cat-row` boxes, each `NewCategoryRow` with its own `useImageUpload`, picture button + file input + remove ×; `add-another` button; Enter in a named last box makes the next; empty boxes ignored; dup name / picture-without-name flagged and block Add; removing a box or Cancel deletes unsaved uploads; button "Add N categories"); opened from a row's "Add sub-category" or the "Add several categories…" link (`add-several`) under the quick "New category" box (which adds exactly what is typed). **Edit window = `CategoryDialog`** (edit only: name, Inside picker with disabled self/descendants/too-deep, one picture, Show on the menu). Left column of `MenuEditor.jsx` is `minmax(340px, 420px) minmax(0, 1fr)`. `ItemsPanel.jsx` (row ticks, `item-bulk`, "Also show dishes from the categories inside", `DeleteItemsDialog`); item form has a **Category** picker (tree). Test ids: `cat-row` (+`data-category`), `item-row` (+`data-item`).
**Tests:** t32 `menu-tree-api` (suites/menu-tree.mjs, port 3087; sections 6b move, 6c bulk create), t33 `menu-tree` (Chrome; builds its own PNG; last step = several boxes with pictures), plus t28 coverage (doc regenerated: 280 routes). Bench quirk: `bench/watch.mjs` auto-accepts native `confirm()` dialogs — never add a second `page.once("dialog")` handler (crashed a run with "dialog already handled").
**Not done / ideas:** bulk "move items to another category" and "hide/show" for ticked items; category description.
STATE: UNCOMMITTED/UNDEPLOYED with the account/delete-order/store-name work ([account-orders-and-delete](#account-orders-and-delete)); rate limiting already deployed ([rate-limiting](#rate-limiting)). Past bug to avoid: [feedback-undefined-names-check](#feedback-undefined-names-check).
