User: "Sold out items should show at last … 15 items, one sold out → first 14 then the sold-out 15th" and "after a filter leaves 3 items, View all still shows." Fix on main ce6c45e (not deployed, ask first — [feedback-ask-before-prod-deploy](#feedback-ask-before-prod-deploy)).
- `packages/config-schema/src/homepage.js`: exported `soldOutLast(items)` = stable split (`isAvailable === false` last); applied in `autoSections` (`itemsOf`) and in `resolveMenuSections` AFTER `sortBy` and BEFORE `maxItems` (a capped list fills with orderable dishes first). `isAvailable` comes from `menuForNow()` (menu.js) so it covers sold out, stock 0 and out-of-hours dishes alike (all go last — I treated "not orderable right now" as sold out; tell the user). ### menu-seed-ludhiana

_2026-11-19 seeding of 345 menu items into the real Gazab Momos / Ludhiana outlet from ~/Downloads/restaurant_menu_seed.json — category mapping decisions, conventions used, the undo log, what was NOT done (Delhi/Noida)_

User pasted `~/Downloads/restaurant_menu_seed.json` (58 categories, 376 items, prices in INR, foodType incl. "drink") and asked to seed the items into the DB "without overwriting what I already added", mapping them to the sub-categories they had created.

**Where:** only **Gazab Momos / Ludhiana** (outlet `6afde050a4133a81a544e30a`, brand `6afde050a4133a81a544e307`) has categories (36) and items; **Delhi (…e325) and Noida (…e32e) are empty with no categories** — not seeded. They can copy Ludhiana's menu with the console's "Import from outlet".

**Result:** 345 created + 31 already there skipped = 376. Run through the app's own `createItem` from a throwaway server-side script (deleted after). Verified live: public menu API 376 items / 36 categories, spot-checked prices, the user's 31 originals untouched (prices differ from the JSON on purpose, e.g. their Plain Dosa ₹110 vs JSON ₹90; all 31 still have their photos). Second dry run = 0 to create (idempotent). API restarted (cache is in memory).

**Undo:** `/root/tablepe-backups/menu-seed-2026-11-19T11-08-37-474Z.json` on the VPS lists every created item id (deleting exactly those reverts it; the seeding script supported `--undo <log>` but was removed from the server — recreate a `deleteMany({_id:{$in:ids}})` if asked).

**Conventions chosen (flag if the user disagrees):** prices ×100 (the app stores paise); a single "Regular" price → `basePrice` with NO sizes (matches how the user entered their own items); several sizes → `variants` (labels as given: "2 Pcs", "Small"…) with basePrice 0 (like their "Paneer tikka"); the app only knows veg/non-veg/egg so the 55 `drink` items were saved as **veg**; GST left at the default 5%; no photos/descriptions; sortOrder appended after existing items in each category; NO categories were created.

**Category mapping (their tree ≠ the JSON's):** JSON sub-categories the user's tree lacks went into the user's parent category: Thalis (3 groups → "Thali Specials" 21), Pizza 14, Burgers & Sandwiches 24, Pasta 11, Momos 13, Biryani & Rice 14, Snacks & Fast Food 22, Rolls & Wraps 14, Desserts & Sweets 17, Combos 9, "South Indian Specials" 7 → directly under "South Indian". Renamed ones mapped by meaning (Veg Starters→North Indian › Starters, Paneer/Vegetable Main Course → "Main Course - Paneer/Vegetables", Chinese › Veg Starters→"Chinese Cuisine › Veg Starter", Chinese Gravy→"Gravy", Indian Non-Veg → "Non-Veg Indian Main Course › Chicken/Mutton/Egg", Cold Drinks & Water→Cold Drinks, Shakes & Cold Coffee→Shakes, Mocktails & Iced Tea→Mocktails). Existing-item matching was by normalised name within the same target category + edit distance ≤2 ("Maysore Masala Dosa" = "Mysore Masala Dosa"; "Paneer tikka" = "Paneer Tikka").

**Known quirk:** 9 names exist in two categories because the JSON lists them twice (Veg Manchurian Dry, Crispy Corn, Fried/Kurkure Veg Momos, Paneer Momos, Fried/Kurkure Chicken Momos, Veg Pulao, Chicken Wings) — both added as the file says; the user may want to delete one of each.

### item-image-shapes

_2026-11-21 — dish pictures in four shapes (square default, landscape 4:3, wide 16:9, portrait 3:4): how it's stored, which card uses which, fallbacks, limits, files, tests t58/t59; UNCOMMITTED_

User asked (2026-11-21): when uploading a menu item image, allow landscape 4:3, 16:9, square 1:1 and portrait 3:4; card variants use the one that fits; if none for a ratio → fall back to SQUARE; existing uploaded images are all square and must NOT be removed.

**How it works**
- `item.images[]` entries now carry `ratio` ("1:1"|"4:3"|"16:9"|"3:4"). An entry without one (everything saved before) counts as square — NO migration; the API maps `ratio: imageRatioOf(im)` on read and `limitImages` on write. Mongoose image subdoc has `ratio` default "1:1".
- Limits: 6 square (gallery, first = cover), 1 each of the other shapes; 9 in all (`MAX_IMAGES_PER_ITEM`); extras dropped by `limitImages`; unknown shape → 400 (zod enum).
- Helpers in `packages/config-schema/src/storage.js`: `ITEM_IMAGE_RATIOS`, `itemImage/itemImageUrl(item, ratio)` (own shape → first square → any), `itemGallery(item, ratio)` (own shape(s) → squares → all), `limitImages`, `imageRoom`, and `CARD_IMAGE_RATIO` (variant id → shape): C02/C03/C09 square, C04/C05/C07 landscape 4:3, C06/C11 wide 16:9, C08 portrait 3:4. C10/C01 have no photo.
- Cards (`packages/ui/src/cards.jsx`) call `itemImageUrl(item, "<shape>")`; dish pop-up uses `itemGallery(item,"16:9")`; order-page suggestions use 4:3; admin/take-order/import thumbnails use square.
- Dashboard `ItemPhotos` (routes/MenuEditor.jsx): four sections (testids `photo-slot-<ratio>`, `item-photo` = squares, `item-photo-<ratio>`, `add-photo[-<ratio>]`, `paste-photo[-<ratio>]`, `photo-file[-<ratio>]`, `remove-photo-…`, `replace-photo-…`, `photo-trim-…`, `paste-target`). Ctrl+V pastes into the last clicked/used section (default square). A picture whose own aspect is >8% off its shape shows "not 4:3 — edges trimmed" (cards crop with object-fit: cover; nothing is cropped on upload).
- AI pictures (ai.service `aiSlotFor`): saved in the shape they're made in; the 3:2 "Photo" shape goes in landscape 4:3; room counted per shape (a non-square shape holds one, so a second needs the first removed).
- Menu import keeps each picture's shape.

**Tests**: t58 (suites/item-image-shapes-api.mjs, 30 checks) and t59 (suites/item-image-shapes-ui.mjs, 45 checks — seeds two square pictures on the bench dish first, checks they're untouched, checks every card style on the storefront) PASS. Existing tests re-run for this change: see the run result in the conversation; t45/t38/t39/t48/t49 etc.

Related: [paste-item-photo](#paste-item-photo), [ai-item-images](#ai-item-images), [growth-features](#growth-features).

### image-compression

_Server-side picture compression on upload (already existed): what it does, the platform settings, the 2026-11-19 hardening (shrink-to-fit, parallel cap, pixel-limit message), t34 test, and the unbuilt thumbnails idea_

User asked (2026-11-19): "do we have image compressor? if any outlet uploads a very high quality image are we compressing server side? if not add a feature". **Answer: yes, it already existed** — `apps/api/src/modules/storage/image.js` `processImage`, called by `putImage` for EVERY picture upload (`POST /api/storage/upload` for store pictures/logos/favicons/category+item pictures, and the website uploads in site.service). Not for WhatsApp chat media (`putFile` keeps them as sent, by design).

**What it does:** accepts anything libvips reads (JPEG/PNG/WebP/AVIF/HEIC/GIF/TIFF/SVG), 30 MB upload cap (the browser's `prepareImage` also trims >6 MB photos to 3200 px first), EXIF auto-rotate, strips ALL metadata (author/GPS), scales to the platform's `maxDimension` (default 1600 px, never enlarges), re-encodes (default WebP q80) and steps quality down to hit `targetMaxKB` (default 400). Platform-wide settings (`PUT /api/platform/storage {processing}`, super admin only; 320–4096 px, q30–100, webp/avif/jpeg/original, 0–20000 KB) — console "Storage". Real numbers: 24 MP q95 JPEG 19 MB → ~300 KB; live dev photos avg 212 KB, max 329 KB (39 files). The storefront `Photo` already lazy-loads.

**Added 2026-11-19 (commit on feature/message-sending, deployed to dev, API restart only):** (1) when quality alone can't reach the size target (grainy detail stopped at 623 KB vs 400) the picture is now shrunk (÷ sqrt of the missing ratio, never below 640 px) until it fits; (2) at most 3 pictures processed at once (dev VPS has ~1.9 GB RAM); (3) a picture over sharp's 268-megapixel ceiling → clear 413 "too many pixels" instead of "isn't an image". Measured: a 225 MP flat PNG "pixel bomb" is handled in ~0.4 s without a memory spike (sequential read), so no extra pixel cap was needed.

**Test:** t34 `image-compression-api` (suites/image-compression.mjs, port 3092, 32 checks) generates real images with sharp (note: to MAKE a >268 MP image in a test pass `limitInputPixels: false` to sharp's create).

**NOT built (offered):** thumbnails/variants — list cards show 72–104 px pictures but load the full ≤400 KB file (~6 MB of images on the Ludhiana page). Portable design = generate a small variant at upload time via the storage provider and store it with the image record (needs schema + model + storefront + delete cleanup + backfill); an on-the-fly `?w=` resizer would only work for the local provider, not R2/S3. Could also lower the defaults (e.g. 1280 px / 300 KB) in the console if the user wants smaller files.

### paste-item-photo

_Dish photos can be pasted from the clipboard (Ctrl/⌘+V or a "Paste photo" tile) in the dashboard's item editor — shared usePasteImages helper, rules, test t45; other picture boxes not yet converted_

2026-11-20, user: "can we add feature to paste image as the item image? currently only upload box (Add Photo) … sometimes we have image on the clipboard." Done in `apps/admin/src/routes/MenuEditor.jsx` (`ItemPhotos`) + new `apps/admin/src/lib/usePasteImages.js` (commit 577c5c6, deployed to dev).

**Rules:** a document-level `paste` listener lives only while the item editor is open; a clipboard picture becomes a photo (uploaded + server-compressed like a chosen file, ≤6 per dish); if the cursor is in a text box AND the clipboard also holds text, the text paste wins (spreadsheet copies); picture-only clipboard works even with the cursor in the Name box. "Paste photo" tile uses `navigator.clipboard.read()` (permission prompt; messages for empty / denied / unsupported). At 6 photos the Add/Paste tiles vanish and a paste says the dish already has six; extra pictures beyond the room are skipped with a note.
**Test t45** (`tests/t45-paste-item-photo.mjs`, driven, headless-safe): draws PNGs on a canvas, puts them on the browser clipboard (`grantPermissions clipboard-read/write`), presses `Meta+V`/`Control+V`; restores the dish's own photos and deletes the new files at the end. The bench dish "Veg Steamed Momos" starts with 0 photos — checks are relative to the starting count.
**Not done (offer if asked):** category pictures (CategoriesPanel), store logo/favicon, store-page photos could reuse `usePasteImages`.

### ai-item-images

_Gemini AI dish pictures (2026-11-19): several API keys managed in the platform console, a ✦ button on each dish row for the super admin ONLY, shape + count pop-up; how keys rotate/rest, the two Google request styles, tests t38/t39, the bench's Google stand-in, what is unverified against real Google_

User asked (2026-11-19): "can we use the Gemini API to generate images? … add a button to the item row, a pop-up asks aspect ratio and how many images, generate with Gemini and save to the item", **only the super admin**; then: **several API keys** (Gemini limits usage per key — spread pictures over them), key management in the super-admin dashboard, the button visible only when the super admin is visiting a store's dashboard, "at last deploy".

**Answer to "can we":** yes — Gemini image models ("Nano Banana"); Imagen is shut down (per Google's docs, 2026). Model ids from Google's guide: `gemini-nano-banana-2.1` (recommended, default), `gemini-3.1-flash-lite-image` (cheapest), `gemini-3.1-flash-image`, `gemini-3-pro-image`, `gemini-2.5-flash-image` (legacy). Needs a billing-enabled key (pricing NOT verified — user must check). Output has an invisible SynthID watermark.

**Build:** config-schema `ai.js` (models, aspect ratios 1:1/4:3/3:2/16:9/3:4, max 4 per request, `buildItemImagePrompt`, `angleFor`); db `PlatformAiModel` (singleton `{model, keys[{id,label,encryptedKey,keyHint,fingerprint,enabled,uses,lastUsedAt,lastError,cooldownUntil}]}`, keys AES-256-GCM via `lib/crypto.js`, max 20); api `modules/ai/gemini.js` (client: tries **Interactions API** `POST /v1beta/interactions` with `response_format:{type:"image",aspect_ratio,image_size:"1K"}`, falls back to `generateContent` on 404/405; header `x-goog-api-key` only; tolerant `findPicture` for either answer shape; `AiError.reason` = "quota"|"key"), `ai.service.js` (key CRUD, free key test = `GET /v1beta/models?pageSize=1`, `generateItemImages`: checks first, pictures made IN PARALLEL each on the least-busy/least-recently-used ready key, a quota-limited key rests 10 min and a refused key 24 h while the picture is retried on the next key, safety refusals/busy don't rest keys, partial success kept, each picture goes through `putImage` (compressed WebP, domain-free)). Routes: platform `GET/PUT /api/platform/ai`, `POST /ai/keys`, `PATCH/DELETE /ai/keys/:id`, `POST /ai/keys/:id/test`; menu `POST /api/menu/items/:id/generate-images {aspectRatio,count,prompt?}` (`requireSuperAdmin` + outlet context; rate policy `ai-images` 15/10 min). Env `GEMINI_API_BASE_URL` (tests only). Admin: console page `/platform/ai` (`PlatformAi.jsx`, nav "AI pictures"), `routes/menu/GenerateImagesDialog.jsx`, ✦ button (`data-testid="ai-button"`) in `ItemsPanel.jsx` only when `useAuth().user.isSuperAdmin`.
**Tests:** t38 `ai-images-api` (suites/ai-images.mjs, port 3101, stand-in Google on a random port; 66 checks) and t39 `ai-item-images` (Chrome, 30 checks; the bench now runs a Google stand-in on :3190 — keys starting `quota-`/`bad-` fail; `GET :3190/__calls` shows what was asked). Lesson: `execFileSync` in a suite freezes the in-memory mongod's pipe — use async.
**NOT verified against the real Google API** (no key available to me; the user must add one): the exact Interactions request/response shape came from a doc summary, hence the tolerant parser + generateContent fallback; if a real call fails, the error message shown in the pop-up carries Google's reason. First thing to do after deploy: console → AI pictures → add key → Test (free) → ✦ on a dish.

### addon-groups-editor

_Dashboard add-on groups — multi-column lists (dish editor tick-list + groups list) and inline edit (✎) of a group's name/options; how selection rules are kept; t46; deployed to dev 2026-11-20_

2026-11-20, requested by the user after the Ludhiana menu-details work ([menu-details-ludhiana](#menu-details-ludhiana)) made the dish editor's Add-on groups tick-list 18 rows long, and groups could not be changed after creation. Committed 88c6674 + 53a6da6 on `feature/message-sending` (pushed, unmerged) and deployed to dev (admin rebuilt only; API/storefront untouched — the `PATCH /api/menu/modifier-groups/:id` route already existed, only the admin UI was missing).

- `apps/admin/src/routes/MenuEditor.jsx`: tick-list in the dish form = CSS grid `repeat(auto-fill, minmax(min(190px,100%),1fr))` (3 columns at the normal card width; the `min(...,100%)` is needed or it overflows on phone widths); groups card at the bottom = same idea at 340px; each row has ✎ (inline `GroupEditor`, spans the full row) and ×.
- Editor changes ONLY name + options (label, ₹ price, add/remove). Option **ids are kept** for existing options (orders/carts/items refer to them); new ones get a server id. Rules are not editable on purpose: `ruleFor()` keeps an "any number" group (max ≥ old option count) at max = new option count, a limited group (pick-one, choose up to N, required) keeps its limit clamped to the option count. Validation: name required, ≥1 option, every option named, price ≥ 0.
- The storefront popup and the API's `priceCart` do NOT enforce group min/max/required (checkboxes only) — only the staff Take-order screen (`ItemSheet`, `modifierSelectionErrors`) does. If rule controls are ever added to the editor, the storefront has to enforce them too or they mislead.
- Test t46 (50 checks): API-made `T46 …` groups, columns/overflow/phone width, edit flow with id preservation, refusals, rule preservation, storefront popup (group names render in CSS uppercase — match case-insensitively). Related re-runs t33/t38/t39/t45 green. Not run: the full suite (change is confined to the add-on UI).

### qr-card-designer

_The QR table-card designer feature — where it lives, how it was verified, and the scannability rules that are load-bearing_

Built 2026-09-05 on branch `feature/qr-card-designer` (pushed, **not merged, not
deployed**). Seven commits, M1–M7. Lets an outlet design one printable table
card and print it for every table, each carrying that table's own signed QR.

**Why it needs care:** the failure mode is silent. A card can look perfect and
not scan, and the owner finds out from a customer after printing forty. Several
non-obvious rules are therefore load-bearing and must not be "simplified" away:

- QR module **edges** snap to whole pixels; the module **size** must stay
  fractional. Flooring the size wastes up to 45% of the box at preview scale.
- Dot modules are drawn **tangent** (radius 0.5×module), not inset. Inset dots
  stop touching and drop below the decode threshold.
- Finder frame and centre must agree about roundness. `isRiskyEyePairing` in
  `packages/config-schema/src/qr-card.js` encodes this; a diamond centre never
  decoded at all and is not offered.
- Eye colours are contrast-checked separately from module colour — a scanner
  locates the code by those three squares before reading anything.

**Verification tooling** (deleted after use, worth recreating for changes):
temporary Vite harness pages under `apps/admin/` plus a sandboxed API on
`mongodb-memory-server` (already a devDependency of apps/api), driven by
`playwright-core` with system Chrome, decoding real rendered output with jsQR
from a CDN. Local dev shares the production database, so UI testing must go
through that sandbox — see [scaffold-state](#scaffold-state).

Benchmark that matters: all 36 templates decode at **4 pixels per QR module**.
Below ~3 px/module you are at the sampling limit and failures are phase noise,
not design defects.

## Pictures and storage

### storage-migration

_2026-11-21 — Storage page: Test connection under the provider list (any storage, nothing activated), usage per store/outlet/kind, "Move files" between the server disk and S3/R2 buckets; how it works, tests t74/t75, real dev numbers_

Built on request (user moved from server disk to Cloudflare R2 and wanted the old media moved; "test any provider without activating", "analytics total/store/outlet", "add your own great features"). Commits 9c9e034 + 7a38512, deployed to dev 2026-11-21 (API restart + admin build).

- **Providers** (`apps/api/src/modules/storage/providers`): local + s3 adapters now also have `get/head/list/urlFor`; s3 signs a query string (ListObjectsV2) — verified against AWS's documented SigV4 example (signature 34b48302…); works for R2 (region `auto`, path-style endpoint).
- **Test** `POST /api/platform/storage/test {provider, values}` (`storage-ops.js testStorage`): write → read back → open from the PUBLIC address → delete; a bucket that takes uploads but isn't public is a *warning* (`publicOk:false`), wrong keys fail step 1 with the service's own message; blank secret = saved one only if same provider; nothing is saved. Probe key is `platform/_probe/<uuid>.txt` (NOT a dot-folder: express.static hides dotfiles).
- **Usage** `POST /api/platform/storage/usage/scan` (`scanStorage`): lists disk + the in-use bucket, groups by `<brandId>/<outletId>/<kind>/file`, plus health: pictures used by stores but nowhere (broken), files nothing refers to (>1 day), files on disk not yet in the bucket. References are found by scanning every collection for upload-key strings (`storage-links.js`; high-volume collections skipped).
- **Move** (`storage-migrate.js`, model `StorageMigration`): source/target = this disk | the storage in use | another S3 (details typed; secret encrypted in the job). list both → copy missing (4 parallel, 3 tries, size verified) → rewrite addresses in the DB for every key now at the destination (old forms: `/uploads/<key>` with any origin, or the old bucket's base URL) → `expireEverything()` → optional delete of VERIFIED old copies. Failed files stay put and keep their old address. One job at a time; cancel; heartbeat every 15 s; a job silent for 60 s is marked `interrupted` (also at API start). Options: one store only, delete old copies, "use destination for new uploads" (saves it as the platform storage at the start).
- Raw collection writes bypass write events, so the move calls `expireEverything()`.
- Tests: t74 (in-process API with `tests/e2e/bench/fake-s3.mjs`, 65 checks), t75 (browser, throwaway store "Storage Test Co").
- **Real dev numbers (read-only check, 2026-11-21):** server disk `/var/www/tablepe-dev/apps/api/.uploads` holds 390 files / 79.4 MB; 373 upload keys are used by stores and ALL are on that disk (none missing) — so the whole old library is still only on the disk and still has to be moved to R2 by the user (Storage page → Move files).

### storage-optimize

_2026-11-21 — Storage page "Optimize pictures" card: scan for pictures over the size limit, view-only grid, select all, optimize (new file + links follow + originals deleted); rules, tests t76/t77, real dev numbers_

User asked: "add an option in the admin dashboard to optimize files (compress the images which are not compressed), scan for those files and list down in a grid (view only), select all and optimize. optimization config (size) is already configured from admin dashboard." Commits 38ffb1e, e996767, 47a41ec; deployed to dev 2026-11-21 (admin build + API restart). Builds on [storage-migration](#storage-migration) and [image-compression](#image-compression).

- **What counts as "not compressed":** a picture the stores USE (referenced in the DB), an image file (jpg/jpeg/png/webp/avif), not a chat file (kinds `whatsapp`/`files`), bigger than the platform's **Target max size** (Storage → Image compression). Target 0 = no limit → scan says so and nothing can start. Unused files are not listed. Pictures that can't get ≥5 % smaller are remembered in `StorageSettled` (per file size + compression-settings fingerprint) and hidden until the settings change.
- **Engine** `apps/api/src/modules/storage/storage-optimize.js`: scan (in-memory `lastScan`, max 3000 listed, biggest first, where = local|cloud; cloud wins when the key is in both) → job (`StorageOptimize` model, 2 at a time because `processImage` has 3 slots server-wide): read → `processImage` with the saved settings → write a NEW key (`<brand>/<outlet>/<kind>/<new uuid>.<ext>`, so CDNs/browsers drop the big one) → verify size → `planKeyReplacements` (storage-links.js: swaps address/key strings of any form + sibling width/height) → `applyChangesIfUnchanged` (filter on the old values: a store owner's edit in between is never overwritten; that doc is skipped) → `expireEverything()` → re-read references, delete old file only if nothing refers to it (option `deleteOriginals`, default ON in the UI with a "can't be undone" confirm), delete a new file nobody ended up using. Cancel still links what was finished. One job at a time; refused while a move runs and vice-versa; `markInterruptedOptimizations` (silent > 60 s, also at API start).
- **Routes** (super admin): GET `/storage/optimize` (job), GET/POST `/storage/optimize/scan`, POST `/storage/optimize` `{keys|all, brandId?, options:{deleteOriginals}}` (only keys from the last scan are accepted), POST `/storage/optimize/:id/cancel`. New rate policy `storage-heavy` (60/10 min) for scan+start.
- **UI** `apps/admin/src/routes/platform/StorageOptimize.jsx`, below "Image compression": summary tiles, store filter, "Select all (n)" (applies to the filtered list), cards with a tick box + size badge (view-only otherwise), 48 at a time + "Show more", job panel with stages/progress/result ("went from X to Y"), auto re-scan when a run ends. Card caption = store / outlet / kind on separate lines.
- **Tests:** t76 (API, 73 checks, real sharp images + fake S3 bucket) and t77 (browser, 37 checks, throwaway "Optimize Test Co", restores compression settings). Gotchas found: the bench API keeps the last scan in memory and its "secrets" rate limit (30/10 min) was exhausted by repeated runs → restart the bench API (`bench.reset()`) after API changes; a job panel from an earlier run is still displayed → wait for a NEW job id.
- **Real dev numbers (read-only scan 2026-11-21):** storage = Cloudflare R2 `tablepe`; saved settings **480 px / q80 / WebP / 250 KB**; 372 pictures in use (80 MB), **122 over 250 KB (35.8 MB)**, all in R2. With 480 px the real saving will be far bigger than the "at least 4.5 MB" shown; nothing has been optimized yet — the user must press it.

### upload-urls-localhost

_Pictures are stored WITHOUT a domain ("/uploads/<key>") and the API adds PUBLIC_API_URL on the way out (deployed 2026-11-19) — how it works, the cleanup script, what still needs doing by hand (files only on the laptop), and the history of the localhost-picture problem_

**Problem history (2026-11-19):** pictures were saved with their FULL address. A local API (no PUBLIC_API_URL) saved `http://localhost:3072/uploads/…` into the database it SHARES with the dev server, so mytimelyne.info showed broken pictures; I repaired it by hand three times (23, 6, 2 items) with a script that re-pointed addresses. The user then asked: "why do we need to fix the image path always — store just the image name and make the domain dynamic."

**Fix (commit a1d1eef, deployed to dev 2026-11-19):**
- DB keeps only `/uploads/<key>` (no domain). `apps/api/src/modules/storage/upload-urls.js` recognises OUR pictures by the key shape `<brand>/<outlet>/<kind>/<uuid>.<ext>` (brand "platform" for the website, outlet "store" for store-wide files) — from localhost, any old domain, or none — and never touches other addresses (CDN links, look-alike /uploads/ paths, data: URLs, text that mentions a path).
- `middleware/upload-urls.js` (mounted in app.js after the body parsers): request bodies → domain stripped (anything the dashboard sends back, even a localhost URL); JSON responses (wraps `res.send` for JSON strings) → `PUBLIC_API_URL` (`publicApiUrl` in env.js; `http://localhost:<API_PORT>` when unset) filled into whole-string values. So old stored absolute addresses heal on read with no migration.
- Local storage provider returns `/uploads/<key>` unless the platform configured an explicit public base (CDN) — that stays absolute. S3/R2 URLs are untouched.
- Non-JSON consumers resolve it themselves with `toPublicUrl()`: receipt preview logo (printing/render.js), `readFile` fallback fetch. WhatsApp chat attachment reader uses the key from disk. The WhatsApp attachment zod schema accepts `/uploads/…` (t02 caught that).
- Cleanup of data at rest: `apps/api/src/scripts/normalize-upload-urls.js` (dry run default, `--apply`, backup JSON, idempotent, lists files missing on this machine). Run on the dev DB 2026-11-19: 69 documents → all addresses domain-free; backup in `/root/tablepe-backups/normalize-upload-urls.backup-2026-11-19T12-24-25-922Z.json`. It REPLACED fix-upload-urls.js (deleted).
- Test t36 `upload-urls-api` (suites/upload-urls.mjs, port 3096 — 3095 is the test bench's OWN helper server (WhatsApp/SMS stand-in) — and 3096 is its WhatsApp-Graph stand-in port, so never run two things on those; 28 checks).

**Still true / gotchas:** a picture only SHOWS if its FILE is on the machine that serves it. Uploads made from the local app (port 3072) land in the laptop's `apps/api/.uploads`; for them to appear on mytimelyne.info the file must also be on the VPS: `rsync -a --ignore-existing --files-from=<keys> apps/api/.uploads/ root@<VPS-IP>:/var/www/tablepe-dev/apps/api/.uploads/` (35 menu photos copied this way 2026-11-19). Shared object storage (R2) is the real fix for that and still not set up — needs the user's bucket details. Local API runs `node --watch`, so it picks up code changes by itself. After editing the DB outside the API, expire the cache (restart the API, or console "expire all").

### browser-image-caching

_2026-11-26 — live pictures come from R2's public address (pub-….r2.dev) with NO Cache-Control; fix = cache header on new uploads (s3.js) + script to give existing pictures the header in place; pushed as 27493f6, NOT deployed, script NOT yet run on prod_

User: "add browser level image caching for storefront." Findings: the storefront's `<img>` already have `loading="lazy" decoding="async"` and stable URLs, `apps/storefront/public` has no images; the API's local `/uploads` already sends `public, max-age=31536000, immutable` (app.js ~line 73). BUT every live picture (389 on the page, logo included) is served from `https://pub-<id>.r2.dev/<brand>/<outlet>/<kind>/<uuid>.webp` with only ETag + Last-Modified (no Cache-Control) → browsers guess / revalidate. (r2.dev is R2's rate-limited public dev address; a custom domain behind Cloudflare would also allow edge caching — not set up, worth telling the user.)

Built (main 27493f6, test t84 23 checks): `providers/s3.js` — `IMAGE_CACHE_CONTROL`, `cacheControlFor(contentType)` (pictures only), `put` sends it as a SIGNED header, `head` returns contentType + cacheControl, new `setCacheControl` = CopyObject onto itself with `x-amz-metadata-directive: REPLACE` (checks for an <Error> inside a 200 body). `modules/storage/storage-cache.js` `refreshImageCacheHeaders({apply,limit,cfg})` (4 parallel, skips pictures already at max-age ≥ 6 digits, picture = jpg/jpeg/png/webp/avif/gif by extension). `scripts/set-image-cache-headers.js` — `node src/scripts/set-image-cache-headers.js` (dry run) / `--apply --limit 1` / `--apply`. Keys are never overwritten (uuid per upload, optimize writes new keys) so immutable is safe. `tests/e2e/bench/fake-s3.mjs` now supports cache-control + CopyObject.

**TO DO after the deploy (needs SSH):** on the VPS in `/var/www/tablepe-prod/apps/api` run the dry run, then `--apply --limit 1`, verify with `curl -I` on that picture (Cache-Control present, content-length/ETag-size unchanged), then `--apply` for the rest; restart not needed. Then re-check a live image's headers. Tell the user the run changed only headers, not the pictures.

**DONE 2026-11-26 (user: "deploy to production now"):** deployed main 27493f6 (rsync 30 files; built storefront + website + admin; restarted api/storefront/website; WhatsApp worker reconnected). Then on the VPS (`/var/www/tablepe-prod/apps/api`): dry run → 513 pictures, 0 had the header; `--apply --limit 1` → exactly 1 set (checked on the public r2.dev address: `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable`, md5 of the downloaded bytes == ETag, valid WebP); `--apply` → 512 set + 1 already, 0 failed. Proof on all 364 pictures of the live page (snapshot before/after with curl -I): 364/364 have the header, ETag changed on 0, size on 0, type on 0 (only Last-Modified moves to "now", expected after a copy). Not done / worth telling the user: r2.dev is R2's rate-limited public dev address; a custom domain through Cloudflare would add edge caching.

## Orders, tables and the kitchen

### tables-dine-in

_Tables + dine-in QR tests t18/t19 (2026-11-18, on feature/analytics, UNCOMMITTED): how tables and table sessions behave, the SSR cookie bug found and fixed, findings left as-is, helpers added to ui.mjs_

User asked (2026-11-18) for saved Chrome-only tests of **table creation with the dine-in option**. Written: `tests/e2e/tests/t18-tables.mjs` (54 checks: add/list/zones/limits/duplicates, QR panel + PNG download, remove, per-outlet + per-store isolation, roles) and `t19-dine-in.mjs` (55 checks: scan → sign in → "Send to kitchen" → running tab, second order, stranger turned away, staff move/settle/void/clean, remove rules, rotate/forged/unknown codes, Dine-in QR ordering switched off in Settings). Docs in `tests/e2e/docs/`. Both on the working branch `feature/analytics`, uncommitted.

**How it behaves (rules the tests lock in):** table numbers are unique per OUTLET (not per store), case-sensitive, trimmed, max 20 chars; seats 1–50 (0/blank silently becomes 4); zone optional (shown as "Main"). A table's QR = `/t/<id>?k=<hmac token>`; rotating invalidates the printed code. Dine-in orders come ONLY from a table QR (no walk-in table picker is offered in the UI, even though `/api/tables/claim` exists). Diner must sign in; order button says "Send to kitchen"; orders join one open session per table (running tab). Closing a table needs every order paid ("Confirm payment" lives in the Orders → Tables overview); "Cancel / void" cancels open orders and frees it; a seated table can't be removed; freed tables go "Needs cleaning" → "Mark cleaned" → Free. Kitchen can only look (`table:read`); manager/cashier can manage; accountant has no table access.

**Bug found + fixed (uncommitted):** the storefront's server-rendered table pages (`/t/[tableId]` and `/order`) called `/api/tables/scan` without the visitor's cookie, so a diner already seated was told "Another party is seated here" and couldn't order twice. Fix: `apps/storefront/src/lib/visitor.js` (`visitorHeaders()`) forwarded in both pages. Also the Tables page now shows the error when "Close" is refused for unpaid orders (it was silent).

**Findings logged, NOT changed:** validation errors say only "Validation failed"; 0/blank seats → 4 silently; "T9" and "t9" are different tables; Table cards page warns codes point at localhost in the bench (STOREFRONT_ORIGIN).

**Helpers added to `bench/ui.mjs`:** `openTables, tableTiles, tableTile, addTable, openTableQr, tableQrUrl`; `customerSignIn` now also accepts the "Send to kitchen" button. Not covered: decoding the QR image itself, the table claim-by-number API, staff "Take order for a table", bill-requested status. See [test-bench](#test-bench), [feedback-save-every-test](#feedback-save-every-test), [analytics](#analytics).

### table-conflict

_Two staff taking an order for the same table — atomic table claim + "still vacant?" check + move to a free table (2026-11-21, t64, deployed to dev)_

Bug found by the user 2026-11-21: two waiters could both take an order for the same free table. Cause: `openSession` read the table, created a session, then `table.save()` — no compare-and-set, and the Take order screen sent no idea of what it had seen.

**Fix (deployed to dev, commits 4b5d28e + a08aca5, test t64):**
- `openSession` (tables.service.js) claims the table with ONE `updateOne` filtered on the `activeSessionId` it read; loser deletes its session and gets 409 `{code:"table_taken", occupied:true, tableNumber}`. The older "Another party is already seated" 409 carries the same details.
- `POST /api/orders/staff` takes optional `expectedSessionId` (party the screen saw at pick time; null = free; missing = older screen, no check). A mismatch → 409 table_taken (`occupied` true/false).
- Take order UI: `seenSessionId` in useTakeOrder state (recorded on pick, or when sessions load; reset to the placed order's sessionId after a send). OrderPanel shows a warning box (`data-testid=table-taken`) with free-table buttons (`move-to-<no>`), blocks Send; `moveTable` keeps items AND typed customer. Occupied→free case offers "Continue on Table N".
- TakeOrder's "clear the typed customer when the party changes" effect is skipped while a conflict exists (otherwise the number vanished before the move).

**Why:** "before confirming the order, first check that table is still vacant or not, if not vacant then show option to move to another table" (user's words).
**How to apply:** any new way of seating/placing on a table must go through the CAS claim; tests t18/t19/t53 still pass.

### account-orders-and-delete

_Storefront Account page (/<outlet>/account, 'My orders' header link) + Delete order on the dashboard Orders page (2026-11-19, branch feature/message-sending, UNCOMMITTED): soft-delete rules, coins handling, tests t29+t30_

User asked (2026-11-19, same message as "deploy it" for rate limiting): "add Account section in storefront where users can see their order history, order status, payment status with mode of payment, total coins (if feature enabled); an option to delete an order from orders page; an option in menu link to manage and see orders". Interpreted: Account = `/<outletSlug>/account` (the slug is already reserved in `RESERVED_PAGE_SLUGS`); "menu link" = the storefront header menu (`headerLinks()` in `components/Chrome.jsx`) → built-in link **"My orders"**; "orders page" delete = the DASHBOARD Orders page (not a customer-side delete).

**Storefront:** `app/[outletSlug]/account/page.jsx` (server, store chrome) + client `components/AccountView.jsx`: signed-out → `CustomerGate` with new optional props `heading`/`intro` ("Sign in to see your orders"); signed-in → name/phone, **Your coins** card only when `coins.enabled`, order cards (status pill + sentence per status/channel, progress tracker while active, payment = mode ("Pay at the counter" / "Cash on delivery" / "Pay at the table" / "Online — Razorpay") + state (To pay / Paid / Payment failed / Refunded / Not charged), total, items, "View details" = bill + coins used/earned), "Show older orders" paging, auto-refresh every 20 s while any order is active (merges, keeps older pages), Sign out. Test ids: `account`, `account-who`, `account-coins`, `account-empty`, `data-order`, `order-status`, `order-status-line`, `order-payment`, `payment-status`, `order-total`, `order-details`.
**API:** `GET /api/customer/orders?outletId=&before=&limit=` (customer cookie; brand-wide history via `customerOrderHistory` in orders.service; returns `{orders[], nextBefore, customer, coins:{enabled,balance,valuePaise}}`; coins enabled ⇔ plan has `wallet` feature AND outlet `wallet.enabled`; limit policy `customer-orders` 90/10 min). `DELETE /api/orders/:id` permission **`order:delete`** (owner = ALL, manager added; cashier/kitchen/accountant no), limit `destructive`.
**Delete = soft delete:** `Order.deletedAt/deletedBy/deletedByName`; a schema middleware in `packages/db/src/models/order.js` hides deleted orders from EVERY find/update/count/distinct/aggregate (`{ includeDeleted: true }` option shows them; `aggregate().option()` too). `store-delete.service` passes includeDeleted so a store wipe removes them. `deleteOrder` (orders.service): blocks **Razorpay + paid** (409 `order_paid_online`, "refund it first"); cash/test-gateway fine; `reverseRedeemForOrder` (idempotent via ledger unique (orderId,kind)) + new `revokeEarnForOrder` (ledger kind **`revoke`**, bounded by the balance, once); cancels queued print jobs; broadcasts `order.deleted` (admin stream refetches on any `order.*`). Dashboard: Orders row ⋮ → "Delete order" → `DeleteOrderDialog` (no Enter-to-confirm, shows coin note + server error).
**Tests:** t29 `orders-account-api` (suites/orders-account.mjs, port 3088, 48 checks; calls `db.WalletLedgerModel.init()` first — raw inserts before index build break the double-refund guard), t30 `account-orders` (Chrome). Bench: Acme has wallet on; seeded customer Asha 9876543210.
**Gotchas:** never `next build` inside apps/storefront (user's dev servers on 3070-3073 use it; the bench builds a copy in `.bench/storefront`); the soft-delete hook means raw `db.collection("orders")` queries still see deleted rows.
See [rate-limiting](#rate-limiting), [customer-auth-checkout](#customer-auth-checkout).

**Follow-ups the same evening (user messages while building):**
- **Customer can remove an order from their OWN account** (`DELETE /api/customer/orders/:id?outletId=`, policy `customer-account`): sets `Order.customerHiddenAt` only — NOT the dashboard soft delete. The customer's history (`customerOrderHistory` filter `customerHiddenAt: null`) skips it; the store still sees it everywhere (orders list, reports, table tab), payment and coins untouched, the customer's direct order link still opens, idempotent, only their own order (404 otherwise), 403 through another store, 401 signed out. UI: "Remove" next to "View details" → inline ask (says the restaurant keeps its record; active orders carry on) → "Remove"/"Keep it" (`order-remove`, `order-remove-ask`). Store can still delete it later from the dashboard.
- **Store name instead of outlet name** in the storefront header, footer, big heading, tab titles, table-QR page and live preview: the API bundle (`outlet-cache.js loadBundle`) adds `brandName` to every public outlet; storefront `lib/outlet.js` carries it and exports `storeName(outlet)` (brandName → identity.name → outlet.name). The outlet's own name now appears only in the outlet dropdown (OrderBar/OutletPicker) and where it is truly about one outlet (checkout "n items · outlet", account order cards, the "unavailable" notice). Test t31 `store-name-header`.
- Tests so far green: t28 124, t29 63, t30 41, t31 12. The wider regression of things touched (t01,06,09,11,12,13,17–20,23,26,27) was started after.
- STATE: all of this (account page, delete order, customer remove, store name, tests t29–t31) is UNCOMMITTED/UNDEPLOYED until the user says "deploy"; the rate-limiting commit 77f94a2 is already deployed.

**CHANGED 2026-11-21 (UNCOMMITTED, not deployed): who may delete an order.** Was owner + manager (`order:delete`); now ONLY the store owner and the platform super admin — `order:delete` removed from the manager role in `packages/config-schema/src/rbac.js` (owner/super_admin get ALL permissions), manager role text now says "not users, payment keys or deleting orders". Effect: `DELETE /api/orders/:id` gives a manager 403 and the Orders ⋯ menu hides "Delete order" for them (it already used `can("order:delete")`). Tests t29 (manager 403 + order untouched, super admin 200; 64 checks) and t30 (manager's menu has no Delete order; 42 checks).

### customer-auth-checkout

_Mandatory customer signup/login gate before checkout, and the cart popup becoming a dedicated Order page — architecture and non-obvious pitfalls_

Built on branch `feature/customer-auth-checkout` (pushed, **not merged, not
deployed**). Two commits: `d41dccd` (API/schema/admin) and `720fc70`
(storefront). Related: [qr-card-designer](#qr-card-designer) for the sandbox-testing pattern
this reused (mongodb-memory-server + Playwright + system Chrome, never against
the shared prod-linked local DB).

**What changed:** the "Your order" popup is now a real page
(`/outletSlug/order`, `/t/tableId/order`), and it's gated on sign-in — coupon
and coin redemption always needed a real `customerId` server-side (the wallet
service already returned "Sign in to use your coins." for a guest), so the
gate is what makes those features reachable at all, not just a UX choice.
Signup now collects name/gender/age (required) + email (optional) alongside
the existing phone+OTP flow.

**Load-bearing decisions, don't re-litigate without re-reading the commits:**

- The signup form **always** shows all profile fields for a logged-out
  visitor, whether the phone is already registered or not — the service only
  knows after the OTP is verified, and branching the form on registration
  status pre-verification would leak which numbers have accounts.
- A **returning** customer's stored profile always wins over whatever the
  form resubmits (someone else's stale browser autofill must never overwrite
  a real account). A **legacy** customer (missing fields, pre-dates this
  feature) gets backfilled from submitted values instead — two different
  code paths in `customer.service.js`, both covered by
  `apps/api/__customer-db-test.mjs`-style tests (recreate from the commit if
  needed, it wasn't committed).
- `gender`/`age` are optional at the Mongoose level on purpose — `isProfileComplete()`
  in `packages/config-schema/src/customer.js` is the single source of truth
  for "does this customer still need the completion gate," used by both API
  and storefront so they can't drift.
- **The dine-in session bug**: a QR table's read-only session lookup
  (`GET /api/tables/scan/:id`, used for the "running tab" banner) only ever
  returns a session that already exists — it's `null` until the *first* order
  creates one. Placing an order must always call the create-or-join endpoint
  (`POST .../session`), never reuse the GET's value, or a table's first order
  of a visit fails outright. If dine-in ordering ever breaks again, check this
  first — it's the one place the old single-component flow's implicit
  behavior was easy to lose during the page split.

**Not built:** past-orders history (explicitly deferred — this session only
built the login/signup gate it depends on). Admin cannot edit a customer's
profile, only view it (no `customer:manage` permission exists).

### kitchen-display

_Kitchen Display System at /kitchen (2026-11-21): per-station stages, order status follows, late colours, waiter bell (off by default), plan feature kitchenDisplay, t65, deployed to dev_

Built on the user's request ("can we build KDS?" → yes; separate /kitchen URL; waiter notified when ready, **can be disabled in store settings, default false**). Commits 0a6f120 + 9668615 on main, deployed to dev 2026-11-21 (admin rebuilt + API restarted).

- **Plan feature `kitchenDisplay`** ("Kitchen display (KDS)") — the REAL Basic/Pro/Custom plans must be ticked in the console (same as the growth features); bench Pro has all.
- **Config** `outlet.config.kitchenDisplay = { notifyWaiter:false, warnAfterMinutes:10, lateAfterMinutes:20 }` (Settings → Kitchen & delivery → Kitchen display card).
- **Model:** `order.kds = { kitchen:{stage,at,by}, bar:{…} }` (Mixed). Stage per station = new|preparing|ready; a station with no record falls back to the order's own status. Stations come from `stationsForItems` + `expandCombos` (same rule as KOT tickets). Written with `$set kds.<station>` so kitchen and bar taps never overwrite each other.
- **Order status follows:** first Start → preparing; ALL involved stations ready → ready (via `updateStatus`, so printers/customer messages fire); recall of a ready order → `setStatusManual(preparing)`.
- **API** `/api/kitchen/orders?screen=all|kitchen|bar` (order:read) and `POST /api/kitchen/orders/:id/stage {screen, stage: preparing|ready}` (order:update), gated by `requireFeature(kitchenDisplay)`. Board shows accepted/preparing orders + ready ones for 30 min; unpaid online orders hidden.
- **UI** `apps/admin/src/routes/kitchen/Kitchen.jsx` — fixed full-screen like Take order; lanes New/Preparing/Ready (tabs on narrow), station tabs (`?screen=bar`), sound switch (localStorage, needs one tap to unlock audio), waiting colours amber/red from `acceptedAt`, WebSocket `order.*` events invalidate `["kitchen"]` + 8 s poll fallback. Sidebar item "Kitchen display" (feature-gated).
- **Waiter bell:** server broadcasts `kds.ready` only when notifyWaiter is on; `components/KitchenBell.jsx` (mounted in App next to Shell) shows a card + beep on any page for users with order:create. Not shown on /kitchen.
- Test t65 (43 checks) uses `page.clock` to fast-forward the late colours. Not built: per-dish (line) tick-off, KDS-specific roles, order-ready sound on the kitchen screen itself.

### kot-tickets

_2026-11-20/21 — kitchen ticket (KOT) upgrade: dishes routed to kitchen/bar printers by "Prepared at", ORDER CHANGED tickets on edit, ORDER CANCELLED tickets on cancel; how it works, rules, gaps left, test t51_

User asked "do we have a proper KOT feature? if not what should be?" — I answered (existing: printers by station/doc/target, templates, auto-print on chosen statuses, retries, reprint; gaps: no item→station routing, edits after the ticket never reach the kitchen, a cancelled order prints a normal-looking ticket, no KOT numbers, no kitchen display screen, no courses, no tests) and the user said "please build" → built items 1–3. Committed on `main` only when the user says (they now work directly on main, no branches).

**What exists now**
- `prepStation` ("kitchen" | "bar" | null) on categories AND dishes (DB models in `packages/db/src/models/menu.js`, schema + `PREP_STATIONS` in config-schema `printing.js`/`menu.js`, menu API body/shape). A dish's own setting wins, else the nearest category up the tree, else the kitchen. Dashboard: category ⋯ → Edit… → "Prepared at"; dish form → "Prepared at" (`data-testid` `category-prep-station` / `item-prep-station`).
- `apps/api/src/modules/printing/kot.js`: `stationsForItems`, `activeKotPrinters`, `entriesForPrinter` (kitchen/bar printers split by station; a dish whose station has no printer goes to the kitchen printer, and if there is no kitchen printer to EVERY ticket — never lost; KOT printers at other stations (counter/packing/manager) are master copies with every dish), `lineKey`, `diffLines` (item+size+add-ons+note identity; qty difference → ADD/REMOVE).
- `printing.service.js`: `enqueueJob` routes lines for docType "kot" (empty ticket = no job; `keepIfEmpty` for hand prints/reprints), stores `PrintJob.ticket` ({kind:"change"|"cancel", changes, noteChanged}) so a reprint is the same ticket; `printOrderChange` (called from `editOrder` in orders.service via `firePrintChange`) = every KOT printer that makes a changed dish gets an ORDER CHANGED ticket, but only if the order's ticket already went out (queued/printing/printed) anywhere; a note-only change prints "Note is now: …"; `printOrderCancelled` (called from `autoPrintForOrder` when status = cancelled, which every cancel path goes through) withdraws queued KOT jobs and sends an ORDER CANCELLED / DO NOT COOK ticket to printers that already printed (a KOT printer's own "fire on cancelled" no longer prints the ordinary ticket).
- `render.js`: `bannerBlocks`/`blocksOf` put a banner in front of any template (so a template can't hide it); change tickets list "ADD:" / "REMOVE (do not make):".
- Print log (`JobQueue.jsx`) shows pills "order changed" / "order cancelled" (API `ticketKind` on jobs).
- Test t51 = `suites/kot-tickets-api.mjs` (in-process API, port 3086, reads ticket text from saved jobs, 46 checks) + `suites/kot-menu-ui.mjs` (Chrome, 16 checks).

**Gotchas / decisions**
- Mongoose `enum` rejects `null`, so `prepStation` has no DB enum; the API zod enforces kitchen|bar.
- A first design only sent change tickets to printers that had the original; wrong (a chai added to a kitchen-only order never reached the bar) — now "ticket sent anywhere ⇒ every printer that makes a changed dish gets it".
- Banner second line says "Only the kitchen note changed" when no dish changed (an always-"See ADD / REMOVE" banner confused both people and the test).
- Not built (still open): KOT numbers/round numbers, kitchen display screen (KDS), courses/hold-fire, per-item "done", more stations than kitchen/bar, category "Prepared at" at creation time (only in Edit), a ticket when an order is *deleted*.

Related: [deploy-workflow](#deploy-workflow) (work on main), [missed-order-alerts](#missed-order-alerts), [tables-dine-in](#tables-dine-in).

**Print station on every page (2026-11-21, committed 51cbebe on main and deployed to dev — admin rebuilt only, no restarts):** the user asked that printing start from any dashboard page. Browser/ePOS-LAN tickets are printed by a browser acting as a "print station" (setting `onetap.printStation` in localStorage, per device, default OFF). It used to run ONLY while the Printing page was open (`usePrintRunner` was called in Printing.jsx). Now `PrintStationProvider` (in `apps/admin/src/lib/usePrintRunner.js`) is mounted once in `App.jsx` around `<Shell>`, and the Printing page uses `usePrintStation()` (the shared instance); it polls every 4 s only when the device is switched on AND `print:job` AND the plan has Printing (so a Starter store never polls), and also checks at once when the tab/window becomes visible/focused. A sidebar chip (`data-testid="print-station-chip"`, "Print station on" / "problem") shows it is active. Limits: the dashboard must stay OPEN in a browser at the outlet (hidden/minimised tabs are throttled by the browser); a full page reload while a ticket is mid-print leaves it "printing" until the server's 90 s stale-claim sweep requeues it; the browser print dialog still needs a click unless Chrome runs with `--kiosk-printing`. Test t52 (14 checks, `suites/print-station-any-page.mjs`; navigate by the sidebar, not `page.goto`, right after switching the station on).

### missed-order-alerts

_Missed-order alerts (Settings → Missed-order alerts, the SLA/pending-order warning) test t20 (2026-11-18, on feature/analytics, UNCOMMITTED): how the feature works, two bugs found + fixed, how the test fakes minutes, what is not covered_

The user's "pending order notification feature" = **Settings → Missed-order alerts** (`operations.sla`, evaluated by `evaluateSla` in config-schema `operations.js`). Test: `tests/e2e/tests/t20-missed-order-alerts.mjs` (74 checks, ~3 min, Chrome-only, docs in tests/e2e/docs). It covers OFF (nothing warns anyone, order stays Placed, dashboard "Missed orders 0", no sound), ON (banner, red row "Placed 1m — over 1m limit", "Running late (n)", "Show late orders", dashboard "Missed orders n"), sound waits for a click, new late order chimes, "Keep reminding me" repeats each minute, "Dismiss all", per-stage messages (placed→"hasn't been accepted", accepted→"was accepted but nobody has started cooking", preparing→"has been cooking longer than expected", ready→"is ready and still waiting to go out"), sound off / reminders off, manager+cashier+kitchen all see it, and the customer side (confirmation identical, nothing changes for them — there is no order-tracking page).

**How it works:** defaults enabled, accept 2 / start 5 / finish 20 / hand over 10 minutes (min 1, max 240; 0 → 1, 241 → "Validation failed"); Orders screen computes lateness in the browser from each order's last status change; the dashboard's "Missed orders" is computed by the API with the server clock. Only open orders (placed/accepted/preparing/ready) have a clock; abandoned prepaid checkouts are excluded. Nothing is sent to the customer or staff outside the dashboard and a late order is never auto-accepted/cancelled.

**Bugs found by the test and fixed (uncommitted):** (1) "Dismiss all warnings" also removed the red row tint and the "Running late" count (hook returned only the un-dismissed list) — `useSlaAlerts` now returns `breaches` (all late, for tint/count) and `alerts` (not dismissed, for banner/sound); (2) "Keep reminding me" never actually re-chimed — the chime effect wasn't keyed on time; added a 10 s `remindSlot` to its deps; (3) banner said "Sound on" even with "Play a sound" off → now "Sound is off (Settings → Missed-order alerts)"; (4) "for 1 minutes" → "1 minute". Verified by temporarily reverting (1)+(2): 5 checks failed, then restored.

**Technique worth reusing:** the counter tab gets an init script that counts AudioContext oscillators (2 per chime) and skews `Date` (`window.__skewSet(ms)`) so minutes pass without waiting — the order data and server stay real; the one real ~63 s wait is for the dashboard's server-side count. Rewinding the skew makes dismissed state reset (orders leave/re-enter "late"), so the stage walk cancels order B first and uses a fresh order D afterwards. Status buttons in an Orders row are labelled with the NEXT status ("Accepted") so row text always contains it — read the status cell, not the row, for the current status. Not covered: real audio output, other phones/devices, abandoned-prepaid exclusion, the per-outlet config of several outlets. See [test-bench](#test-bench), [tables-dine-in](#tables-dine-in), [feedback-save-every-test](#feedback-save-every-test).

**CHANGED 2026-11-21 (UNCOMMITTED, not deployed) — only two moments alert now.** User: the red delayed-order alert should show only while an order is still pending (nobody accepted it); after accepting, a NEW timer runs once it is in Preparing, and it alerts if Preparing lasts longer than the store's limit (e.g. 20 min); no alerts for Ready or Completed. Done in `packages/config-schema/src/operations.js`: `slaSchema` lost `startMinutes` (accepted) and `handoverMinutes` (ready); `SLA_FOR_STATUS` = `{ placed: "acceptMinutes", preparing: "prepMinutes" }` (so `evaluateSla` — used by the Orders screen via `useSlaAlerts` AND the dashboard's "Missed orders" count on the API — returns null for accepted/ready/completed/cancelled). The preparing limit is the existing "Finish cooking within (minutes)" setting (default 20), counted from the moment the order entered Preparing (`statusSince` = last status change). Settings → Missed-order alerts now has just "Accept within" and "Finish cooking within". Old saved values of the two removed limits are simply ignored (zod strips unknown keys). Consequence to remember: an order accepted but never moved to Preparing never alerts. Tests: t20 (75 checks: defaults 2/20, no start/hand-over fields, accepted + ready never warn however long, preparing gets a fresh timer) and t08 (settings spec no longer fills the two removed fields).

### growth-features

_2026-11-20/21 — the 11-feature batch (bookings, pre-orders, out-for-delivery + riders, ratings, order again, upsell/combos, stock, menu hours, cart reminders, BOGO): design decisions, where the code is, test suites, UI status; UNCOMMITTED on main_

User asked (2026-11-20, after my competitor comparison): "build the Table reservation system, Takeaway schedule/pre-order, Out for delivery status, Assigning rider, rating feedback, order again, upsell/combo, stock count, timebased menu, abandoned cart reminder (enable/disable per store), buy 1 get 1 — later I will ask questions". Working on `main`, NOT committed (commit only on "deploy").

**Decisions (made by me, tell the user):**
- New PLAN features (FEATURES in config-schema plan.js): `reservations`, `scheduledOrders` (Pre-orders), `feedback` (Ratings), `cartReminders`. Stores on NO plan get them automatically; the real Basic/Pro/Custom plans have explicit lists → the super admin must TICK them. Not plan-gated: riders/out-for-delivery (part of Delivery), order again, upsell/combos, stock, menu hours, BOGO (part of Coupons).
- India time everywhere via `packages/config-schema/src/clock.js` (istParts, istDate, shiftDay, formatWhen…). Names `addDays`/`WEEKDAYS` already existed in analytics.js → mine are `shiftDay`/`WEEKDAY_SHORT` (export * silently drops duplicate names!).
- Opening hours (operations.hours) were NEVER enforced for ASAP orders (finding, not fixed) — pre-order slots and booking slots use opensAt/closesAt.
- Status `out_for_delivery` (delivery only; `nextStatusesFor(status, channel)`, `statusLabelFor` → completed delivery = "Delivered"). Rider = outlet list (RiderModel, name+phone, no login), `PATCH /api/orders/:id/rider {riderId|name+phone, sendOut}`; customer sees rider only while out.
- Pre-orders: takeaway only, `operations.scheduling` (maxDaysAhead, minLeadMinutes, slotMinutes, maxPerSlot), `GET /api/orders/pickup-slots`, order.scheduledFor; accept-alert clock starts 60 min before pickup; KOT banner "PRE-ORDER / Pickup 8:30 PM, Sat 22 Nov".
- Stock: item.stock {tracked,left,daily,lowAt,resetDay}; held atomically at placement (order.stockHold), given back on cancel/delete(not completed)/unpaid-online >30 min (sweeper every 5 min, all processes); daily refill lazily on first menu read of the IST day; staff bypass dish HOURS but not stock.
- Menu hours: `availability {windows:[{days,from,to}]}` on category (inherited) or dish; `menuForNow()` marks unavailable + label "Available 8 AM – 11 AM"; priced at the pre-order's pickup time; `config.menuTimes.hideOutsideHours`.
- Combos: item.combo {items:[{itemId,variantId,quantity}]} (no nested combos); priced line carries `includes`; KOT expands parts to their own printers (`expandCombos` in printing/kot.js).
- Upsell: item.pairsWith + auto "ordered together" (90 days completed orders, cached 6 h), `POST /api/menu/suggestions`, `config.upsell`.
- Ratings: FeedbackModel (one per order, editable 7 days, orders ≤30 days, completed only), Google link when ≥ googleMinStars, `GET /api/feedback` (feedback:read: owner/manager/accountant).
- Bookings: ReservationModel (code B-0001), statuses requested→confirmed→seated→completed / declined / cancelled / no_show; capacity = maxGuestsPerSlot or sum of table seats; staff can `force`; customer max 3 upcoming, cancel window; messages via `sendCustomerText` (linked WhatsApp else SMS).
- Cart reminders: BRAND-level `BrandModel.cartReminders` (store-wide), carts saved only while on (`PUT /api/customer/cart`), worker runs only where WHATSAPP_WORKER or MARKETING_WORKER is on; one per customer per minDaysBetween (CustomerModel.cartRemindedAt), sending hours, carts ≤24 h; CartReminderModel log keeps stats (won back = order ≤48 h after); link `/<outlet>/order?cart=<id>`.
- BOGO: coupon discountType "bogo" (buyQty, getQty, itemIds, categoryIds, maxFreeUnits); cheapest eligible units free.
- New permissions: reservation:read/manage (manager, staff), feedback:read (manager, accountant), rider:manage (manager).
- New rate-limit policies: cart-sync, customer-feedback, reservation-request.
