## Messaging, marketing and analytics

### whatsapp-chat

_WhatsApp chat over the store's own linked numbers (Baileys, no paid API) — branch feature/whatsapp-chat (uncommitted); ONE worker process rule (WHATSAPP_WORKER=on only on the dev server), user-chosen pacing/limits, routing rules, access model, tests, deploy state_

Built 2026-11-15 on branch `feature/whatsapp-chat` (created from main @ 11ec565, which already had the favicon/filters batch — the user committed that themselves as "Fixed some bugs"). **Uncommitted**; deployed to the dev server ([dev-server-mytimelyne](#dev-server-mytimelyne)).

**User's choices (asked):** library **Baileys** (`@whiskeysockets/baileys@7.0.0-rc14`, not whatsapp-web.js — Chrome per number won't fit the 1.9 GB VPS); a **plan feature** `whatsappChat` (no existing real plan includes it — Gazab is on Pro, so the user must tick it); **"very careful" pacing** for numbers that never wrote to the store: 2–4 min apart, 15/day per number, none 22:00–09:00 IST; broadcasts only to the store's customers, with "Reply STOP to stop offers" + STOP/START handling.

**Load-bearing design:**
- **Exactly one process holds the WhatsApp connections**: env `WHATSAPP_WORKER=on` (default off). Set ON only in `/var/www/tablepe-dev/apps/api/.env`. Local dev, live food-api etc. share the same Mongo DB — two copies connecting one session makes WhatsApp kick them ("connectionReplaced"). Everything else talks to the worker through DB docs: queued WaMessage = send; pending WaAccount = link (worker writes `qr`); `wantsLogout` = unlink; worker heartbeats `seenAt` (online = fresh < 90 s).
- Session keys in `waauthkeys`, encrypted with ENCRYPTION_KEY (so a key mismatch breaks linked numbers like it breaks S3 — see [storefront-banners-pages-orderbar](#storefront-banners-pages-orderbar) / the local key issue).
- Pace per message: `warm` (customer has written to us, or a manual follow-up within 24 h of our last send) 1–3 s typing + 1.2 s gap; `cold` (above limits, quiet hours); `order` (order update to someone who never chatted: 30–60 s apart, 40/day/number, no quiet hours — my decision, told to the user). Cold/order sends check `onWhatsApp` first; never message a non-WhatsApp number.
- Routing: chat has a sticky `accountId` = the number the customer last wrote to. Owner sends while it's offline → 409 `number_offline` + online alternatives → UI asks; the chosen one becomes the chat's number. New chats / broadcasts / order updates to new people pick the online number with the fewest first contacts today (broadcast: round-robin). Broadcast to someone whose sticky number is offline waits for that number.
- Access: owner = manage (numbers, broadcast, access); other roles = none/read/send set by the owner in WhatsApp → Access (`Brand.whatsapp.access`); platform super admin = read (opening a store from the console). Plan feature gate on every route (`waContext`).
- Order updates: `orderNotify.linked` (free text, default `WA_ORDER_DEFAULT_BODY`), Settings → Notifications → "Your WhatsApp numbers"; queued from `fireOrderNotifications` before the paid-API `notifications` feature check.
- Media: `putFile`/`readFile` in storage.service (raw, 16 MB); inbound media downloaded by the worker and stored. Baileys v7 may address contacts by `@lid` — mapped to phone via `remoteJidAlt` / `lidMapping.getPNForLID`. Our sends use a pre-chosen `messageId` (saved first) so the echo isn't recorded as "sent from phone".

**Verified:** API+worker suite 85/85 (stand-in socket for sending/receiving; a REAL link QR fetched from WhatsApp's servers; encryption round-trip), dashboard browser suite 44/44 (owner/manager/cashier, offline-number prompt, attachments, clear/delete, numbers + QR, broadcast, access, notifications tab, phone layout). Never tested with a real scanned phone — that's the user's first step.

**Added later the same day:** number colours (`WaAccount.color`, chip filled with it, `waTextOn` picks black/white text; new numbers take the next unused palette colour); per-number stats (`GET /stats`, Numbers page); "Share chats out" (`planWaDistribution(chats, share, giveAway)` — Share = equal part, Give away = empties into the sharers; never-wrote chats move first, then the quietest; idempotent); moved chats keep `lastInboundAccountId` = the number the customer actually talked to, so the NEW number's first message is paced as cold (ban safety) and a customer writing to the old number pulls the chat back; Clean up tab (messages older than N days / before a date via `DatePickerInput`, or whole quiet chats, optional per number; queued messages never removed); delete message = confirm box `WaConfirm` (Enter = main action unless focus is on a control, Esc cancels) with "Delete for everyone" (own sent msg < WA_REVOKE_HOURS=48 h; worker sends `{ delete: key }`; shown as "You deleted this message") and "Delete for me" (dashboard only); a customer's own delete-for-everyone (protocolMessage REVOKE) marks ours deleted too. Tests then: API 119/119, UI 44 + 12 + 20.

**Gaps / not done:** no realtime push (polling 3–5 s); delete/clear only affect the dashboard, not the phone; no "delete for everyone"; no per-store override of the pacing numbers; the 40 order-updates/day cap per number can delay updates for a busy store with few numbers.

**Plan gating (2026-11-16, branch `feature/whatsapp-plan-gating`, from origin/main):** the user asked why the dashboard still "connects to the websocket" with WhatsApp chat off. Findings: the DASHBOARD's only websocket is the live ORDER feed (`/realtime`, Orders page) — WhatsApp chat never opens a browser websocket (it polls `/api/whatsapp/*`); the real culprit was the SERVER worker, which connected every linked number to WhatsApp regardless of the store's plan, and the dashboard polled `/api/whatsapp/me` (403s) every 30 s. Fixed: `revokeStore()` / `enforceWhatsAppPlan()` in whatsapp.service.js unlink ALL of a store's numbers at once (status logged_out + wantsLogout, waiting messages failed, chats kept) — called from `setStorePlan` (plan change or per-store lock) and `updatePlan` (every store on the plan); the worker tick also refuses to hold a connection for a store without the feature (safety net, e.g. after a restart) and keeps the reason on the card. Dashboard: `useWaMe` only runs when the plan has the feature; `WaGate` (routes/whatsapp/WaGate.jsx) shows `NotFoundSection` (a 404 in the content area, sidebar/shell stay) for every /whatsapp address when the feature is missing or the person lacks access (`manage` for numbers/broadcast/access/clean-up). Re-enabling does NOT re-link: numbers stay "Unlinked" and need a new scan. Tests: t16-whatsapp-plan-gating (Chrome) + a new phase 11 in suites/wa-api.mjs (worker with fake sockets).

### message-sending

_Settings → Message sending (2026-11-19, branch feature/message-sending): owner maps how verification codes (sign-up/sign-in + staff coin codes) go out — linked WhatsApp / WhatsApp Business / SMS / don't send; RANDOM linked number per code; Sign-in codes switch on WhatsApp → Numbers; server-enforced RESEND WAITS (30s,1m,2m,5m,15m,1h,6h); tests t23-t27_

User asked (2026-11-19): "add an option in admin panel to map Message sending method — linked WhatsApp, WhatsApp Business or SMS; if mapped to linked WhatsApp then user account registration OTP should be sent using this method." Built on `feature/message-sending` (branched from `feature/onboarding-form`, stacked). **Deployed 2026-11-19** ("deploy it"): 3 commits pushed (b75c41a Menu add-on row fix, efbeb1e the feature, 8e95d52 tests) and put on the DEV server (mytimelyne.info) — admin + storefront rebuilt, tablepe-dev-api + -storefront restarted, no pnpm install (no dependency changes), no env changes; website not rebuilt/restarted. Not merged to main, not on prod (food-*).

**What it is:** a new Settings tab **Message sending** (`/message-sending`, `routes/MessageSending.jsx`; shown if the plan has `notifications` OR `whatsappChat` — `feature` in SETTINGS_TABS / sidebar may now be an array = any-of). One card "Verification codes" with four radio options + per-option Ready / Needs setting up (with reason) / Not in your plan; a second card lists where order updates (Notifications), marketing (per campaign) and chat/broadcasts (always linked) are chosen — those were NOT moved under the mapping (they already pick their own channel). Only the owner (`notification-config:manage`) can save.

**Rules to keep:**
- Mapping lives on `Brand.messaging.codes = { method: screen|linked|whatsapp|sms, linkedMessage, whatsappTemplate:{name,language,copyButton} }` (shared definition `packages/config-schema/src/messaging.js`; null = never chosen).
- **Legacy default** when never chosen: SMS if the old `sms.otpEnabled==="on"` + gateway complete, else `screen`. The old "Send login & coin codes by SMS" switch was REMOVED from the SMS form (NOTIFY_FIELDS); `otpTemplate` ("Code message") stays and is what SMS codes use.
- `screen` = nothing sent, code shown on the sign-in screen, 123456 accepted (as before). Any real method ⇒ code never returned, 123456 refused — **even if that method is offline** (no silent fallback; customer gets "We can't send the code right now…"). Save refuses a method that can't send now (409 with reason) and one the plan lacks (linked ⇒ `whatsappChat`; whatsapp/sms ⇒ `notifications`); `screen` always allowed.
- API: `GET/PUT /api/notify/messaging` (notify.service `codeSettings/codeSender/getMessaging/saveMessaging`); `codeSender(brandId)` replaced `smsCodeSender` (used by customer.service requestOtp/consumeCode and staff-orders coin codes). `requestOtp` now returns `{sent:true, via}`; storefront CustomerGate says "on WhatsApp"/"by SMS".
- **Linked WhatsApp code lane** (`whatsapp.service.sendCodeLinked`, worker): WaMessage `source:"code"`, `pace:"code"` — sent FIRST (the worker's `sendNext` looks at the code lane BEFORE the other queued messages — without that a queued broadcast starved a code to the 25 s timeout), no cold spacing / quiet hours / first-contact budget, 0.3–0.7 s typing, still checks "number is on WhatsApp"; requestOtp polls up to 25 s for sent/failed (needs the ONE WhatsApp worker process — dev server has `WHATSAPP_WORKER=on`; a local API with worker off still works because the queue is the shared DB); on timeout the queued message is cancelled. Cap `WA_PACING.code.perDay=300` per store per rolling 24 h (failed ones count). **The code is wiped from the stored message once sent/failed** (`WA_CODE_REDACTED`); the stranger's chat is created `hidden:true` (WaChat.hidden; listChats filters it; inbound/outbound real messages and startChat unhide), code sends never touch lastMessageAt/lastOutboundAt; a failed code can't be retried from the inbox.
- WhatsApp Business: template message with the code as {{1}}; optional `copyCode` adds the URL-button component (Authentication templates). Meta's address is now env `WHATSAPP_GRAPH_URL` (default graph.facebook.com) so a test can stand in for Meta.

**Tests:** `t23-message-sending` (Chrome, 67 checks; bench control server on :3095 = stand-in connected linked number: `POST /wa/online|offline?account=`, `/wa/reset?brand=`, `/wa/clear`, `GET /wa/sent`; fake SMS gateway :3098; fake Meta :3096 — the bench sets `WHATSAPP_GRAPH_URL` to it) and `t24-message-sending-api` (suites/msg-api.mjs, 47 checks, incl. a code jumping ahead of a queued broadcast). Gotchas: bench-seeded numbers look online for their first 90 s (reset them first); the bench blocks an address after 3 wrong customer codes in 60 s (keep wrong guesses ≤2 per test); the code boxes need Backspace-clearing between attempts.

**Left out on purpose:** a "send me a test code" button; moving order updates/marketing under the same mapping (asked the user); email codes (still on-screen, as before). See [whatsapp-chat](#whatsapp-chat), [marketing](#marketing), [customer-auth-checkout](#customer-auth-checkout), [test-bench](#test-bench).

**Follow-up (same day, after the first deploy) — commits 088718f + f4a7b2b pushed and DEPLOYED to dev 2026-11-19 ("deploy it"; admin+storefront rebuilt, api+storefront restarted, no env/deps change; real DB got the new `otpthrottles` collection with its unique + TTL indexes; the tests commit came after the full run):** the user's real store (Gazab Momos, Custom plan, 1 linked number "Postpaid") was still showing the on-screen "dev code" because nobody had CHOSEN a method (`Brand.messaging` null ⇒ "screen" by design — linking a number does NOT switch codes on; I deliberately kept it opt-in because with a real method there is no on-screen fallback). Fixes:
- **Switch on WhatsApp → Numbers** ("Sign-in codes" card, `SignInCodesCard` in `routes/whatsapp/WhatsAppNumbers.jsx`, checkbox "Send sign-in codes from my WhatsApp numbers", `data-testid=signin-codes`): same setting as Message sending (`useMessaging/useSaveMessaging`); unticking asks first (`WaConfirm`, "Stop sending sign-in codes?" → falls back to `screen`); disabled with the reason when no number is connected; warns when on but every number is offline. I did NOT turn it on for the user's real store (their data) — they tick it.
- **Random number per code:** `pickRandomOnlineAccount` (crypto `randomInt`) in whatsapp.service — replaces "the chat's own number first". Offline numbers never picked. (Chats/replies elsewhere still use the number the customer wrote to.)
- **Resend waits, enforced by the SERVER** (`apps/api/src/modules/customer/otp-throttle.js`, model `OtpThrottle` in db customer.js, schedule in `packages/config-schema/src/otp.js`): after the 1st code 30 s, 2nd 1 min, 3rd 2 min, 4th 5 min, 5th 15 min, 6th 1 h, 7th and every later 6 h (my reading of "30s, 1m, 2m, 5 … till 6 hours" — user didn't confirm). Key = (brand, normalised number, purpose login|coins). `reserveSend` is atomic (unique-key insert / conditional update) so parallel requests let exactly one through; early request ⇒ 429 `{code:"otp_wait", retryAfterSec, liveCode, via}` + `Retry-After` header (error.js now sets it for any 429 with retryAfterSec). Success returns `resendAfterSec`. A send that FAILS is given back (`releaseSend`: count restored, 30 s before retry) and its unsent challenge is DELETED (before, it hid the code the customer already had). Signing in (`consumeCode` success) wipes the row; row also forgotten after 24 h (TTL). Replaced the old `MAX_PER_HOUR` count (which could never work: challenges are TTL-deleted after 10 min).
- **`normalizeDestination` now canonicalises Indian numbers** (+91 / 0091 / 0 prefix / spaces / dashes → the 10 digits) — otherwise each spelling of one phone was a separate throttle key (found by test). Customers stored before are 10-digit so unaffected.
- **Storefront** (`CustomerGate.jsx`): countdown comes from the server (`resendAfterSec`), is kept per number and survives Back/retype (Back used to zero it — a client-only loophole), reads the clock not tick counts; a 429 `otp_wait` with `liveCode` jumps straight to the code step with a note (`data-testid=otp-notice`); label uses `formatCountdown` ("24s", "4:32", "5:59:12"). The on-screen hint now reads "Codes aren't being sent for this store yet — dev code:" (was "No SMS provider configured yet").
- **Tests:** t25 `otp-resend-api` (suites/otp-resend.mjs, 43 checks, port 3091/gateway 3090, LOGIN_GUARD off), t26 `signin-resend-timer` (Chrome, ~37 s real time — `d.pause` is a NO-OP headless, use `page.waitForTimeout` for real waiting), t27 `signin-codes-switch` (Chrome); t24/msg-api now 51 checks (its `request` helper clears `otpthrottles`, needs `LOGIN_FLOOD_PER_MINUTE=1000`). Bench stand-in log entries now carry `from` (which number sent). A bench phone ending "0000" = "not on WhatsApp" — avoid in generated numbers.

**Message editor + variables (2026-11-19) — commit e26b44e, tests bb76259, pushed and DEPLOYED to dev (admin+storefront rebuilt, api+storefront restarted):** user reported the "What the message says" box "not supporting multi line". Sending/saving always kept line breaks (checked: zod, Mongo Mixed, worker `sendMessage({text})`); what broke it visually was the preview `div` (no `white-space: pre-wrap`) and a small one-line-looking box. Rebuilt in `MessageSending.jsx`: 6-row `pre-wrap` textarea (`#code-message`, aria-label "Message sent with the code"), char counter /1000 (limit raised from 500), WhatsApp-style preview bubble (`data-testid=code-preview` wraps "Preview:" + `code-preview-text`), **Variables** button → modal "Message variables" (`li[data-variable]`, Insert puts the token at the remembered cursor, stays open, "Inserted ✓", Done), inline problem note (`data-testid=message-problem`) + Save disabled on unknown variable / missing {{code}} (`messageProblem` = `codeMappingSchema.safeParse`). Shared list `CODE_MESSAGE_VARIABLES` in `packages/config-schema/src/messaging.js`: code, store, outlet, name, phone, minutes, date, time, store_phone (`renderCodeMessage(template, values, fallback)`, `variablesIn`, `sampleCodeValues`; tolerant of `{{ CODE }}`; unknown variable ⇒ save refused 400, at send time left literal). Server fills them in `notify.service.codeValues` (outlet name + `config.identity.phone` via OutletModel with brandId, name from CustomerModel on file else the typed name — `firstNameOf` allows letters only, else "there" — so a link typed into the name box can't be injected into a message to someone else's number; date/time fixed to Asia/Kolkata; `OTP_VALID_MINUTES` in config-schema otp.js). Storefront now sends `name` with the code request. SMS "Code message" renders through the same function (also supports the variables; its field hint points to the list). Tests: t23 has a multi-line/variables/pop-up section (Enter presses, Insert at cursor), msg-api section "3b" (61 checks). Gotcha: `ui.fill(d, "What the message says")` no longer works (label is `htmlFor`, not wrapping) — use `getByLabel("Message sent with the code")`.

Final state 2026-11-19: branch `feature/message-sending` fully pushed (088718f, f4a7b2b, e26b44e, bb76259 on top of the first three), all of it on the dev server; NOT merged to `main`, NOT on prod. All 28 tests (t00–t27) have a passing run on the final code (t23 81, t24 61, t25 43, t26 20, t27 21). Real store (Gazab Momos) still has `messaging` null = on-screen code until its owner ticks the switch on WhatsApp → Numbers. Running a test while another run holds the bench (ports 3097/3170/3171) breaks both — stop the old `node run.mjs` first; `--rebuild` also restarts the bench.

### marketing

_Marketing feature (2026-11-16, branch feature/marketing, pushed; plan feature `marketing`): send to customers over linked WhatsApp / SMS / WhatsApp Business API with audience filters, contact lists, campaigns; ages now grow yearly; sidebar split with a divider; how it works, rules, deploy needs_

Built 2026-11-16 on `feature/marketing` (from `main`). User's ask: owner dashboard Marketing where the owner messages customers over the 3 channels (linked WhatsApp, SMS, WhatsApp Business API — each only if set up/plan allows), from imported numbers or the customer list, with filters: hasn't ordered for N days/weeks/months (whole store or chosen outlets), ordered chosen items at least N times, age (exact/range), gender, many takeaways, area; plus the sidebar split.

**Ages grow:** `Customer.age` was a fixed number. Added `Customer.ageYear` (stamped by a pre-save hook whenever age is set) and `currentAge()` in config-schema (age + years since; legacy rows fall back to createdAt year — approximate). Customers page, storefront `/me`, and the marketing age filter all use it. No backfill script was run.

**Where:** config-schema `marketing.js` (channels, filter schema, `parseContacts`, `renderMarketing`, `dateAgo`; order-type constants are `MARKETING_ORDER_TYPES` because `ORDER_TYPES` already exists in storefront.js — a duplicate export silently breaks the admin build), db `models/marketing.js` (MarketingList/Contact/Campaign/Message), api `modules/marketing/{audience,marketing.service,worker,marketing.routes}.js` mounted at `/api/marketing`, admin `routes/marketing/{NewCampaign,FilterBuilder,Campaigns,Lists,MarketingNav}.jsx` + `lib/useMarketing.js` + `mk*` in api.js.

**Rules decided:** audience = customers (filters AND-ed) + WhatsApp chat contacts + saved lists + pasted/CSV numbers, de-duplicated by normalised phone; anyone with `WaChat.optedOut` (said STOP) is excluded from EVERY channel; customers without a phone are left out; "visited" = any non-cancelled order; item match by name (case-insensitive) across chosen outlets, "any" = total quantity ≥ N, "all" = each ≥ N; "never ordered" only included if ticked; cap 20,000 per campaign. Linked WhatsApp reuses `createBroadcast` (extended with `recipients` + per-person `{{customerName}}/{{storeName}}`) so its slow pacing/STOP footer apply. SMS + Business API are queued `MarketingMessage` rows sent by `worker.js` (~6/s). Business API provider now accepts explicit `whatsapp.params` (marketing templates); campaign needs the approved template name + what fills {{1}}…. Permissions `marketing:read` / `marketing:send` = owner (and super admin) only by default. **Plan feature `marketing`** (added 2026-11-16): the API refuses every marketing route without it (`requireFeature`), the worker refuses to send without it, the sidebar item hides, and the pages show "Marketing isn't in your plan". Manageable in the console: tick it in Plans → edit, or unlock/lock per store on the store's Features tab (lock beats plan). In addition each channel needs its own thing (`whatsappChat`; `notifications` + credentials for SMS/API). The REAL DB's Pro plan stores an expanded feature list, so it does NOT have `marketing` until the user ticks it in Plans → Pro (same gotcha as customDomain); Basic correctly lacks it; `seed-plans.js` Pro gets it by default.

**Deploy needs:** new env `MARKETING_WORKER=on` on exactly ONE API process (same rule as WHATSAPP_WORKER — a local dev API shares the real DB and must never send real messages); off by default. Real DB: nothing to migrate.

**Sidebar:** groups now "Store & orders" (Take order, Dashboard, Orders, Menu, Tables, Printing) | divider | "Customers & growth" (Customers, Marketing, WhatsApp, Coupons) | "Setup" (Storefront, Settings). My reading of "store and order options vs other options" — easy to change in `NAV_GROUPS` (App.jsx).

**Tests:** `tests/e2e/tests/t13-marketing.mjs` (Chrome-only, 70 checks; fake SMS gateway on :3098 run by the test); bench seed has a third store "Marketing Mart" (o@mart.test / Owner123!, mart.localhost:3170) with 8 fixture customers; the bench API now starts the marketing worker. Not covered by a browser test: the WhatsApp Business API send (checked with a stubbed fetch only) and real SMS gateways. See [test-bench](#test-bench), [whatsapp-chat](#whatsapp-chat), [feedback-save-every-test](#feedback-save-every-test).

### analytics

_Store Analytics (2026-11-16, branch feature/analytics, UNCOMMITTED; plan feature `analytics`): sales/outlet graphs, items + stock prep suggestions, customers, compare outlets, operations, profit & loss, GST, cash close, exports xlsx/csv/pdf/json; rules, permissions, deploy needs_

Built 2026-11-16 on `feature/analytics` (from `main` at f6aa7d8). **Nothing committed or pushed yet** — the user asks for that explicitly. User's ask: owner sees detailed store sales and per-outlet sales as graphs, exports (weekly/monthly/yearly, whole store or one outlet, several formats), a profit & loss calculator that remembers monthly bills, compare outlets with the date picker, most-sold items, and daily-selling analysis that suggests stock/prep limits to cut wastage. Answers to my questions: plan feature + role access = yes; "save to my store" = yes; all export formats; stock suggestions per item.

**Rules that must stay true:**
- A **sale** = order not cancelled AND `payment.status == "paid"` (`SALES_MATCH` in config-schema `analytics.js`). Money is paise; days are India time (IST). Net sales = grand total − GST.
- Overview/Sales/etc. take `from`+`to` or `preset`, and `outlets` (csv|all). Ranges ≤ 1100 days. Previous period = same length just before.
- **Stock suggestion** = average quantity sold on that weekday over the last N (default 6) whole weeks ending yesterday × (1 + safety %, default 15) rounded up, per item (no ingredient tracking). Tomorrow list + 7-weekday table.
- **Profit & loss:** food cost is a % of net sales (default 35, no per-item cost price yet — deferred); monthly bills are charged per day of each calendar month (`monthsInRange`, a full month = exactly one month's bill). Outlet-scope bills (rent, salaries, electricity, water, gas, internet, packaging, repairs) per outlet; store-scope (marketing, software, licences, other) shared and split between the chosen outlets by net sales; a one-outlet store gets all bills in one list. Bills live in localStorage `tablepe.analytics.finance.<brandId>` and can be saved to the store (`AnalyticsProfileModel`, `GET/PUT /api/analytics/profile`); with no local copy the saved copy loads by itself (note "Loaded the numbers saved to your store"); "Use the version saved to my store" overrides local.
- **Permissions:** `analytics:read` = owner, manager, accountant; `analytics:finance` (GST, daily cash close, profit & loss, saved bills) and `analytics:export` = owner, accountant. A manager sees figures but NOT Profit & loss/Reports (their pages are 404). A page can take `needs` as a list = any of (Reports needs export OR finance — a bug found by the test, the manager used to reach it).
- **Plan feature `analytics`** ("Analytics & reports"): API `requireFeature` on every route, sidebar hides, page says "Analytics isn't in your plan". The REAL DB's Pro plan stores an expanded feature list, so the user must tick "Analytics & reports" in Plans → Pro (same gotcha as marketing/customDomain); `seed-plans.js` not updated for it.

**Where:** config-schema `analytics.js` (dates, presets, SALES_MATCH, EXPENSE_LINES, `computeProfitLoss`, export schema), db `models/analytics.js`, api `modules/analytics/{analytics.service,analytics.export,analytics.routes}.js` mounted `/api/analytics` (new deps `exceljs`, `pdfkit`), admin `routes/analytics/*` + `lib/useAnalytics.js` + `an*` in `lib/api.js`; charts reuse `routes/platform/viz` (`ColumnChart` got a `whole` prop so counts never get 2.5-ticks; `niceTicks(max, target, whole)`). `?lapsed=N` on `/marketing/new` pre-fills "hasn't ordered for N days" (Customers tab → "Message them").

**Tests:** `tests/e2e/tests/t17-analytics.mjs` (Chrome-only, 93 checks): bench store Marketing Mart gets ~70 days of orders; independent expected totals are computed from the stored orders in `serve-bench.mjs` into `ids.json → analytics` (it throws if the bench was seeded on a different day — `node bench/bench.mjs reset`). Date picker: day clicks apply at once but the calendar stays open until **OK**. Downloads are saved to `tests/e2e/.bench/downloads` and parsed (exceljs, CSV sums, PDF header, JSON). See [test-bench](#test-bench), [feedback-save-every-test](#feedback-save-every-test), [marketing](#marketing).

**Deploy needs:** just `pnpm install` for the new libs. No env vars. No migration.

### app-updates

_2026-11-26/27 — OMA update system (console "App updates" page, website /app download page, in-app optional/required update gate, 426 at sign-in) + super-admin store→outlet chooser in the app; decisions, deploy needs (APP_RELEASES_DIR, rsync exclude), tests t93–t95, what is NOT yet deployed/committed_

**Asked by the user (2026-11-26):** (1) a platform admin signing in to OMA must be asked *store first, then outlet* — even with one store/one outlet; (2) when the app opens, a popup checks for updates: optional (Update/Later) or **forced** (can't enter the app until updated); a button opens a web page in an external browser where the APK is downloaded; (3) console page to manage versions + force flag + upload the APK; (4) a separate website page for app updates/downloads.

**Built (committed + pushed as main 1cc1fcf and **DEPLOYED to production 2026-11-26 night** after the user's "yes deploy to production" — details in [production-rasoipulse](#production-rasoipulse); nothing uploaded there yet):**
- Shared rules `packages/config-schema/src/app-release.js` (`parseAppVersion`, `decideUpdate`: any NEWER published release with force ⇒ force, so a phone that skipped a forced one still must update; unknown build 0 is never judged; `formatBytes`), model `packages/db/src/models/app-release.js`, API `apps/api/src/modules/app-releases/` (public `/api/app-updates/check|releases|download/:id`; super-admin `/api/platform/app-releases` list/PATCH/DELETE + chunked upload `uploads` → `chunks/:i` (exactly 4 MB each, resumable, in `.app-releases/.parts`) → `complete` (zip + AndroidManifest check, sha256, duplicate build 409)). `assertAppCurrent` in `/api/auth/app-login`: header `x-oma-version-code` too old while an update is forced ⇒ `426 update_required` BEFORE the password is looked at; no header = let through. Three new rate-limit policies (`app-update-check`, `app-download`, `release-upload` 200/10 min — deliberately under team-write's 240/min).
- Console `apps/admin/src/routes/platform/AppReleases.jsx` (+ `lib/uploadApk.js`: piece-by-piece XHR with progress, 4 retries on connection drops, 4xx not retried). "Cancel upload" only stops the upload (bug found by t94: it used to close the pop-up).
- Website `apps/website/src/app/app/page.jsx` = `/app` (ISR 300 s + the API's revalidate ping on every publish/withdraw/edit); "app" added to RESERVED_SITE_SLUGS; noindex.
- App `mobile-apps/oma/src/updates/` (`store.ts`, `UpdateGate.tsx`, `open.ts` http(s)-only), `ChooseStoreScreen.tsx` + route `choose` (session: super admin ⇒ `outletId` null after sign-in AND after a kept-sign-in start; `changeOutlet()`; Account "Change store or outlet" + "Check for updates"). The required screen is a full-screen overlay (NOT a Modal: a Modal window had an unreadable status bar) and holds the hardware Back button; root layout hides the app from screen readers while it is up. 448 jest tests (was 383), tsc + lint clean.
- Tests: t93 API (67), t94 console UI (45), t95 website (29), t28 (133, incl. the 3 new policies), t87 (34). Bench: `node tests/e2e/bench/bench.mjs website` builds/starts the marketing site on :3173 from a copy in `.bench/website` (never touches apps/website/.next); bench API now has WEBSITE_URL=http://localhost:3173 and APP_RELEASE_MAX_MB=20. New fixtures recorder `_record-staff-app-superadmin-update-fixtures.mjs` (only adds its own files).
- Verified on the **Android 14 emulator** (debug APK v-261126-001 against the bench): optional pop-up over the sign-in screen, Update opens Chrome on `/app`, super-admin chooser (stores → outlets, Back goes outlets→stores), Account screen, forced update screen (Back keeps it, Check again toast, Download update → Chrome).

**Bugs found on the way (fixed + covered):** Express `res.download` refuses files under a dot-folder (`.app-releases`) unless `dotfiles: "allow"` → every download 404'd (t93 now uses a dot-named folder); a zustand selector returning a new object re-rendered forever (use one selector per value); Modal-based blocking screen had an unreadable status bar → overlay; `Date.now` mock for "30 minutes later" and the AppState/BackHandler listeners are replaced by assignment in tests (the setup's own mocks must not be restored with spies).

**Deploy needs (done 2026-11-26, kept as the recipe for next time):** API restart; new env on prod — `APP_RELEASES_DIR` OUTSIDE the code folder (e.g. `/var/lib/rasoi-pulse/app-releases`, writable by the API user), `WEBSITE_URL` must be the public site (`https://rasoipulse.in`) because the app is sent to `<WEBSITE_URL>/app`; rsync must `--exclude .app-releases` (it is git-ignored); admin + website rebuild (config-schema changed → all consumers); nginx `client_max_body_size 12m` on api.rasoipulse.in is already enough (pieces are 4 MB). Console/website show "App updates" only after the deploy.

**Caveats to tell the user:** (the debug-key caveat is RESOLVED 2026-11-26 — release key made, see [release-signing](#release-signing)); the app's own version (`v-261126-001`) was not bumped (only when asked) so the first real update needs `npm run version:bump -- --confirm` + a build; "version management" was built as: console page = manage (upload/publish/withdraw/required/notes/delete/counters), website page = public download + history.

### feedback-app-flow-parity

_standing rule (2026-11-26) — when a requested STOREFRONT flow change would also need to change in the OMA (Order Management App), ASK the user first; change the app only after they confirm_

User: "when I ask to change any flow in the storefront and that also needs to be changed in the app, then please ask me first; I will confirm and you should change the flow in the app also."

**Why:** the OMA app (Rasoi Pulse OMA) (`mobile-apps/oma/` (Rasoi Pulse OMA, the Order Management App)) mirrors parts of the storefront/dashboard order and table flows. The user wants to decide per change whether the app follows, not have it drift or change silently.
**How to apply:** when a storefront (or dashboard take-order / tables) flow change touches something the app also does (ordering rules, table states, pricing display, order statuses, login/session rules…), finish the storefront change as asked, then ASK "this flow also exists in the OMA app (Rasoi Pulse OMA) — change it there too?" and only change the app after a yes. Applies to the API too when an endpoint the app uses changes shape. Record the answer in the app's changelog. See [oma-app](#oma-app).

### feedback-app-icons

_standing UI preference (2026-11-26) — the OMA app must use icons properly (headings, fields, badges, rows, buttons); the user said the first version used too few_

User: "you have used very less icons in the mobile app. use icons properly to make the app UI looks good."

**Why:** the first OMA version had icons only on a few buttons and the tab bar; the user wants a polished, scannable UI.
**How to apply:** every new or changed screen in `mobile-apps/oma` gets icons where they carry meaning — a tinted `IconTile` or `ScreenTitle` on page headings, `SectionTitle` for sections, a `leftIcon` on every text field, status icons next to status words (never colour alone), icons in chips/badges/buttons/empty states. Reuse the set in `src/ui/Icon.tsx` (add new paths there, 24×24 stroke) and the status maps in `features/orders.ts` / `features/tables.ts`. See [oma-app](#oma-app).

### feedback-app-versioning

_standing rule (2026-11-26) — the OMA app (Rasoi Pulse OMA)'s version is v-DDMMYY-NNN (first one v-261126-001); NEVER bump it unless the user asks to upgrade the version_

User: "app should support version management (initial should be "v-DDMMYY-001") and only upgrade when I ask you to upgrade."

**Rule:** the app version looks like `v-DDMMYY-NNN` — the day the version was cut (DDMMYY) and a running build number NNN that only ever goes up. The first is `v-261126-001`. **Do not change the version on your own** — not when fixing bugs, not when adding features, not when deploying. Only when the user says to upgrade/bump the app version run `npm run version:bump` in `mobile-apps/oma/` (Rasoi Pulse OMA, the Order Management App) (it writes today's date and NNN+1, and sets the Android versionCode to NNN). The version lives in ONE place (`mobile-apps/oma/app-version.json`); see [oma-app](#oma-app).

### feedback-readme-maintenance

_standing rule (2026-11-26) — maintain ONE common README.md at the repo root for the whole project (all apps incl. the OMA app (Rasoi Pulse OMA)); keep it current when things change_

User: "maintain a common readme file for my whole project."

**How to apply:** `README.md` at the repo root describes every part (API, dashboard, storefront, website, OMA app), how to run, test, version and deploy each, and where to read more (`MEMORY.md` = the detailed project memory, `mobile-apps/oma/README.md` = app specifics). When a change alters how something is run, tested, deployed or named, update the README in the same change. No secrets, server addresses or credentials in it.

### oma-app

_"Rasoi Pulse OMA" (Order Management App) — React Native/Expo Android app for table staff in mobile-apps/oma; stack, rules, how it was verified, what is NOT yet verified/deployed (2026-11-26)_

**What:** Android app (phones + tablets, min Android 11 / API 30) for staff who walk to tables: sign in, see the table board, take orders (menu → sizes/add-ons → server-priced review → send), manage tables (move, confirm payment, free, cancel — only with `table:manage`), watch orders. Lives in `mobile-apps/oma/` (folder-per-app; `mobile-apps/` is NOT in the pnpm workspace; npm project). Name "Rasoi Pulse OMA", package `com.rasoipulse.oma`, scheme `rpoma`.

**Stack:** Expo SDK 58 (RN 0.88, React 19.3, TS 6), Expo Router (`src/app`), CNG (`android/` is generated by `expo prebuild` + gitignored, never edit), TanStack Query (polling 8 s tables / 10 s orders, paused in background via AppState/NetInfo), zustand vanilla stores (session, per-table drafts), zod-validated API client, SecureStore only for "Keep me signed in". arm64-only + Gradle cache/parallel via local plugin `plugins/with-fast-gradle.js`. `scripts/build-android.js` = prebuild + Gradle + copy APK to `build-output/`.

**Rules from the user (standing):** sign-in required at EVERY start (+ API rate limit); version `v-DDMMYY-NNN`, first `v-261126-001`, ONLY bumped when the user asks (`npm run version:bump -- --confirm`) — see [feedback-app-versioning](#feedback-app-versioning); storefront flow changes that also exist in the app → ASK first — see [feedback-app-flow-parity](#feedback-app-flow-parity); ONE root README — see [feedback-readme-maintenance](#feedback-readme-maintenance); support tablets (phone <600dp, tablet 600–839, wide ≥840 split view); target Android 11+; tests required.

**API side (committed db67616 + DEPLOYED to production 2026-11-26, user said "if api needs to be updated for app please update"):** `POST /api/auth/app-login` (Bearer token, shares the dashboard's brute-force guards), `GET /api/auth/guard`, Bearer support in `authenticate`; t87 passes 34/34. Live now: only two files were synced (apps/api/src/middleware/auth.js, modules/auth/auth.routes.js — server's old copies were byte-identical to 8dc946b's, backups in /root/tablepe-backups/before-app-login-2026-11-26/), API restarted, verified (empty app-login → 400, dashboard login unchanged, bogus Bearer → 401, via Cloudflare with okhttp UA). Header-highlight 8dc946b is still NOT deployed. Don't test the live server with WRONG passwords: they count against the user's (shared) internet address and can block their own dashboard sign-in.

**Server facts learned:** move / confirm-payment / close / void need `table:manage` (not just order:create + table:read) → buttons hidden without it. Orders carry `expectedSessionId` = the party the waiter SAW; a mismatch is 409 `table_taken` → app stops and never retries with the new id (would land on a stranger's bill). First order at a free table needs the customer's phone. Outlet ids are `_id` in `/api/outlets`.

**Bugs the tests caught (keep these tests):** `seen ?? current` treated "table was free" (null) as unknown → fixed (state, no `??`); Spinner/ProgressBar/headings lacked `accessible` (TalkBack skipped them); screens crashed for one render during sign-out (`useOutletId` threw) → queries now just switch off.

**Tests:** `npx jest --no-watchman` (Watchman on this Mac is broken — Homebrew icu4c mismatch; `brew reinstall watchman`), 305 tests / 18 files at last count, run against recorded real API answers in `test/fixtures` (re-record with `tests/e2e/suites/_record-staff-app-fixtures.mjs`). `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npx expo lint` clean. `npx expo export --platform android` bundles in ~10 s (3.6 MB Hermes).

**Verified on a real Android system (2026-11-26, Pixel_8 emulator = Android 17 / API 37, headless, against the test bench API):** sign-in (also via keyboard Go key), Tables board, table page, take order (dish sheet → review → phone → Send), order shows on the table, confirm payment, free the table, phone layout, wide-tablet split layout (1280×800 dp via `adb shell wm size 2560x1600; wm density 320`), dark mode (`cmd uimode night yes`), keyboard handling, the app staying signed in through display-size/density changes. First Gradle debug build 14 m 54 s (incl. one-time downloads, ~370 KB/s), rebuild 1 m 08 s, debug APK 95.7 MB. NOT verified: Android 11 itself (no API 30 image installed; the build targets minSdk 30), a physical phone, a physical tablet, Expo Go behaviour in detail.

**Bugs the device test found (all fixed + tested):** (1) re-mounting the root layout (Android rebuilds the activity on display-size/density change) re-ran `session.start()` → signed the waiter OUT and wiped unsent orders → `start()` now runs only while status is "starting"; (2) Send button hidden behind the keyboard → Sheet uses KeyboardAvoidingView and the OrderPanel is split into body + pinned footer (render-prop `children({body, footer})`); (3) table cards crushed on a wide tablet (columns were computed from the window width, not the list's real width) → `columnsForWidth(measuredWidth)`; (4) the left tab bar took ~358 dp → `tabBarVariant: "material"` + `tabBarLabelPosition: "below-icon"` (the library forces a min width when labels sit beside icons; "below-icon" is forbidden with the "uikit" variant on a side bar); (5) disabled "Add · ₹0" → "Add".

**Emulator gotchas (not app bugs):** Android 17 debug builds ask "nearby devices" = `ACCESS_LOCAL_NETWORK`, declared only in React Native's DEBUG manifest (to reach Metro; loopback via adb reverse is exempt) — check the release manifest does not have it; Play Store auto-updating WebView kills every app ("Killing … installPackageLI") → `adb shell pm disable-user --user 0 com.android.vending`; the emulator hangs (0% CPU) when the Mac is short on RAM (stop Gradle daemons with `./gradlew --stop`, run `-no-window -gpu swiftshader_indirect`); `CI=1` Metro does not reload (never use it for live testing); shell: wrap adb in a function (`adb() { "$HOME/Library/Android/sdk/platform-tools/adb" -s emulator-5554 "$@"; }`), a quoted variable holding a command does not word-split in zsh; the dev-only "linking in multiple places" toast appears after an activity rebuild (LogBox, not in release).

**Phone testing (2026-11-26):** the user scanned the Expo Go QR and got "Not Found" at sign-in = the default server is LIVE api.rasoipulse.in which has no `/api/auth/app-login` (404). Fix: `npm run phone` (scripts/phone.js) points the app at the bench API on the Mac's Wi-Fi address :3097 (test login `s@acme.test` / `Staff1234!`, 2 outlets, 5 tables, 18 dishes), probes the server first and refuses a server without app-login; sign-in screen shows the server host; 404 now has a plain message; empty EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL no longer blanks the address. Expo Go works with the app (user confirmed it opens). The user's own Metro ran on :8081 — never kill it.

**Skipped on purpose:** dish photos in the list (speed/data), per-tab polling pause, push notifications, offline order queue (offline banner only; orders can't be sent offline).

**Batch 2026-11-26 evening (user's 14-point list + follow-ups) — all built + tested, COMMITTED + PUSHED to main (server+dashboard 13e775d, app+READMEs 618911f; MEMORY.md deliberately NOT committed — 362 KB of internal notes, user asked for the file but not for GitHub), NOT DEPLOYED to production (prod still has only app-login from db67616 — until the next deploy the app falls back to loading the whole menu each order, and no outlet-mapping / re-seat / unavailable call on live):**
- API (apps/api): `GET /api/menu/unavailable` (+ `version` fingerprint in the menu answer; excludes stock counts) → app loads the menu once after sign-in, asks only this each order (falls back to full reload on a 404 = old server); `reseatForReopenedOrder` (tables.service) — a Completed/Cancelled order put back by hand re-seats its table, or 409 `table_taken` + `freeTables`, retry with `tableId`; `/api/outlets` filtered by membership `outletIds` + users create/PATCH accept `outletIds` (validated, owners always all). Dashboard: Orders pick-a-table dialog, Users page "Outlets" field/"Change outlets". Tests t88 (20), t89 (29), t90 (33), t91 (18, customer sees staff orders — answer to "can the customer see it on their phone": YES, by same phone, incl. +91 formats), t92 (30, browser). t28 124/124.
- App: collapsible categories (closed by default), pinned search/filters, dish pictures (`resolveImageUrl` — bench links say localhost:3097 → rewritten to the API host; real ratio words are "1:1","4:3","16:9","3:4"), food-type icons, customer search under the phone box (+ auto-fill when a full 10-digit number matches exactly ONE customer, only into an EMPTY name, once per number), Move-table tiles ("Table T2 · 4 seats", grouped by area), board grouped by area with collapsible areas — user then asked: collapsed BY DEFAULT and REMEMBERED per user+outlet across app restarts (AsyncStorage, `src/prefs/areas.ts`, key `oma.areas.v1:<userId>:<outletId>`, search/filter opens without saving, move sheet follows the saved areas but its own taps aren't saved), EmptyState action centred, big icon pass (IconTile/ScreenTitle/SectionTitle, status icons), sheet keyboard self-correction + scroll-into-view, memoised rows (user pasted a "VirtualizedList slow to update" warning from Expo Go; fixed: DishRow/TableCard memo, stable callbacks, debounced search). 383 jest tests, tsc + lint clean.
- Gotchas learned: RNTL v14 `unmount()` is async (await it); `queryByTestId` hides `accessibilityElementsHidden` nodes (use `{hidden:true}`); a FlatList only draws near the top (open lower sections first in tests); zsh: wrap adb in a function, never `$ADB -s ...` in a variable; macOS sed -i needs a suffix (use python); the bench API is rate-limited ("team-write") — restart it with `RATE_LIMITS=off node tests/e2e/bench/bench.mjs reset` before seeding; Android 14 emulator = `Pixel_8_API34` AVD (created by copying Pixel_8 config), `Pixel_8` = Android 17.
- AsyncStorage is a NEW native module: Expo Go has it; the debug APK must be rebuilt (done) — the old APK would just not remember areas (storage errors are swallowed).

**Standing UI preference (2026-11-26):** the user said the app used too few icons → use icons properly everywhere (headings, fields, badges, rows, buttons, empty states). Kit: `IconTile`, `ScreenTitle`, `SectionTitle`, status→icon maps in features/orders.ts + tables.ts, ~60 icons in ui/Icon.tsx. New screens must follow it. See [feedback-app-flow-parity](#feedback-app-flow-parity).

### release-signing

_2026-11-26 — OMA release signing key (made, where it lives, fingerprint, commands, rules, the backup the user must do); debug→release needs one uninstall; verified on Android 14_

**Why:** Android only installs an update over an app signed with the SAME key. The first APKs used Android's shared debug key, so the user asked "set up the release key, and tell me how we can set it up" (2026-11-26, after the app-updates deploy — see [app-updates](#app-updates)).

**Done:** `npm run signing:create` (in `mobile-apps/oma`) made the key ONCE: PKCS12 `oma-release.jks`, alias `rasoipulse-oma`, RSA 4096, 10000 days, subject `CN=Rasoi Pulse OMA, O=Rasoi Pulse, C=IN` (no personal data), random 32-char password passed to keytool via `-storepass:env` and written only to `keystore.properties` (mode 600, never printed, never committed). Both live in **`~/.rasoi-pulse/signing/oma/`** (outside every repo, dir 700). Public SHA-256 fingerprint (committed in `mobile-apps/oma/signing-fingerprint.txt`): `06:63:26:8E:8A:CF:67:9C:B1:58:70:A8:6A:39:89:14:29:86:D4:59:92:5D:6B:3D:B7:DE:AD:0D:C4:B0:08:3C`.

**How it is wired:** config plugin `plugins/with-release-signing.js` patches the GENERATED `android/app/build.gradle` (idempotent, throws if the Expo template changes) so the release type uses `signingConfigs.release` read from the file named by env `OMA_KEYSTORE_PROPERTIES` (nothing secret in `android/`). `scripts/build-android.js release`: finds the key (env → app folder `keystore.properties` → default folder), **refuses with no key** (override only `RP_ALLOW_DEBUG_SIGNING=1`, output named `…-release-DEBUGKEY.apk`), runs `apksigner verify --print-certs` on the result and **refuses to copy** an APK that is debug-signed, signed by another key, has >1 signer/cert, or doesn't verify. Commands: `signing:create|show|verify`. Real apksigner output on this SDK says `V2 Signer: certificate DN/SHA-256 digest:` (not `Signer #1`) — the parser handles both.

**Verified:** release build 85 s, 34.3 MB, apksigner shows our cert (4096-bit, v2 scheme); guards tested (no key, missing .jks, incomplete settings, debug APK refused); 32 jest tests (480 total in the app) incl. a real keytool run and mutation checks; Android 14 emulator: release-key APK over the old debug-key install → `INSTALL_FAILED_UPDATE_INCOMPATIBLE` (so every existing debug install needs ONE uninstall), clean install OK, same-key reinstall `-r` OK, debug build over the release app refused, app runs (shows api.rasoipulse.in). NOT tried: a real phone; a true update to a HIGHER versionCode (version is never bumped without the user asking).

**Open for the user:** BACK UP `~/.rasoi-pulse/signing/oma/` (the .jks AND keystore.properties) into a password manager — lost key = every phone must uninstall.
